- उप अघाणयत् (*upa aghāṇayat*)—urges on
- तदित् (*tadit*)—at that very moment
- तक्वः (*takvaḥ*)—he who desires to come to the sacrifice
- नेता (*netā*)—and who brings about the fruit, that Indra
- वपुः (*vapuḥ*)—produces water
- यः (*yaḥ*)—the Indra who
- उपमा (*upamā*)—is himself his own comparison, that Indra
- अमुच्यत्रुगळिंद (*amucyatrugaḷiṃda*)—is freed from enemies **NOTE**

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The Indra who urges on his horses, of many kinds of movement and well fastened to his chariot, to come to the sacrificer who offers the oblation, at that very moment he who desires to come to the sacrifice and brings about the fruit, that Indra, produces water. The Indra who is his own comparison is freed from enemies.

##### English translation
He who directs towards the worshipper his well-yoked prancing steeds,—he, (Indra), the swift bearer of blessing, (produces) rain,—he, who being comparable only to himself is delivered (from all his enemies).

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the recitation of the Śastra mantras of the Ṣoḍaśin, the three Ṛks beginning यो व्यतीन् (*yo vyatīn*) are to be recited as the third triplet in the Anuṣṭubh metre; this is explained in the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta-Sūtra यो व्यतीरथाणयदिति तृचा आनुष्टुभाः (*yo vyatīrathāṇayaditi tṛcā ānuṣṭubhāḥ*) (Ā. 6-2). **NOTE**

### Printed p. 39

#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अतीदु॑ श॒क्र ओ॑हत॒ इंद्रो॒ विश्वा॒ अति॒ द्विषः॑ ।  
> भि॒नत्क॒नीन॑ ओद॒नं प॒च्यमा॑नं प॒रो गि॒रा ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अति । इत् । ऊं इति । शक्रः । ओहते । इंद्रः । विश्वाः । अति । द्विषः ।  
> भिनत् । कनीनः । ओदनम् । पच्यमानम् । परः । गिरा ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अयमिंद्रः शक्रः शक्तः सन् अतीत् ओहते । अतिक्रम्य गच्छत्येव संग्रामे निरोधकान् शत्रून् । तदेवाह । अयम् इंद्रः विश्वाः द्विषः द्वेष्टॄन् शत्रून् अति अतिक्रम्य गच्छति । कनीनः कमनीयः परः मेघानां परस्ताद्वर्तमान इंद्रः ओदनम् । मेघनामैतत् । मेघं भिनत् अभिनत् भिनत्ति वृष्ट्यर्थम् । कीदृशम् । गिरा माध्यमिकया वाचा स्तनितलक्षणया पच्यमानम्। वज्रनिर्घोषेण ताड्यमानमित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Indra, being mighty, goes beyond all, passing over the enemies who obstruct him in battle. The same is said again: this Indra passes beyond all hateful enemies. Indra, the lovely, who is beyond, that is, abides beyond the clouds, cleaves the Odana (this is a name for the cloud) for the sake of rain. What sort of cloud? One that is being cooked by the voice, that is, the voice of the middle region, which has the nature of thunder; the meaning is: struck by the crash of the thunderbolt.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शक्रः (*śakraḥ*)—Indra, the all-powerful
- अतीत् (*atīt*)—passing beyond all the enemies
- ओहते (*ohate*)—he moves about
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—that same Indra
- विश्वाः (*viśvāḥ*)—all
- द्विषः (*dviṣaḥ*)—the enemies also
- अति (*ati*)—surpasses
- कनीनः (*kanīnaḥ*)—he who has an attractive form
- परः (*paraḥ*)—and is supreme, Indra
- गिरा (*girā*)—with his thundering sound
- पच्यमानं (*pacyamānaṃ*)—struck
- ओदनं (*odanaṃ*)—the cloud
- भिनत् (*bhinat*)—has split for the sake of rain

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Indra, the all-powerful, moves about passing beyond all the enemies. That same Indra surpasses all the enemies. Indra, who has an attractive form and is supreme, has split, for the sake of rain, the cloud struck by his thundering sound.

##### English translation
Shakra verily overpowers; Indra, overpowers all his enemies; he, worthy of love, abiding beyond, cleaves the cloud smitten by his thunder-voice.

### Printed p. 40

#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒र्भ॒को न कु॑मार॒कोऽधि॑ तिष्ठ॒न्नवं॒ रथ॑म् ।  
> स प॑क्षन्महि॒षं मृ॒गं पि॒त्रे मा॒त्रे वि॑भु॒क्रतु॑म् ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अर्भकः । न । कुमारकः । अधि । तिष्ठत् । नवम् । रथम् ।  
> सः । पक्षत् । महिषम् । मृगम् । पित्रे । मात्रे । विभुऽक्रतुम् ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अयमिंद्रः अर्भको न अल्पशरीरः कुमारकः कुमारक इव नवं स्तुत्यं रथम् अधि तिष्ठत् अधितिष्ठति । सः इंद्रः महिषं महांतं मृगं मृगवदितस्ततो धावंतं सर्वैर्मृग्यं वा विभुक्रतुं बहुकर्माणं मेघं पक्षत् पचति । वृष्टयभिमुखं करोतीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Indra, like a small-bodied child, mounts the new chariot, worthy of praise. That Indra makes ready, that is, makes favourable to rain, the great cloud, which runs here and there like a deer or is sought by all, the cloud of many works.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- अर्भकः (*arbhakaḥ*)—of tender age
- कुमारकः न (*kumārakaḥ na*)—like a young boy
- नवं (*navaṃ*)—his own praiseworthy
- रथं (*rathaṃ*)—chariot
- अधि तिष्ठत् (*adhi tiṣṭhat*)—he has mounted and sits
- सः (*saḥ*)—that same Indra
- महिषं (*mahiṣaṃ*)—of a great form
- मृगं (*mṛgaṃ*)—moving like a deer
- विभुक्रतुं (*vibhukratuṃ*)—and of many works, the cloud
- पित्रे मात्रे (*pitre mātre*)—for his father and mother
- पक्षत् (*pakṣat*)—prepares for rain

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Like a young boy of tender age, Indra has mounted his own praiseworthy chariot and sits there. That same Indra prepares for rain the cloud, of a great form, moving like a deer, and of many works, for the convenience of his father and mother, heaven and earth.

##### English translation
(Indra), like a young boy, has mounted his splendid chariot; he makes ready for his father and mother the great deer-like many-functioned cloud.

### Printed p. 41

#### Ṛk 16

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ तू सु॑शिप्र दंपते॒ रथं॑ तिष्ठा हिर॒ण्यय॑म् ।  
> अध॑ द्यु॒क्षं स॑चेवहि स॒हस्र॑पादमरु॒षं स्व॑स्ति॒गाम॑ने॒हस॑म् ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । तु । सुऽशिप्र । दम्ऽपते । रथम् । तिष्ठ । हिरण्ययम् ।  
> अध । द्युक्षम् । सचेवहि । सहस्रऽपादम् । अरुषम् । स्वस्तिऽगाम् । अनेहसम् ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सुशिप्र सुहनो हे दंपते गृहस्वामिन् । अत्र गृहो रथः। तस्य स्वामिन् । तु त्वं तावत् रथम् आ तिष्ठ। हविःस्वीकरणांतरं पश्चादहमारोहामीति भावः । कीदृशम् । हिरण्ययं हिरण्मयम्। अध तवारोहणानंतरमहमप्यारुह्योभौ सचेवहि संगच्छेवहि । संगतौ भवेव। पुनः कीदृशम् । द्युक्षं दीप्तं रथं सहस्रपादं बहुपादम् अरुषम् आरोचमानं स्वस्तिगां कुशलगमनम् अनेहसम् अपापम् ॥
> पूर्वाह्णे प्रवर्ग्ये ‘तं घेमित्था ' इत्येषा । सूत्रितं च -- तं घेमित्था नमस्विन इति प्रागाथीं पूर्वाह्ने' (आश्व. श्रौ. ४. ७) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Suśipra, of fine jaws, O Dampati, lord of the house (here the house is the chariot, and he is its lord): mount, for your part, the chariot first; the sense is that after accepting the oblation, I shall mount afterwards. What sort of chariot? A golden one. Then, after you have mounted, I too shall mount, and we two shall meet and be united. Again, what sort of chariot? Bright, thousand-footed, that is, many-footed, shining, of good movement, free from fault.

In the forenoon Pravargya the Ṛk beginning तं घेमित्था (*taṃ ghemitthā*) is used. It is so laid down in the sūtra तं घेमित्था नमस्विन इति प्रागाथीं पूर्वाह्ने (*taṃ ghemitthā namasvina iti prāgāthīṃ pūrvāhne*) (Āśva. Śrau. 4. 7).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुशिप्र (*suśipra*)—O Indra, of fine jaws
- दंपते (*daṃpate*)—and lord of the house called the chariot
- तु (*tu*)—as for you
- हिरण्ययं (*hiraṇyayaṃ*)—made of gold
- रथं (*rathaṃ*)—in the chariot
- आ तिष्ठ (*ā tiṣṭha*)—mount and sit
- अध (*adha*)—afterwards
- द्युक्षं (*dyukṣaṃ*)—the bright one
- सहस्रपादं (*sahasrapādaṃ*)—the one of a thousand (pillar) feet
- अरुषं (*aruṣaṃ*)—the golden-coloured one
- स्वस्तिगां (*svastigāṃ*)—the one of excellent movement
- अनेहसं (*anehasaṃ*)—the one free from fault, the chariot, having mounted it
- सचेवहि (*sacevahi*)—let us two be united

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, of fine jaws and lord of the house called the chariot, mount at once the chariot made of gold. Afterwards, having mounted the bright chariot of a thousand (pillar) feet, golden-coloured, of excellent movement and free from fault, let us two be united.

##### English translation
Handsome-jawed (Indra), householder, mount your golden chariot: then let us meet mounted together on that bright thousand-footed brilliant auspiciously-moving sinless (car).

### Printed p. 42

#### Ṛk 17

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तं घे॑मि॒त्था न॑म॒स्विन॒ उप॑ स्व॒राज॑मासते ।  
> अर्थं॑ चिदस्य॒ सुधि॑तं॒ यदेत॑व आव॒र्तयं॑ति दा॒वने॑ ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तम् । घ । ईम् । इत्था । नमस्विनः । उप । स्वऽराजम् । आसते ।  
> अर्थम् । चित् । अस्य । सुऽधितम् । यत् । एतवे । आऽवर्तयंति । दावने ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> तं घ तं खलु ईम् एनमिंद्रम् इत्था इत्थमनेन प्रकारेण नमस्विनः अन्नवंतः स्तुतिवंतो वाध्वर्य्वादयः स्वराजं स्वयं राजमानं वा उप आसते सेवंते । तथा कृत्वा अर्थं चित् अरणीयं धनं सुधितं सुष्ठु स्थापितम् अस्य इंद्रस्य संबंधिनं प्राप्नुवंतीति शेषः । कदेति आह । यत् यदा एतवे अस्येंद्रस्य गमनाय स्वयं प्राप्तुं वा दावने हविर्दानाय आवर्तयंति स्तुतयः तदेत्यर्थः । अथवाश्वा वावर्तयंति तदेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Him, this very Indra, in this manner, the devout, that is, the offering-bearing or praising Adhvaryus and the others, attend upon, that is, serve, the self-ruling one, or the self-shining. Having done so, they obtain the desirable wealth, well placed, belonging to this Indra; "they obtain" is to be supplied. When? When, for his going, or for reaching of oneself, the praises turn him to the giving of the oblation; or else, when the horses turn him.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- स्वराजं (*svarājaṃ*)—the self-shining one
- तं (*taṃ*)—and famous
- ईं घ (*īṃ gha*)—this Indra himself
- नमस्विनः (*namasvinaḥ*)—the Adhvaryus who bear the oblation
- इत्था (*itthā*)—in this manner
- उप आसते (*upa āsate*)—serve
- यत् (*yat*)—when
- दावने (*dāvane*)—for the giving of the oblation
- एतवे (*etave*)—to go
- आवर्तयंति (*āvartayaṃti*)—the horses of this Indra are ready, then
- अस्य (*asya*)—belonging to this Indra
- सुधितं (*sudhitaṃ*)—and well placed
- अर्थं चित् (*arthaṃ cit*)—the wealth itself they obtain

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The Adhvaryus, who bear the oblation, serve in this manner this same Indra, self-shining and famous. When, for going to give the oblation, the horses of this Indra become ready (or, their praises become ready for the giving of the oblation), then they obtain the wealth belonging to this Indra and well placed.

### Printed p. 43

##### English translation
(The priests), presenting praise, thus worship that self-resplendent (Indra) ; they obtain his well-stored wealth, when (his horses) bring him on his way for the offering.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

This Ṛk beginning तॆंघोमित्रा (*tèṃghomitrā*) is to be recited in the Pravargyeṣṭi performed in the forenoon, as is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, तेघोमित्रा नमस्विन इति प्रागाथीं पूर्वाह्णे (*teghomitrā namasvina iti prāgāthīṃ pūrvāhṇe*) (Ā. 4-7) **NOTE**.

#### Ṛk 18

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अनु॑ प्र॒त्नस्यौक॑सः प्रि॒यमे॑धास एषाम् ।  
> पूर्वा॒मनु॒ प्रय॑तिं वृ॒क्तब॑र्हिषो हि॒तप्र॑यस आशत ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अनु । प्रत्नस्य । ओकसः । प्रियऽमेधासः । एषाम् ।  
> पूर्वाम् । अनु । प्रऽयतिम् । वृक्तऽबर्हिषः । हितऽप्रयसः । आशत ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अनया स्तुतिमुपसंहरति । एषां देवानामिंद्रादीनां प्रत्नस्य पुराणस्य ओकसः स्थानस्य पुराणं स्थानं प्रियमेधासः प्रियमेधाः अनु आशत अनुप्राप्ताः । कीदृशाः प्रियमेधाः । पूर्वां मुख्यं प्रयतिं प्रदानम् अनु लक्षीकृत्य वृक्तबर्हिषः स्तीर्णदर्भाः हितप्रयसः आसादितसोमादिहविष्काः ॥ ॥ ७ ॥ ॥ ७ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

With this Ṛk Sāyaṇa concludes the praise. The Priyamedhas, the descendants of Priyamedha, have reached the ancient dwelling-place of these deities, Indra and the others; प्रत्नस्य (*pratnasya*) means "ancient" and ओकसः (*okasaḥ*) means "place", so that the whole is the old place, and आशत (*āśata*) is explained as "have attained". He then asks what kind of Priyamedhas these are. They have in view the foremost (chief) offering, and they have strewn the sacred grass; they have set in readiness the soma and the other oblations.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पूर्व्यं (*pūrvyaṃ*)—the most chief
- प्रयुतिं (*prayutiṃ*)—the offering
- अनु (*anu*)—with a view to (to present the offering)
- वृक्तबर्हिषः (*vṛktabarhiṣaḥ*)—those who have spread the seat of darbha grass
- हितप्रयसः (*hitaprayasaḥ*)—and who are possessed of the oblation
- प्रियमेधासः (*priyamedhāsaḥ*)—the descendants of Priyamedha
- एषां (*eṣāṃ*)—of these deities, Indra and the others
- प्रत्नस्य (*pratnasya*)—ancient
- ओकसः (*okasaḥ*)—the place
- आनु आशत (*ānu āśata*)—have reached

### Printed p. 44

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The descendants of Priyamedha, who have spread the seat of darbha grass for the sake of the chief offering and are possessed of the oblation, have reached the ancient place of these deities, Indra and the others.

##### English translation
The Priyamedhas have reached the ancient dwelling-place of these deities, having strewed the sacred grass and placed their oblations after the manner of a pre-eminent offering.

## Sūkta 70 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अष्टमेऽनुवाक एकादश सूक्तानि । तत्र  ‘ यो राजा ' इति पंचदशर्चं प्रथम सूक्तम् । अत्रानुक्रमणिका-- ' यो राजा पंचोना पुरुहन्मा बार्हतं त्रिप्रगाथाद्युष्णिगनुष्टुप्पुरउष्णिगंतम्' इति । पुरुहन्मा ऋषिः । स च क्वचित् कथंचिदविशेषितम्' (अनु. २, ३) इति परिभाषयांगिरसः । आद्यातृतीयापंचम्यो बृहत्यः। द्वितीयाचतुर्थीषष्ठ्यः सतोबृहत्यः । सप्तम्याद्याः षट् बृहत्यः । त्रयोदश्युष्णिक् चतुर्दश्यनुष्टुप् पंचदशी पुरउष्णिगाद्यद्वादशका द्व्यष्टका । ' आद्यश्चेत्पुरउष्णिक्' (अनु. ५.२ ) इति हि तल्लक्षणम् । इंद्रो देवता। महाव्रते निष्केवल्ये बार्हततृचाशीतावादित एकादशर्चः । तथा च पंचमारण्यके सूत्रितं -- यो राजा चर्षणीनामित्येकादश' (ऐ. आ. ५. २. ४) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The eighth Anuvāka has eleven sūktas, and the first of them is the sūkta of fifteen Ṛks beginning यो राजा (*yo rājā*). The Anukramaṇikā describes it in its own words, as a बार्हतं (*bārhataṃ*) sūkta with Ṛks in the three prāgāthas, the first of them beginning with उष्णिक् (*uṣṇik*), then अनुष्टुप् (*anuṣṭup*), and ending with पुरउष्णिक् (*purauṣṇik*). The seer is Puruhanman, who, by the general rule of the Anukramaṇikā (Anu. 2, 3) that a seer not otherwise specified belongs to the Aṅgirases, is an Āṅgirasa. The first, third and fifth Ṛks are बृहत्यः (*bṛhatyaḥ*), the second, fourth and sixth are सतोबृहत्यः (*satobṛhatyaḥ*), and the six from the seventh onward are बृहत्यः (*bṛhatyaḥ*). The thirteenth is उष्णिक् (*uṣṇik*), the fourteenth अनुष्टुप् (*anuṣṭup*), and the fifteenth पुरउष्णिक् (*purauṣṇik*), which is a metre of twelve and two times eight syllables, since the definition is that when the first is a पुरउष्णिक् (*purauṣṇik*) (Anu. 5.2). The deity is Indra. In the Mahāvrata, in the Niṣkevalya recitation, the first eleven Ṛks are used for the बार्हत (*bārhata*) triplet, and so it is laid down in the fifth Āraṇyaka, यो राजा चर्षणीनामित्येकादश (*yo rājā carṣaṇīnāmityekādaśa*) (Ai. Ā. 5. 2. 4).

In the eighth Anuvāka of the eighth Maṇḍala there are eleven sūktas. Among them this sūkta beginning यो राजा (*yo rājā*) is the first. It has fifteen Ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: यो राजा पंचोना पुरुहन्मा जार्पतं त्रिप्रगाथाद्युष्णिगनुष्टुप्पुरउष्णिगंतं (*yo rājā paṃconā puruhanmā jārpataṃ tripragāthādyuṣṇiganuṣṭuppurauṣṇigaṃtaṃ*) **NOTE**. The seer of this sūkta is Puruhanman; this seer is stated in the Anukramaṇikā (Anu. 2-3) to belong to the Āṅgirasa gotra. The first, third and fifth Ṛks of this sūkta are in the बृहती (*bṛhatī*) metre. The second, fourth and sixth Ṛks are in the सतोबृहती (*satobṛhatī*) metre. The six Ṛks from the seventh onward are in the बृहती (*bṛhatī*) metre. The thirteenth is in the उष्णिक् (*uṣṇik*), the fourteenth in the अनुष्टुप् (*anuṣṭup*) and the fifteenth in the पुरउष्णिक् (*purauṣṇik*) metre. If a Ṛk has three pādas, with twelve syllables in the first pāda and eight

### Printed p. 45

syllables each in the remaining ones, that metre is called पुरउष्णिक् (*purauṣṇik*); such is its definition. Indra is the deity. In the Mahāvratesṭi, when the Niṣkevalya-śastra mantras are recited, the first eleven Ṛks of this sūkta are employed for the Ṛks in the बृहती (*bṛhatī*) metre, as is specified in the fifth Āraṇyaka of the Aitareya Āraṇyaka by the sentence यो राजा चर्षणीनामित्येकादश (*yo rājā carṣaṇīnāmityekādaśa*) (Ai. Ā. 5-2-4).

Sūkta 70 of Maṇḍala 8; Anuvāka 8.

Aṣṭaka 6; Adhyāya 5; Varga 8, 9, 10.

Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 15.

Seer: Puruhanmā.

Deity: Indra.

Metre: 1-5. Prāgātha (the odd ones बृहत्यः (*bṛhatyaḥ*), the even ones सतोबृहत्यः (*satobṛhatyaḥ*)). 7-12. बृहती (*bṛhatī*) **NOTE**. 13. उष्णिक् (*uṣṇik*). 14. अनुष्टुप् (*anuṣṭup*). 15. पुरउष्णिक् (*purauṣṇik*).

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यो राजा॑ चर्षणी॒नां याता॒ रथे॑भि॒रध्रि॑गुः ।  
> विश्वा॑सां तरु॒ता पृत॑नानां॒ ज्येष्ठो॒ यो वृ॑त्र॒हा गृ॒णे ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । राजा । चर्षणीनाम् । याता । रथेभिः । अध्रिऽगुः ।  
> विश्वासाम् । तरुता । पृतनानाम् । ज्येष्ठः । यः । वृत्रऽहा । गृणे ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यः इंद्रः चर्षणीनां मनुष्याणां राजा स्वामी रथैः याता गंता च अध्रिगुः अधृतगमनोऽन्यैः विश्वासां पृतनानां सेनानां तरुता तारकः । यः च ज्येष्ठः गुणैर्ज्यायान् । यश्च वृत्रहा वृत्रं हतवान् । तं महाभागमिंद्रं गृणे स्तौमि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Indra who is the lord of the people (mankind), who travels in chariots and whose going is not held back by others, who is the one who carries across all the armies, who is the eldest, that is, the greatest in qualities, and who is the slayer of Vṛtra, that glorious Indra the seer praises.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—the Indra who
- चर्षणीनां (*carṣaṇīnāṃ*)—of all mankind
- राजा (*rājā*)—is the lord
- अध्रिगुः (*adhriguḥ*)—of a going not stopped by others
- रथेभिः (*rathebhiḥ*)—in chariots (with chariots)

### Printed p. 46

- याता (*yātā*)—and moves about
- विश्वासां (*viśvāsāṃ*)—of all
- पृतनानां (*pṛtanānāṃ*)—the armies
- तरुता (*tarutā*)—and is the protector
- यः (*yaḥ*)—the Indra who
- ज्येष्ठः (*jyeṣṭhaḥ*)—is the most eminent in qualities
- वृत्रहा (*vṛtrahā*)—and is the slayer of Vṛtra (that Indra)
- गृणे (*gṛṇe*)—I praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

I praise that Indra who is the lord of all mankind, who moves about in chariots with a going not stopped by others, and who is the protector of all armies, and who is the most eminent in qualities and the slayer of Vṛtra.

##### English translation
I praise that Indra who is the lord of men, who proceeds irresistible in his chariots, the breaker-through of all armies, the pre-eminent one, the slayer of Vṛtra.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्रं॒ तं शुं॑भ पुरुहन्म॒न्नव॑से॒ यस्य॑ द्वि॒ता वि॑ध॒र्तरि॑ ।  
> हस्ता॑य॒ वज्रः॒ प्रति॑ धायि दर्श॒तो म॒हो दि॒वे न सूर्यः॑ ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्रम् । तम् । शुंभ । पुरुऽहन्मन् । अवसे । यस्य । द्विता । विऽधर्तरि ।  
> हस्ताय । वज्रः । प्रति । धायि । दर्शतः । महः । दिवे । न । सूर्यः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे पुरुहन्मन् ऋषे त्वं तम् इंद्रं शुंभ हविष्प्रदानादिनालंकुरु । किमर्थम् । अवसे रक्षणाय । एवमात्मानं संबोध्य ब्रवीति । यस्य तव विधर्तरि विधारक इंद्रे द्विता द्वित्वमस्त्यौग्य्मनौग्र्यम् । तव शत्रून् हंतुमुग्रत्वं त्वदनुग्रहायानौग्र्यं चेति द्वैतमस्ति । तत्रौग्र्यचिह्नं दर्शयति । हस्ताय कराय हननाय शत्रूणां दर्शतः दर्शनीयः महः महान् वज्रः प्रति धायि प्रतिनिहितो भवति दिवे प्रकाशाय दिवसाय वा अथवा दिवि दृश्यमानः सूर्यः इव ॥
> तस्मिन्नेव शस्त्रे ‘नकिष्टम्' इति प्रगाथो वैकल्पिकः स्तोत्रियः । सूत्रितं च - ‘ नकिष्टं कर्मणा नशन्न त्वा बृहंतो अद्रयः' (आश्व. श्रौ. ७. ४) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O seer Puruhanman, adorn that Indra with the offering of oblations and the like, for protection." Thus the seer addresses himself. In you the upholder, Indra, there is a twofold nature: fierceness, to slay your enemies, and non-fierceness, for favour to you. He shows the mark of the fierceness: the glorious, great thunderbolt is placed in the hand for the slaying of enemies, like the sun seen in heaven, for light or for the day.

In that same Śastra the pragātha नकिष्टं (*nakiṣṭaṃ*) is an optional stotriya, and it is laid down in the sūtra नकिष्टं कर्मणा नशन्न त्वा बृहंतो अद्रयः (*nakiṣṭaṃ karmaṇā naśanna tvā bṛhaṃto adrayaḥ*) (Āśv. Śrau. 7. 4) **NOTE**.

### Printed p. 47

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पुरुहन्मन् (*puruhanman*)—O seer named Puruhanman
- यस्य (*yasya*)—in which
- विधर्तरि (*vidhartari*)—Indra, your upholder
- द्विता (*dvitā*)—there are instruments of two kinds, restraint and favour
- तं इंद्रं (*taṃ iṃdraṃ*)—that Indra
- अवसे (*avase*)—for protection
- शुंभ (*śuṃbha*)—adorn with offerings of oblations and the like
- दिवे (*dive*)—for light in the daytime
- सूर्यः न (*sūryaḥ na*)—as the sun
- हस्ताय (*hastāya*)—for the slaying of enemies
- दर्शतः (*darśataḥ*)—of a pleasing form
- महः (*mahaḥ*)—and of a great nature
- वज्रः (*vajraḥ*)—the thunderbolt
- प्रतिधायि (*pratidhāyi*)—has been taken up by Indra

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O seer named Puruhanman, adorn with offerings of oblations and the like, for your protection, that Indra, your upholder, in whom there are instruments of two kinds, restraint and favour. For the slaying of enemies the thunderbolt, of a pleasing form and of a great nature, like the sun for light in the daytime, has been taken up by Indra.

##### English translation
Puruhanman, honour that Indra for your protection, for in your upholder there is a two-fold might ; he holds in his hand ( to smite his enemies) the glorious thunderbolt great as the sun in heaven.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नकि॒ष्टं कर्म॑णा नश॒द्यश्च॒कार॑ स॒दावृ॑धम् ।  
> इंद्रं॒ न य॒ज्ञैर्वि॒श्वगू॑र्त॒मृभ्व॑स॒मधृ॑ष्टं धृ॒ष्ण्वो॑जसम् ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नकिः । तम् । कर्मणा । नशत् । यः । चकार । सदाऽवृधम् ।  
> इंद्रम् । न । यज्ञैः । विश्वऽगूर्तम् । ऋभ्वसम् । अधृष्टम् । धृष्णुऽओजसम् ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> तं जनमन्यो मर्षको जनः कर्मणा हननादिव्यापारेण नकिः नशत् नैव व्याप्नोति । यः इंद्रं चकार इंद्रमेवानुकूलं यज्ञैः साधनैः । कीदृशमिंद्रम् । सदावृधं सदा वर्धकं विश्वगूर्त सर्वैः स्तुत्यम् ऋभ्वसं महांतम् अधृष्टम् अन्यैः धृष्ण्वोजसं धर्षकबलम् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

No other person, no hostile man, reaches that man by any action such as slaying: him who, by the means of sacrifices, has made Indra favourable to himself. Sāyaṇa then describes Indra: always the increaser, to be praised by all, great, not to be overcome by others, and of daring (overcoming) strength.

### Printed p. 48

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—the sacrificer who
- सदावृधः (*sadāvṛdhaḥ*)—who always grows
- विश्वगूर्तं (*viśvagūrtaṃ*)—who is to be praised by all
- ऋभ्वसं (*ṛbhvasaṃ*)—the great-souled
- अधृष्टं (*adhṛṣṭaṃ*)—not struck back by enemies
- धृष्ण्वोजसं (*dhṛṣṇvojasaṃ*)—and of a daring strength
- इंद्रं न (*iṃdraṃ na*)—Indra himself
- यज्ञैः (*yajñaiḥ*)—with the oblations and the like, the means of the sacrifice
- चकार (*cakāra*)—worships
- तं (*taṃ*)—that sacrificer
- कर्मणा (*karmaṇā*)—by deeds of violence and the like
- नकिः नशत् (*nakiḥ naśat*)—none among his enemies can harm

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

None among his enemies can harm, by deeds of violence and the like, that sacrificer who worships with the oblations and the like, the means of the sacrifice, Indra himself, who always grows, is to be praised by all, is great-souled, not struck back by enemies and of a daring strength.

##### English translation
None can touch him by his deeds, who has made Indra his friend by sacrifices,—(Indra) ever giving fresh strength, to be hymned by all, great, unconquered, of ever-daring might.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the Niṣkevalya-śastra mantras, the pragātha beginning नकिष्टं (*nakiṣṭaṃ*) (Ṛks 3-4) is to be recited as an optional stotriya mantra, as is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, नकिष्टं कर्मणा नशन्न त्वा बृहंतो अद्रयः (*nakiṣṭaṃ karmaṇā naśanna tvā bṛhaṃto adrayaḥ*) (Ā. 7-4).

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अषा॑ळ्हमु॒ग्रं पृत॑नासु सास॒हिं यस्मि॑न्म॒हीरु॑रु॒ज्रयः॑ ।  
> सं धे॒नवो॒ जाय॑माने अनोनवु॒र्द्यावः॒ क्षामो॑ अनोनवुः ॥ ४ ॥

### Printed p. 49

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अषाळ्हम् । उग्रम् । पृतनासु । ससहिम् । यस्मिन् । महीः । उरुऽज्रयः ।  
> सम् । धेनवः । जायमाने । अनोनवुः । द्यावः । क्षामः । अनोनवुः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अषाळ्हम् असोढम् उग्रम् उद्गूर्णबलं पृतनासु शत्रुसेनासु सासहिम् अभिभवितारं स्तौमीत्यर्थः । यस्मिन् इंद्रे जायमाने महीः महत्यः उरुज्रयः बहुवेगाः धेनवः हविरादिना प्रीणयित्र्यः प्रजा गाव एव वा सम् अनोनवुः समस्तुवन् । न केवलं धेनव एव अपि तु द्यावः द्युलोकाः क्षामः पृथिव्यश्च सम् अनोनवुः । तत्रत्याः सर्वे प्राणिनो नमंत इत्यर्थः।  ‘ त्रिवृतो लोकाः (ऐ. ब्रा. २. १७) इति श्रुतेर्बहुवचनम् ॥
> तृतीयेऽहनि निष्केवल्ये वैरूपसामपक्षे ‘यद्द्यावः' इति प्रगाथः स्तोत्रियः । सूत्रितं च -- ‘यद्याव इंद्र ते शतं यदिंद्र यावतस्त्वमिति प्रगाथौ स्तोत्रियानुरूपौ' (आश्व. श्रौ. ७. १०) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"I praise him who is not to be withstood, the fierce one of uplifted strength, the overcomer in the enemy hosts": so the verse is to be construed. Indra, at whose birth the great, much-speeding milch-cows, that is, the subjects who please him with oblations and the like, or else the cows themselves, praised him together: not the cows alone, but also the heavens (the worlds of heaven) and the earths praised him together; that is, all the creatures there bow to him. The plural is used because of the Vedic statement त्रिवृतो लोकाः (*trivṛto lokāḥ*) (Ai. Brā. 2. 17).

On the third day, in the Niṣkevalya, in the alternative with the Vairūpa Sāman, the pragātha यद्द्यावः (*yaddyāvaḥ*) is the stotriya. And it is laid down in the sūtra यद्याव इंद्र ते शतं यदिंद्र यावतस्त्वमिति प्रगाथौ स्तोत्रियानुरूपौ (*yadyāva iṃdra te śataṃ yadiṃdra yāvatastvamiti pragāthau stotriyānurūpau*) (Āśv. Śrau. 7. 10) **NOTE**.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यस्मिन् (*yasmin*)—the Indra whom
- जायमाने (*jāyamāne*)—as soon as he was born
- महीः (*mahīḥ*)—of great nature
- उरुज्रयः (*urujrayaḥ*)—and of much speed
- धेनवः (*dhenavaḥ*)—the cows
- समुनोनवुः (*samunonavuḥ*)—praised, and
- द्यावः (*dyāvaḥ*)—the heavens
- क्षामः (*kṣāmaḥ*)—and the earth, joining together
- अनोनवुः (*anonavuḥ*)—bowed to (such an Indra)
- अषाळ्हं (*aṣāḷhaṃ*)—the unresisted
- उग्रं (*ugraṃ*)—the terrible
- पृतनासु (*pṛtanāsu*)—among the hosts of enemies
- सासहिं (*sāsahiṃ*)—of exceeding strength, that Indra (I praise)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

I praise that Indra, the unresisted, the terrible, of exceeding strength among the hosts of enemies, whom, as soon as he was born, the cows of great nature and much speed praised, and whom the heavens and the earth joining together bowed to.

##### English translation
(I laud) him who is not to be withstood, the mighty, the conqueror in hostile hosts ; whom, when he was born, the strong rushing cows wel-comed and the heavens and the earths praised.

### Printed p. 50

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यद्द्याव॑ इंद्र ते श॒तं श॒तं भूमी॑रु॒त स्युः ।  
> न त्वा॑ वज्रिन्स॒हस्रं॒ सूर्या॒ अनु॒ न जा॒तम॑ष्ट॒ रोद॑सी ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यत् । द्यावः । इंद्र । ते । शतम् । शतम् । भूमीः । उत । स्युरिति स्युः ।  
> न । त्वा । वज्रिन् । सहस्रम् । सूर्याः । अनु । न । जातम् । अष्ट । रोदसी इति ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र ते तव प्रतिमानार्थं यत् यदि द्यावः द्युलोकाः स्युः तथापि नाश्नुवंति । उत अपि च भूमीः भूम्यस्ते तव मूर्तिप्रतिबिंबाय शतं स्युः तथापि नाश्नुवंति । हे वज्रिन् त्वा त्वां सहस्रं सूर्याः अगणिता अपि सूर्याः न अनु भवंति । न प्रकाशयंतीत्यर्थः। ‘न तत्र सूर्यो भाति' ( क. उ. ५. १५) इति श्रुतेः । किं बहुना । जातं पूर्वमुत्पन्नं किंचन न अष्ट नाश्नुते । तथा रोदसी द्यावापृथिव्यौ नाश्नुवाते । सर्वेभ्योऽतिरिच्यस इत्यर्थः। ‘ज्यायान्पृथिव्या ज्यायानंतरिक्षाज्ज्यायांदिवो ज्यायानेभ्यो लोकेभ्यः' (छा. उ. ३. १४. ३) इति श्रुतेः ॥ ॥ ८ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Indra, even if there were a hundred heavens to match you, they would not attain to you; and if there were a hundred earths for the image of your form, they too would not attain. O wielder of the thunderbolt, a thousand suns, even countless suns, do not shine after you", that is, they do not illuminate you, as the Śruti says, न तत्र सूर्यो भाति (*na tatra sūryo bhāti*) (Ka. U. 5. 15) **NOTE**. What more need be said: nothing born earlier attains you, and likewise heaven and earth do not attain you; you exceed all, as the Śruti says, ज्यायान्पृथिव्या ज्यायानंतरिक्षाज्ज्यायांदिवो ज्यायानेभ्यो लोकेभ्यः (*jyāyānpṛthivyā jyāyānaṃtarikṣājjyāyāṃdivo jyāyānebhyo lokebhyaḥ*) (Chā. U. 3. 14. 3).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ते (*te*)—to compare with you
- यत् (*yat*)—if perchance
- शतं (*śataṃ*)—in the number of a hundred
- द्यावः (*dyāvaḥ*)—the heavens
- स्युः (*syuḥ*)—were
- उत (*uta*)—and
- शतं भूमिः (*śataṃ bhūmiḥ*)—if there were a hundred earths (they could not equal you)
- वज्रिन् (*vajrin*)—O Indra, bearer of the thunderbolt
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- सहस्रं (*sahasraṃ*)—a thousand in number
- सूर्याः (*sūryāḥ*)—even suns

### Printed p. 51

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- न अनु (*na anu*)—cannot equal your radiance
- जातं (*jātaṃ*)—any created thing
- न अष्ट (*na aṣṭa*)—cannot pervade you
- रोदसी (*rodasī*)—even heaven and earth cannot pervade you

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, to compare with you, even if the heavens were a hundred in number and even if there were a hundred earths, they could not equal you. O Indra, bearer of the thunderbolt, even a thousand suns cannot equal your radiance (all of them are made radiant by you). No created thing, nor heaven and earth, can pervade you.

##### English translation
Indra, were there one hundred heavens to compare with you, or were there one hundred earths,—O thunderer, not even a thousand suns would reveal you,—yes, no created thing would fill you, nor heaven and earth.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

On the third day of the sacrifice, when the Niṣkevalya-śastra mantras are recited, in the alternative with the Vairūpa Sāman, this pragātha beginning यद्द्यावः (*yaddyāvaḥ*) (Ṛks 5-6) is to be recited as a stotriya pragātha mantra **NOTE**, as is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, यद्द्याव इंद्र ते शतं यदिंद्र यावतस्त्वमिति प्रगाथौ स्तोत्रियानुरूपौ (*yaddyāva iṃdra te śataṃ yadiṃdra yāvatastvamiti pragāthau stotriyānurūpau*) (Ā. 7-10).

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ प॑प्राथ महि॒ना वृष्ण्या॑ वृष॒न्विश्वा॑ शविष्ठ॒ शव॑सा ।  
> अ॒स्माँ अ॑व मघव॒न्गोम॑ति व्र॒जे वज्रिं॑चि॒त्राभि॑रू॒तिभिः॑ ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । पप्राथ । महिना । वृष्ण्या । वृषन् । विश्वा । शविष्ठ । शवसा ।  
> अस्मान् । अव । मघऽवन् । गोऽमति । व्रजे । वज्रिन् । चित्राभिः । ऊतिऽभिः ॥ ६ ॥

### Printed p. 52

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वृषन् अभिमतवर्षकेंद्र त्वम् आ पप्राथ आपूरयसि व्याप्नोषि । कानि। विश्वा सर्वाणि वृष्ण्या वर्षकाणि बलानि शत्रुसंबंधीनि । केन साधनेन । महिना महता शवसा बलेन स्वीयेन । अथवा वृष्ण्येत्येतच्छवोविशेषणम्। तथा सति अभिमतवर्षकेण महता बलेनास्मदीयानि बलानि पूरयसीत्यर्थः । अथ तथा कृत्वा अस्मान् गोमति बहुभिर्गोभिर्युक्ते व्रजे शत्रुसंबंधिनि निमित्ते सत्यस्मान् अव हे वज्रिन् वज्रयुक्तेंद्र। कैः साधनैः। चित्राभिः नानाविधैः ऊतिभिः रक्षणैरिति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O showerer of desired things, Indra, you fill, that is, pervade, all the showering strengths belonging to the enemies": by what means? By your own great strength. Or else the word for "showering" is an epithet of strength; then the meaning is that with great strength that showers desired things you fill our strengths. Having done so, when there is occasion in the enemies' pen rich in many cows, protect us, O wielder of the thunderbolt, with manifold protections.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शविष्ठ (*śaviṣṭha*)—O Indra, most powerful
- वृषन् (*vṛṣan*)—and showerer of desires
- महिना (*mahinā*)—great
- शवसा (*śavasā*)—by your strength
- विश्वा (*viśvā*)—all
- वृष्ण्या (*vṛṣṇyā*)—the army belonging to the enemy
- आ पप्राथ (*ā paprātha*)—you have pervaded and overrun
- मघवन् (*maghavan*)—O Indra, possessor of wealth
- वज्रिन् (*vajrin*)—and bearer of the thunderbolt
- गोमति (*gomati*)—full of cows
- व्रजे (*vraje*)—in the cowpens of the enemies (when we go to obtain those cows)
- अस्मान् (*asmān*)—us
- चित्राभिः (*citrābhiḥ*)—with manifold
- ऊतिभिः (*ūtibhiḥ*)—protections
- अव (*ava*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, most powerful and showerer of desires, by your great strength you have pervaded and overrun all the armies of the enemy. O Indra, possessor of wealth and bearer of the thunderbolt, when we go to obtain cows in the cow-filled cowpens of the enemies, protect us with manifold protections.

##### English translation
Mightiest showerer (of blessings), you have filled all (our hosts) with your vast bountiful power ; O Maghavan, thunderer, guard us with your manifold protections, (when we march) against the well-stocked cowpen of our enemies.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न सी॒मदे॑व आप॒दिषं॑ दीर्घायो॒ मर्त्यः॑ ।  
> एत॑ग्वा चि॒द्य एत॑शा यु॒योज॑ते॒ हरी॒ इंद्रो॑ यु॒योज॑ते ॥ ७ ॥

### Printed p. 53

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> न । सीम् । अदेवः । आपत् । इषम् । दीर्घायो इति दीर्घऽआयो । मर्त्यः ।  
> एतऽग्वा । चित् । यः । एतशा । युयोजते । हरी इति । इंद्रः । युयोजते ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे दीर्घायो नित्येंद्र सः अदेवः इंद्राख्यदेवरहितः मर्त्यः मरणधर्मा मनुष्यः सीं सर्वम् इषम् अन्नं न आपत् न प्राप्नोति । यः मर्त्योऽस्येंद्रस्य एतग्वा चित् । एतवर्णावेवाश्वौ भवतोऽभिमतदेशगमनाय । एतशा एतशावश्वौ युयोजते योजयति रथे यज्ञं गंतुम् । यश्च इंद्रः हरी युयोजते तं न यः स्तौति । स न प्राप्नोतीति समन्वयः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O long-lived Indra, the mortal who is without the god called Indra does not obtain any food." Which mortal? The one who does not praise this Indra, whose horses are of the Eta colour, for going to the desired place, and who yokes the two Etaśa horses to his chariot to go to the sacrifice, and who yokes the two bay horses. The connection is that whoever does not praise him does not obtain food.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- दीर्घायो (*dīrghāyo*)—O Indra of eternal life
- यः (*yaḥ*)—you who
- एतग्वा चित् (*etagvā cit*)—of many kinds of colour
- एतशा (*etaśā*)—the horses
- युयोजते (*yuyojate*)—yoke to your chariot
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—(you who), as lord
- हरी (*harī*)—the horses called the bays
- युयोजते (*yuyojate*)—yoke to your chariot (not praising such a you)
- अदेवः (*adevaḥ*)—not regarding you as a god
- मर्त्यः (*martyaḥ*)—the man
- सीं (*sīṃ*)—certainly everywhere
- इषं (*iṣaṃ*)—food
- न आपत् (*na āpat*)—does not obtain at all

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra of eternal life, you who yoke to your chariot the horses of many kinds of colour, you who as lord yoke the horses called the bays: the man who does not praise such a you and does not regard you as a god certainly obtains food nowhere at all.

##### English translation
O long-lived Indra, the mortal who has not you as his deity obtains no food ; (he who praises not) that steed-borne Indra, who yokes to his car the two variegated, who yokes the two bay steeds.

### Printed p. 54

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तं वो॑ म॒हो म॒हाय्य॒मिंद्रं॑ दा॒नाय॑ स॒क्षणि॑म् ।  
> यो गा॒धेषु॒ य आर॑णेषु॒ हव्यो॒ वाजे॒ष्वस्ति॒ हव्यः॑ ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तम् । वः । महः । महाय्यम् । इंद्रम् । दानाय । सक्षणिम् ।  
> यः । गाधेषु । यः । आऽअरणेषु । हव्यः । वाजेषु । अस्ति । हव्यः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे ऋत्विजः महः महांतः वः यूयं तं महाय्यं पूज्यम् इंद्र दानाय सक्षणिं सचमानं परिचरतेति शेषः । यः इंद्रः गाधेषु उदकेषु हव्यः अस्ति आह्वातव्यो भवति । यः च आरणेषु गंतव्येषु निम्नेषूदकेषु स्थलेषु वा हव्योऽस्ति । तथा वाजेषु संग्रामेषु जयाय हव्यः ह्वातव्योऽस्ति भवति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O priests, you who are great, serve that worshipful Indra, who is accompanying you for the gift." Indra is to be invoked in the waters, in the deep places which are hard to go to, whether waters or dry ground, and likewise in the battles, to be called for victory.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—(O priests) the Indra who
- गाधेषु (*gādheṣu*)—in the waters
- हव्यः (*havyaḥ*)—is called for help
- अस्ति (*asti*)—is
- यः (*yaḥ*)—who
- आरणेषु (*āraṇeṣu*)—in the low places hard to pass through
- वाजेषु (*vājeṣu*)—and in battles
- हव्यः (*havyaḥ*)—is called, such
- तं (*taṃ*)—the famous
- महाय्यं (*mahāyyaṃ*)—the worshipful
- दानाय (*dānāya*)—for the sake of the gift
- सक्षणिं (*sakṣaṇiṃ*)—the one who is together with us
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- महः (*mahaḥ*)—possessed of great qualities
- वः (*vaḥ*)—you (worship)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O priests, the Indra who is called for help in the waters, who is called in the low places hard to pass through and in battles: that famous, worshipful Indra, who is together with us for the sake of the gift, you, possessed of great qualities, worship.

##### English translation
Great (priests), worship that Indra who is propitiated by gifts ; who is to be invoked in the shallows and in the depths, and who is to be invoked in battles.

### Printed p. 55

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उदू॒ षु णो॑ वसो म॒हे मृ॒शस्व॑ शूर॒ राध॑से ।  
> उदू॒ षु म॒ह्यै म॑घवन्म॒घत्त॑य॒ उदिं॑द्र॒ श्रव॑से म॒हे ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत् । ऊं इति । सु । नः । वसो इति । महे । मृशस्व । शूर । राधसे ।  
> उत् । ऊं इति । सु । मह्यै । मघऽवन् । मघत्तये । उत् । इंद्र । श्रवसे । महे ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वसो वासयितः शूर इंद्र त्वं नः अस्मान् सु सुष्ठु उदु मृशस्व उन्मृशस्वैव । उत्थापय । किमर्थम्। महे महते राधसे अन्नाय । तथा हे शूर मघवन् इंद्र उत् मृशस्व मह्यै महते मघत्तये धनदानाय । तथा उत् मृशस्व इंद्र महे महत्यै श्रवसे कीर्त्यै ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Vasu, giver of dwelling, O hero Indra, raise us well up": the verb उदु मृशस्व (*udu mṛśasva*) is explained as "set us up". For what? For great food. Likewise, O hero Maghavan Indra, raise us up for great wealth-giving, and raise us up, Indra, for great fame.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वसो (*vaso*)—O Indra, giver of dwelling
- शूर (*śūra*)—and hero
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- महे (*mahe*)—great
- राधसे (*rādhase*)—for the gaining of food
- सु (*su*)—in the best manner
- उदु मृशस्व (*udu mṛśasva*)—make us rise up
- मघवन् (*maghavan*)—O Indra, possessor of wealth
- मह्यै (*mahyai*)—very great
- मघत्तये (*maghattaye*)—for the giving of wealth also
- सु (*su*)—well
- उदु (*udu*)—make us rise up
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- महे (*mahe*)—very great
- श्रवसे (*śravase*)—for fame also
- उत् (*ut*)—make us rise up

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, giver of dwelling and hero, make us rise up in the best manner for the gaining of great food. O Indra, possessor of wealth, make us rise up well also for the giving of very great wealth. O Indra, make us rise up also for very great fame.

### Printed p. 56

##### English translation
O hero, giver of dwellings, raise us up to enjoy abundant food; raise us up, Maghavan, for abundant wealth; raise us up, Indra, for abundant fame.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वं न॑ इंद्र ऋत॒युस्त्वा॒निदो॒ नि तृं॑पसि ।  
> मध्ये॑ वसिष्व तुविनृम्णो॒र्वोर्नि दा॒सं शि॑श्नथो॒ हथैः॑ ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वम् । नः । इंद्र । ऋतऽयुः । त्वाऽनिदः । नि । तृंपसि ।  
> मध्ये । वसिष्व । तुविऽनृम्ण । ऊर्वोः । नि । दासम् । शिश्नथः । हथैः ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र ऋतयुः यज्ञकामः त्वं नः अस्मान् त्वानिदः । त्वां यो निंदति सः । अयजनमेव निंदा तव । तस्मादयष्टुः सकाशात् नि नितरां तृंपसि प्रीणयसि । तस्य धनमपहृत्येति भावः । एवं संतर्प्य हे तुविनृम्ण प्रभूतधन स त्वं तव ऊर्वोः मध्ये अस्मान् वसिष्व । ऊरुभ्यामाच्छादय रक्षार्थम् । दासम् उपक्षपयितारमस्मद्द्वेषिणं पापं वा हथैः हननैः नि शिश्नथः मारयसि । अथवा त्वद्विरोधिनं दासमसुरं नि शिश्नथो हननैः ॥ ॥ ९ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Indra, desirous of sacrifice, you, from him who reviles you", that is, from the man who does not sacrifice, for not sacrificing is a reproach to you, "thoroughly satisfy us", the sense being that you take away that man's wealth. Having so satisfied us, O Tuvinṛmṇa, possessor of abundant wealth, you cover us between your thighs, for protection. The Dāsa, the one who injures us, our hater, or the sinner, you slay with blows; or else you slay with blows the Dāsa, the Asura, who opposes you.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ऋतयुः (*ṛtayuḥ*)—you who desire sacrifice
- त्वां (*tvāṃ*)—you
- त्वानिदः (*tvānidaḥ*)—from the wicked man who reviles you (having taken away his wealth)
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- नि (*ni*)—fully
- तृंपसि (*tṛṃpasi*)—satisfy
- तुविनृम्ण (*tuvinṛmṇa*)—O Indra, possessor of abundant wealth
- ऊर्वोः (*ūrvoḥ*)—of your thighs
- मध्ये (*madhye*)—in the middle, hiding us
- वसिष्ठ (*vasiṣṭha*)—give shelter for our protection
- दासं (*dāsaṃ*)—the injuring enemy
- हथ्यैः (*hathyaiḥ*)—with instruments of killing
- नि शिश्नथः (*ni śiśnathaḥ*)—destroy entirely

### Printed p. 57

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you who desire sacrifice, take away the wealth of the wicked man who reviles you and fully satisfy us. O Indra, possessor of abundant wealth, hide us in the middle of your thighs and give shelter for our protection; destroy entirely the injuring enemy with instruments of killing.

##### English translation
Indra, who delights in offerings, satisfy us abuudantly with (the possessions of him) who despises you ; O you, possessed of vast wealth, shelter us between your thighs ; smite down the Dasa with your blows.

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒न्यव्र॑त॒ममा॑नुष॒मय॑ज्वान॒मदे॑वयुम् ।  
> अव॒ स्वः सखा॑ दुधुवीत॒ पर्व॑तः सु॒घ्नाय॒ दस्युं॒ पर्व॑तः ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अन्यऽव्रतम् । अमानुषम् । अयज्वानम् । अदेवऽयुम् ।  
> अव । स्वः । सखा । दुधुवीत । पर्वतः । सुऽघ्नाय । दस्युम् । पर्वतः ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र अन्यव्रतं व्यतिरिक्तकर्माणम् अत एव अमानुषं मानुषाणामिंद्रयाजिनामप्रियम् अयज्वानम् अयष्टारम् अदेवयुम् अदेवकर्मिणं पापिनं स्वः स्वर्गात् अव दुधुवीत । अवचालयेदित्यर्थः । कः । सखा पर्वतः तव सखिभूतः पर्वत ऋषिः । यद्यप्यन्यं देवमिष्ट्वा स्वर्गं प्राप्नोति सः तथापि पातयति ऋषिः । न केवलं धूननमात्रमपि तु सुघ्नाय सुष्ठु हंत्रे मृत्यवे दस्युम् उक्तलक्षणं पर्वतः प्रेरयतीति शेषः । अत इंद्रमेवावश्यं यजध्वमिति शेषः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Indra, may Parvata, your friend, the seer, hurl down from heaven the man who follows other rites", one whose action is outside the rule, who for that very reason is not of men, being disagreeable to the men who sacrifice to Indra, who does not sacrifice, who is devoted to what is not god's rite, the sinner. Even if he has gone to heaven by sacrificing to another god, the seer makes him fall. Nor only shaking him down: Parvata sends the Dasyu, of the character described, to the good smiter, death. Therefore sacrifice to Indra alone.

### Printed p. 58

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सखा (*sakhā*)—(O Indra) your friend
- पर्वतः (*parvataḥ*)—the seer named Parvata
- अन्यव्रतं (*anyavrataṃ*)—him who has rites contrary to the ordinance
- अमानुषं (*amānuṣaṃ*)—the enemy of the men who worship you
- अयज्वानं (*ayajvānaṃ*)—who does not sacrifice
- अदेवयुं (*adevayuṃ*)—who does not worship the gods, that sinner
- स्वः (*svaḥ*)—from heaven
- अव दुधुवीत (*ava dudhuvīta*)—may he thrust down
- पर्वतः (*parvataḥ*)—that same Parvata
- दस्युं (*dasyuṃ*)—the injuring enemy
- सुघ्नाय (*sughnāya*)—may he deliver to death, the slayer

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, may the seer named Parvata, your friend, thrust down from heaven that sinner who has rites contrary to the scripture, who is the enemy of the men who worship you, and who does not sacrifice. May that same Parvata deliver the injuring enemy to death, the slayer.

##### English translation
May your friend, Parvata, hurl down from heaven him who follows other rites, the enemy of men, him who offers not sacrifice and who worships not the gods ; may Parvata hurl the Dasyu down to the stern smiter (death).

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वं न॑ इंद्रासां॒ हस्ते॑ शविष्ठ दा॒वने॑ ।  
> धा॒नानां॒ न सं गृ॑भायास्म॒युर्द्विः सं गृ॑भायास्म॒युः ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वम् । नः । इंद्र । आसाम् । हस्ते । शविष्ठ । दावने ।  
> धानानाम् । न । सम् । गृभाय । अस्मऽयुः । द्विः । सम् । गृभाय । अस्मऽयुः ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे शविष्ठ बलवन् इंद्र त्वं नः अस्मभ्यं दावने प्रदानाय आसां गवाम् । कर्मणि षष्ठी । एता गाः हस्ते गृभाय गृहाण । ग्रहणे दृष्टांतः । धानानां न । धाना भृष्टयवाः । अत्रापि कर्मणि षष्ठी । धाना यथा संगृह्णाति तद्वद्गृहाण । कीदृशस्त्वम् । अस्मयुः अस्मान् कामयमानः । गृहीत्वा च अस्मयुः सन् द्विः सं गृभाय । पुनर्द्विवारं संगृहाण ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O mighty Indra, take in your hand these cows, to give to us", and as illustration Sāyaṇa gives धानानां न (*dhānānāṃ na*): the dhānās are parched barley; as one gathers up parched grain, so gather up these cows. What kind are you? Desiring us. Having taken them, being desirous of us, take them again, twice.

### Printed p. 59

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शविष्ठ (*śaviṣṭha*)—of exceeding strength
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- अस्मयुः (*asmayuḥ*)—desiring to help us
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- दावने (*dāvane*)—for giving
- धानानां न (*dhānānāṃ na*)—as one holds grain in the hand
- आसां (*āsāṃ*)—these cows
- हस्ते (*haste*)—in the hand
- सं गृभाय (*saṃ gṛbhāya*)—take hold of
- अस्मयुः (*asmayuḥ*)—desiring to do good to us
- द्विः (*dviḥ*)—two times
- सं गृभाय (*saṃ gṛbhāya*)—lift them in the hand and hold them

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, of exceeding strength, take hold of these cows in your hand, as one holds grain in the hand, to give to us. Desiring to do good to us, lift them in the hand and hold them two times.

##### English translation
Most powerful Indra, in your favour towards us, take these cows in your hand, as fried grain, to give to us ; yes, take them twice in your favour towards us.

#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सखा॑यः॒ क्रतु॑मिच्छत क॒था रा॑धाम श॒रस्य॑ ।  
> उप॑स्तुतिं भो॒जः सू॒रिर्यो अह्र॑यः ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सखायः । क्रतुम् । इच्छत । कथा । राधाम । शरस्य ।  
> उपऽस्तुतिम् । भोजः । सूरिः । यः । अह्रयः ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सखायः ऋत्विजोऽध्वर्य्वादयः क्रतुं कर्म इंद्रसंबंधिनम् इच्छत कर्तुम् । हे सखायः शरस्य । ‘शॄ हिंसायाम्' । हिंसकस्येंद्रस्य कथा कथं राधाम साधयाम । किम् । उपस्तुतिं स्तोत्रम् । यः इंद्रः भोजः शत्रूणां भोजयिता सूरिः प्रेरकः अह्रयः अनवनतः शत्रूणामिति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O friends, priests, the Adhvaryus and the rest, wish to perform the sacrifice that belongs to Indra." O friends, how shall we accomplish the praise, the hymn, of Indra the destroyer, the injurer: he who is the one who makes his enemies feed, the impeller, the one not bowed down before his enemies?

### Printed p. 60

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सखायः (*sakhāyaḥ*)—O priests, who are friends
- क्रतुं (*kratuṃ*)—the sacrificial rite directed to Indra
- इच्छत (*icchata*)—wish to perform
- यः (*yaḥ*)—the Indra who
- भोजः (*bhojaḥ*)—causes his enemies the experience of sorrow
- सूरिः (*sūriḥ*)—the impeller
- अप्रयः (*aprayaḥ*)—who does not bend to his enemies, such an Indra
- शरस्य (*śarasya*)—of the injuring Indra
- उपस्तुतिं (*upastutiṃ*)—the hymn
- कथा (*kathā*)—in what manner
- राधामु (*rādhāmu*)—shall we accomplish

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O priests, who are friends, wish to perform the sacrificial rite directed to Indra. In what manner shall we accomplish the hymn of such an Indra, destroyer of enemies, who causes his enemies the experience of sorrow, the impeller, and who does not bend to his enemies?

##### English translation
Associated priests, give good heed to the sacrifice, for how can we (worthily) perform the praise of (Indra) the destroyer, who is the recompenser of enemies, the sender of reward, the unvanquished ?

#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> भूरि॑भिः समह॒ ऋषि॑भिर्ब॒र्हिष्म॑द्भि स्तविष्यसे ।  
> यदि॒त्थमेक॑मेक॒मिच्छर॑ व॒त्सान्प॑रा॒ददः॑ ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> भूरिऽभिः । समह । ऋषिऽभिः । बर्हिष्मत्ऽभिः । स्तविष्यसे ।  
> यत् । इत्थम् । एकम्ऽएकम् । इत् । शर । वत्सान् । पराऽददः ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र समह समानपूज। सर्वैः पूज्येत्यर्थः। भूरिभिः बहुभिः ऋषिभिः अतींद्रियज्ञैर्ऋत्विग्भिः बर्हिष्मद्भिः यागवद्भिः स्तविष्यसे स्तूयसे। यत् य इंद्रः इत्थमेकमेकमित् एकमेकमेव वत्समित्येवं बहून् वत्सान् स्तोतृभ्यः पराददः प्रयच्छसि। हे शर हिंसक शत्रोः । अत्र वत्सशब्दो वत्समातॄणां गवामुपलक्षकः । गाः प्रयच्छसीति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Indra, equally worshipped, that is, to be worshipped by all, you are praised by many seers, that is, by priests who know what is beyond the senses, by those who have the sacred grass, who are engaged in the sacrifice." The Indra who thus gives away to the praisers one by one so many calves: O destroyer, injurer of the enemy. Here the word "calf" stands for the cows who are the mothers of the calves; the meaning is that you give cows.

### Printed p. 61

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शर (*śara*)—O Indra, destroyer of enemies
- यत् (*yat*)—for which reason
- इत्थं (*itthaṃ*)—in this manner
- एकं एकं इत् (*ekaṃ ekaṃ it*)—of many numbers, saying "this one, another one"
- वत्सान् (*vatsān*)—cows
- पराददः (*parādadaḥ*)—because you give to those who praise you
- समुह (*samuha*)—O Indra, who are worshipped by all alike **NOTE**
- बर्हिष्मद्भिः (*barhiṣmadbhiḥ*)—by those who are engaged in the sacrifice
- भूरिभिः (*bhūribhiḥ*)—and are many in number
- ऋषिभिः (*ṛṣibhiḥ*)—by the seers
- स्तुविष्यसे (*stuviṣyase*)—you are praised

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, destroyer of enemies, because you give to those who praise you cows of many numbers in this manner, saying "this one, another one", therefore, O Indra, who are worshipped by all alike, you are praised by the seers, who are engaged in the sacrifice and are many in number.

##### English translation
Indra, the common object of our worship, you are praised by many sacrificing Rishis ; for it is you, destroyer of foes, who thus give calves in succession to your worshippers.

#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> क॒र्ण॒गृह्या॑ म॒घवा॑ शौरदे॒व्यो व॒त्सं न॑स्त्रि॒भ्य आन॑यत् ।  
> अ॒जां सू॒रिर्न धात॑वे ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> कर्णऽगृह्य । मघऽवा । शौरऽदेव्यः । वत्सम् । नः । त्रिऽभ्यः । आ । अनयत् ।  
> अजाम् । सूरिः । न । धातवे ॥ १५ ॥

### Printed p. 62

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अयं मघवा इंद्रः त्रिभ्यः हिंसकेभ्यः सकाशात् शौरदेव्यः । दीव्यंति क्रीडंत इति देवा योद्धारः। शूराश्च ते देवाश्च शूरदेवाः । तेषां हितं शौरदेवं युद्धम्। तत्संबंधिन्यो गाः। युद्धे शत्रून् हत्वा तत्संबंधिन्य इत्यर्थः । ताः वत्सं न । नेति चार्थे। वत्ससहिता अस्मभ्यं कर्णगृह्य कर्णे गृहीत्वा आनयत् आनयतु । सूरिर्न प्रेरकः स्वामीव धातवे पानाय अजां यथा स आनयति कर्णे गृहीत्वा तद्वदिति ॥ ॥ १० ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Maghavan, Indra, brings to us from three destroying enemies the cows that are called शौरदेव्यः (*śauradevyaḥ*). Those who play, who sport, are the gods, that is, the warriors; they are heroes and they are gods, hence "hero-gods". What is beneficial to them is battle; the cows connected with it are meant, that is, cows connected with it after the enemies have been slain in battle. Let him lead those cows, together with their calves, to us, taking them by the ear. As a master, the impeller, leads a goat by the ear to drink, so let him bring them.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- धातवे (*dhātave*)—for drinking milk
- अजां (*ajāṃ*)—a goat
- सूरिः न (*sūriḥ na*)—as its owner leads it, taking it by the ear
- मघवा (*maghavā*)—Indra, the wealthy
- शौरदेव्यः (*śauradevyaḥ*)—the cows found on the battlefield filled with heroes
- वत्सं न (*vatsaṃ na*)—together with their calves
- कर्णगृह्य (*karṇagṛhya*)—taking them by the ear and dragging them
- त्रिभ्यः (*tribhyaḥ*)—seizing them from the enemies of three kinds
- आनयत् (*ānayat*)—let him bring them to us

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As an owner leads a goat by the ear for drinking milk, so let Indra, the wealthy, taking them by the ear, drag the cows found on the battlefield filled with heroes, together with their calves, and, seizing them from the enemies of three kinds, bring them to us.

##### English translation
May Maghavan, taking them by the ears, lead the cows with their calves from our three (destructive enemies), as the owner leads a goat to drink.

## Sūkta 71 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ‘त्वं नो अग्ने ' इति पंचदशर्चं द्वितीयं सूक्तम् । सुदीतिपुरुमीळ्हावृषी तयोरन्यतरो वा । आदौ नव गायत्र्यो दशमीद्वादशीचतुर्दश्यो बृहत्य एकादशीत्रयोदशीपंचदश्यः सतोबृहत्यः । अग्निर्देवता । तथा चानुक्रांतं - त्वं नः सुदीतिपुरुमीळ्हौ तयोर्वान्यतर आग्नेयं तु त्रिप्रगाथांतम् ' इति । प्रातरनुवाक आग्नेये क्रतौ गायत्रे छंदस्याश्विनशस्त्रे चादितो नवर्चः। तथा च सूत्रितं -- त्वं नो अग्ने महोभिरिति नव ' (आश्व. श्रौ. ४. १३) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The fifteen-Ṛk sūkta beginning त्वं नो अग्ने (*tvaṃ no agne*) is the second sūkta. Its seer is Sudīti or Purumīḷha, or else either one of the two. The deity is Agni. The first nine Ṛks are in the Gāyatrī metre; the tenth, twelfth and fourteenth are in the Bṛhatī metre; the eleventh, thirteenth and fifteenth are in the Satobṛhatī metre. The Anukramaṇikā says as much: that the sūkta is Agni's, that the seer is one or the other of Sudīti and Purumīḷha, and that it ends with three pragāthas. In the Prātaranuvāka, in the Āgneya kratu, in the Gāyatra metre, and in the Āśvinaśastra, the first nine Ṛks are used. So it is laid down in the sūtra त्वं नो अग्ने महोभिरिति नव (*tvaṃ no agne mahobhiriti nava*) (Āśv. Śrau. 4. 13).

### Printed p. 63

The Anuvāda: this sūkta beginning त्वं नो अग्ने (*tvaṃ no agne*) is the second sūkta in the eighth Anuvāka. It has fifteen Ṛks. The seers of this sūkta are the two, Sudīti and Purumīḷha; or it may be some other seer. Agni is the deity. In this sūkta the first nine Ṛks are in the Gāyatrī metre; the tenth, twelfth and fourteenth Ṛks are in the Bṛhatī metre; the eleventh, thirteenth and fifteenth Ṛks are in the Satobṛhatī metre. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: त्वं नः सुदीतिपुरुमीळ्हौ तयोर्वान्यतर आग्नेयं तु त्रिप्रगाथांतं (*tvaṃ naḥ sudītipurumīḷhau tayorvānyatara āgneyaṃ tu tripragāthāṃtaṃ*). At the time of reciting the Prātaranuvāka mantras, the first nine Ṛks of this sūkta are employed as Ṛks of the Gāyatrī metre pertaining to the Āgneya kratu, and for the Āśvinaśastra mantras, as is explained by the sūtra त्वं नो अग्ने महोभिरिति नव (*tvaṃ no agne mahobhiriti nava*) of the Āśvalāyana Śrautasūtra (Ā. 4-13).

Sūkta 71 of Maṇḍala 8; Anuvāka 8.

Aṣṭaka 6; Adhyāya 5; Varga 11, 12, 13.

Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 15.

Seer: सुदीतिपुरुमीळ्हौ तयोर्वान्यतरः (*sudītipurumīḷhau tayorvānyataraḥ*).

Deity: अग्निः (*agniḥ*).

Metre: 1-9. Gāyatrī. 10-15. Pragātha.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वं नो॑ अग्ने॒ महो॑भिः पा॒हि विश्व॑स्या॒ अरा॑तेः ।  
> उ॒त द्वि॒षो मर्त्य॑स्य ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वम् । नः । अग्ने । महःऽभिः । पाहि । विश्वस्याः । अरातेः ।  
> उत । द्विषः । मर्त्यस्य ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने त्वं नः अस्मान् महोभिः पूजाभिर्महद्भिर्धनैर्वा पाहि रक्ष। विश्वस्याः बहुविधात् अरातेः अदातुः सकाशात् अदानाद्वा पाहि। त्वमेव महद्धनं दत्त्वादातुरदानाद्वा सकाशाद्रक्षेत्यर्थः । यद्वा । महोभिर्युक्तस्त्वमिति योज्यम् । उत अपि च द्विषः द्वेष्टुः मर्त्यस्य मर्त्यात् सकाशात् पाहि । अस्मभ्यं बलं दत्त्वेति भावः । अथवा मर्त्यस्य द्विषो द्वेषाद्रक्षेति संबंधः। अरातेरित्यस्यादानादिति पक्षे तत्रापि मर्त्यस्यादानादिति संबंधनीयम् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, protect us by honours, or by great riches; protect us from every manifold niggard, that is, from the non-giver, or from non-giving. That is, protect us by giving great wealth, and from the non-giver or from non-giving. Or else the construction is: you, endowed with great splendours. And protect us from the hater, from the mortal who hates; that is, giving us strength. Or else the connection is: protect from the hatred of a mortal. In the alternative in which अरातेः (*arāteḥ*) means "from non-giving", the connection with the mortal's non-giving is to be made there also.

### Printed p. 64

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- महोभिः (*mahobhiḥ*)—by the giving of venerable wealth
- पाहि (*pāhi*)—protect
- विश्वस्याः (*viśvasyāḥ*)—of every kind
- अरातेः (*arāteḥ*)—protect also from the miser who is not a sacrificer
- उत (*uta*)—and
- द्विषः (*dviṣaḥ*)—who is an enemy
- मर्त्यस्य (*martyasya*)—protect also from the man

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, protect us by the giving of venerable wealth. Protect us also from every miser who is not a sacrificer, or who does not give. And protect us also from the man who is an enemy.

##### English translation
Agni, protect us by great wealth from every niggard and mortal foe.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न॒हि म॒न्युः पौरु॑षेय॒ ईशे॒ हि वः॑ प्रियजात ।  
> त्वमिद॑सि॒ क्षपा॑वान् ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नहि । मन्युः । पौरुषेयः । ईशे । हि । वः । प्रियऽजात ।  
> त्वम् । इत् । असि । क्षपाऽवान् ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे प्रियजात अग्ने वः तव पौरुषेयः पुरुषसंवंधी मन्युः क्रोधः न ईशे नेष्टे बाधितुम् । अस्मदादिभी रक्षितत्वादिति भावः । दिवाचराः खलु पुरुषाः अतो दिवा तव हानिर्नास्तीति भावः । अथ रात्रिंचरा रक्षःप्रभृतयः । तेभ्योऽपि पीडा नास्तीत्युच्यते । त्वमित् त्वमेव खलु क्षपावान् रात्रिमान् असि । रात्रौ ह्यग्निर्विशेषेण तेजस्वी भवति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, born loved, your anger that belongs to man cannot prevail to hinder you, because you are protected by us and others. Men are indeed day-goers, therefore by day there is no harm to you. Then there are those who go by night, the Rākṣasas and the rest; it is said that there is no affliction from them either. You alone are the lord of night, the possessor of night; for at night Agni is especially brilliant.

### Printed p. 65

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- प्रियजाते (*priyajāte*)—O Agni, whose birth causes delight
- पौरुषेयः (*pauruṣeyaḥ*)—that is manifested by a man **NOTE**
- मन्युः (*manyuḥ*)—anger
- वः (*vaḥ*)—you
- नहि ईशे हि (*nahi īśe hi*)—is not able to harm
- त्वमित् (*tvamit*)—you alone
- क्षपावान् (*kṣapāvān*)—as the lord of all the night
- असि (*asi*)—you are

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, whose birth causes delight, such anger, by whatever man it is manifested, is not able to harm you. You alone are the lord of all the night.

##### English translation
O you who were born loved, no human anger can harm you,—you only are the lord of night.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स नो॒ विश्वे॑भिर्दे॒वेभि॒रूर्जो॑ नपा॒द्भद्र॑शोचे ।  
> र॒यिं दे॑हि वि॒श्ववा॑रम् ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । नः । विश्वेभिः । देवेभिः । ऊर्जः । नपात् । भद्रऽशोचे ।  
> रयिम् । देहि । विश्वऽवारम् ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने सः स्तुत्यस्त्वं नः अस्मभ्यं वस्वो वसु धनमुप मासि प्रयच्छसि । हे ऊर्जो नपात् बलस्य नप्तर्न पातयितर्वा हे भद्रशोचे स्तुत्यप्रकाश । तदेवादरार्थं पुनराह। रयिं धनं विश्ववारं सर्वैर्वरणीयं धनं देहि । अथवा यद्वसूप मासि अस्मभ्यं दातुं तद्धनं गृहादिलक्षणं तच्च रयिं दानार्हं गोहिरण्यादिकं च देहीत्यपुनरुक्तिः ॥ ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, you, the praised, give to us wealth; **NOTE**. O son of strength, that is, grandson of strength or one who does not cause it to fall; O one of auspicious light, that is, of praiseworthy radiance. He says the same again for emphasis: give wealth, that is, wealth desirable by all. Or else: that wealth which he gives to us, of the nature of houses and the like, and also wealth fit to be given, cows, gold and the like, give; thus there is no repetition.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विश्वेभिः (*viśvebhiḥ*)—all
- देवेभिः (*devebhiḥ*)—who is accompanied by the gods

### Printed p. 66

- ऊर्जो (*ūrjo*) नपात् (*napāt*)—son of strength
- भद्रशोचे (*bhadraśoce*)—who has auspicious brilliance, O Agni
- सः (*saḥ*)—you, the famous one
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- विश्ववारं (*viśvavāraṃ*)—desired by all
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—wealth
- देहि (*dehi*)—give

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, who are accompanied by all the gods, son of strength, who have auspicious brilliance, you, the famous one, give us wealth desired by all.

##### English translation
Son of strength, auspicius in brilliance, associated with all the gods, give us all desirable wealth.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न तम॑ग्ने॒ अरा॑तयो॒ मर्तं॑ युवंत रा॒यः ।  
> यं त्राय॑से दा॒श्वांस॑म् ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> न । तम् । अग्ने । अरातयः । मर्तम् । युवंत । रायः ।  
> यम् । त्रायसे । दाश्वांसम् ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने तं स्तोतारम् अरातयः अदानशीला द्वेषिणः रायः रयिमंतः न युवंत न पृथक्कुर्वंति । रायो धनाद्वा न युवंत । यं दाश्वांसं हविर्दातारं त्रायसे पालयसे ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, the enemies who are not givers, the haters, the wealthy, do not separate that praiser from wealth, or do not part him from riches; him, the giver, the giver of oblations, whom you protect, whom you preserve.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- यं (*yaṃ*)—which
- दाश्वांसं (*dāśvāṃsaṃ*)—sacrificer who offers oblations
- त्रायसे (*trāyase*)—you protect
- तं मर्तं (*taṃ martaṃ*)—such a hymn **NOTE**
- अरातयः (*arātayaḥ*)—enemies
- रायः (*rāyaḥ*)—from his wealth
- न युवंत (*na yuvaṃta*)—cannot separate

### Printed p. 67

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, the enemies of that sacrificer who offers oblations, whom you protect, such a hymn, cannot seize his wealth and make him destitute.

##### English translation
That sacrificing mortal whom you, Agni, protect, the niggardly connot seperate from wealth.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यं त्वं वि॑प्र मे॒धसा॑ता॒वग्ने॑ हि॒नोषि॒ धना॑य ।  
> स तवो॒ती गोषु॒ गंता॑ ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यम् । त्वम् । विप्र । मेधऽसातौ । अग्ने । हिनोषि । धनाय ।  
> सः । तव । ऊती । गोषु । गंता ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने विप्र त्वं यं मर्यंन  मेधसातौ यज्ञस्य संभजने हिनोषि प्रेरयसि धनाय गवादिधनलाभाय सः यजमानः तव ऊती ऊत्या रक्षणेन गोषु गंता भवति । गोमान् भवतीत्यर्थः ॥ ॥ ११ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, wise one, the mortal whom you urge on, in the sharing of the sacrifice, for the gaining of wealth such as cows, that sacrificer, by your protection, becomes one who goes among the cows; the meaning is that he becomes possessed of cows. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विप्र (*vipra*)—wise
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- यं (*yaṃ*)—the sacrificer whom
- धनाय (*dhanāya*)—for the gaining of wealth
- मेधसातौ (*medhasātau*)—in the performance of the sacrifice
- हिनोषि (*hinoṣi*)—you incite
- सः (*saḥ*)—that same sacrificer
- तव (*tava*)—your
- ऊती (*ūtī*)—by the protection
- गोषु (*goṣu*)—as lord of all the cows
- गंता (*gaṃtā*)—moves among them

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O wise Agni, the sacrificer whom you incite in the performance of the sacrifice for the gaining of wealth, that same sacrificer, by your protection, as lord of all the cows, moves among them. (He obtains cattle-wealth in great abundance.)

### Printed p. 68

##### English translation
Wise (Agni), he whom in his performance of the sacrifice you incite to attain wealth, by your protection walks (lord) among crowds of cattle.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वं र॒यिं पु॑रु॒वीर॒मग्ने॑ दा॒शुषे॒ मर्ता॑य ।  
> प्र णो॑ नय॒ वस्यो॒ अच्छ॑ ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वम् । रयिम् । पुरुऽवीरम् । अग्ने । दाशुषे । मर्ताय ।  
> प्र । नः । नय । वस्यः । अच्छ ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने त्वं दाशुषे हविर्दत्तवते मर्त्याय यजमानाय रयिं धनं पुरुवीरं बहुभिर्वीरैर्युक्तं प्रयच्छसि । अतः नः अस्मानपि वस्यः वसीयो धनम् अच्छ अभिप्राप्तुं प्र णय प्रापय ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, you give to the mortal, the sacrificer who has given oblations, wealth endowed with many heroes. Therefore lead also us to better wealth, so that we may reach it.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- दाशुषे (*dāśuṣe*)—to the one who offers oblations
- मर्ताय (*martāya*)—to the man
- पुरुवीरं (*puruvīraṃ*)—endowed with many heroic sons
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—wealth (you give)
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us also
- वस्यः (*vasyaḥ*)—excellent wealth
- अच्छ (*accha*)—so that it may reach us
- प्र णय (*pra ṇaya*)—supply it in the best manner

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, you give to the man who offers oblations wealth endowed with many heroic sons. Make excellent wealth reach us also.

##### English translation
You, Agni, give to the offerer wealth comprehending many male descendants ; conduct us to affluence.

### Printed p. 69

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒रु॒ष्या णो॒ मा परा॑ दा अघाय॒ते जा॑तवेदः ।  
> दु॒रा॒ध्ये॒३॒॑ मर्ता॑य ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उरुष्य । नः । मा । परा । दाः । अघऽयते । जातऽवेदः ।  
> दुःऽआध्ये । मर्ताय ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे जातवेदः जातविद्य जातधन वाग्ने नः अस्मान् उरुष्य रक्ष। 'उरुष्यती रक्षाकर्मा' (निरु. ५. २३) इति यास्कः । मा परा दाः मा परादेह्यस्मान् । कस्मा इति स उच्यते । अघायते अघं पापमिच्छते दुराध्ये दुराध्यानाय दुर्विचिंतकाय हिंसाबुद्धये मर्ताय मनुष्याय ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Jātavedas, that is, one who knows what is born, or one whose wealth is born, O Agni, protect us. Yāska says: उरुष्यती रक्षाकर्मा (*uruṣyatī rakṣākarmā*) (Ni. 5. 23). Do not give us over. To whom? It is said: to the one who desires evil, that is, sin; to the evil-thinker, one of ill meditation, whose intent is to injure; to the mortal, the man.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- जातवेदः (*jātavedaḥ*)—O Agni, who knows all things
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- उरुष्य (*uruṣya*)—protect
- अघायते (*aghāyate*)—to the one who does us injury
- दुराध्ये (*durādhye*)—and who wishes us harm
- मर्ताय (*martāya*)—to the man
- मा परा दाः (*mā parā dāḥ*)—do not give up

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, who knows all things, protect us. Do not give us up to the man who does us injury and wishes us harm.

##### English translation
Defend us, Jatavedas ; deliver us not over to the malevolent, to the man whose thoughts are evil

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अग्ने॒ माकि॑ष्टे दे॒वस्य॑ रा॒तिमदे॑वो युयोत ।  
> त्वमी॑शिषे॒ वसू॑नाम् ॥ ८ ॥

### Printed p. 70

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्ने । माकिः । ते । देवस्य । रातिम् । अदेवः । युयोत ।  
> त्वम् । ईशिषे । वसूनाम् ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने देवस्य द्योतमानस्य ते तव रातिं दानं दत्तं धनं वा कश्चित् अदेवः मर्त्यादिः माकिः युयोत मैव पृथक्करोतु । त्वम् एव ईशिषे । कस्य । वसूनां धनानामीश्वरो भवसि दातुम् । अनेनामिश्रणीयाया रातेः सद्भाव उक्तो भवति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, shining one, let no godless one, a mortal or the like, take away your gift, the wealth given by you; let him not separate it. You alone rule. Over what? You are lord of the riches, to give them. By this the existence of a gift that is not to be mingled with others is declared.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- देवस्य (*devasya*)—who are shining
- ते (*te*)—given by you
- रातिं (*rātiṃ*)—the wealth
- अदेवः (*adevaḥ*)—who is without devotion to the gods
- माकिः (*mākiḥ*) युयोत (*yuyota*)—let no one take it away
- वसूनां (*vasūnāṃ*)—of all riches
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you alone
- ईशिषे (*īśiṣe*)—are the lord

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, let no one without devotion to the gods take away the wealth given by you who are shining. You alone are the lord of all riches.

##### English translation
Agni, let not the godless take away the wealth which you, the divine have given, for you are the lord of treasures.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स नो॒ वस्व॒ उप॑ मा॒स्यूर्जो॑ नपा॒न्माहि॑नस्य ।  
> सखे॑ वसो जरि॒तृभ्यः॑ ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । नः । वस्वः । उप । मासि । ऊर्जः । नपात् । माहिनस्य ।  
> सखे । वसो इति । जरितृऽभ्यः ॥ ९ ॥

### Printed p. 71

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे ऊर्जो नपात् बलस्यान्नस्य पुत्र । बलेन मध्यमानत्वादाज्यलक्षणेनान्नेन प्रवर्धनादूर्जोनपात्त्वम् । हे सखे सखिवद्धितकारिन् वसो वासकाग्ने सः स्तुत्यत्वेनैव प्रसिद्धस्त्वं जरितृभ्यः गरितृभ्यः स्तोतृभ्यः नः अस्मभ्यं माहिनस्य वस्वः । माहिन इति महन्नाम । महद्धनम् उप मासि समीपे मासि निर्मासि । प्रयच्छसीत्यर्थः ॥
> प्रातरनुवाके बार्हते छंदसि ‘अच्छा नः' इत्याद्याः षडृचः । तथा च सूत्रितम् -- अच्छा नः शीरशोचिषमिति षट् ' ( आश्व. श्रौ. ४.१३) इति । दशमेऽहनि प्रातःसवने अच्छा वो अग्निम् ' इति तृचस्य स्थाने ' अच्छा नः' इति तृचोऽच्छावाकवादः । सूत्रितं च --- अच्छा नः शीरशोचिषं प्रति श्रुताय वो धृषदिति तृचौ ' (आश्व. श्रौ. ८.१२) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O son of strength, that is, son of force or of food: because you are churned by force, and because you are made to grow by food in the form of ghee, you are ऊर्जो नपात् (*ūrjo napāt*). O friend, who do good like a friend, O Vasu, Agni who causes to dwell, you, famous precisely by being praised, to us, the praisers, give great wealth; माहिन (*māhina*) is a name for great. उप मासि (*upa māsi*) means that you measure out near; the meaning is that you give. In the Prātaranuvāka, in the Bārhata metre, the six Ṛks beginning अच्छा नः (*acchā naḥ*) are used. So it is laid down in the sūtra अच्छा नः शीरशोचिषमिति षट् (*acchā naḥ śīraśociṣamiti ṣaṭ*) (Āśv. Śrau. 4.13). On the tenth day, at the morning pressing, in the place of the trca beginning अच्छा वो अग्निम् (*acchā vo agnim*), the trca beginning अच्छा नः (*acchā naḥ*) is the Acchāvāka's. And it is laid down in the sūtra अच्छा नः शीरशोचिषं प्रति श्रुताय वो धृषदिति तृचौ (*acchā naḥ śīraśociṣaṃ prati śrutāya vo dhṛṣaditi tṛcau*) (Āśv. Śrau. 8.12).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऊर्जो नपात् (*ūrjo napāt*)—son of strength
- सखे (*sakhe*)—friend
- वसो (*vaso*)—giver of dwellings, O Agni
- सः (*saḥ*)—you, the famous one
- जरितृभ्यः (*jaritṛbhyaḥ*)—to us, who are praisers
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- माहिनस्य (*māhinasya*)—exceedingly great
- वस्वः (*vasvaḥ*)—wealth
- उप मासि (*upa māsi*)—you supply and give

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, son of strength, friend, giver of dwellings to us, you, the famous one, supply and give to us, the praisers, wealth of exceeding greatness.

##### English translation
Son of strength, the friend, the giver of dwellings, you mete out abundant treasure to us your praisers.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अच्छा॑ नः शी॒रशो॑चिषं॒ गिरो॑ यंतु दर्श॒तम् ।  
> अच्छा॑ य॒ज्ञासो॒ नम॑सा पुरू॒वसुं॑ पुरुप्रश॒स्तमू॒तये॑ ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अच्छ । नः । शीरऽशोचिषम् । गिरः । यंतु । दर्शतम् ।  
> अच्छ । यज्ञासः । नमसा । पुरुऽवसुम् । पुरुऽप्रशस्तम् । ऊतये ॥ १० ॥

### Printed p. 72

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अच्छ अभिमुखं यंतु गच्छंतु नः अस्माकं गिरः स्तुतयः । कम् । शीरशोचिषम् अशनशीलज्वालं दर्शतं सर्वैर्दर्शनीयमग्निम् । तथा यज्ञासः यज्ञाश्चास्मदीयाः नमसा हविषाज्यादिलक्षणेन अच्छ अभिमुखं यंतु गच्छंतु । कीदृशम् । पुरुवसुं प्रभूतधनं पुरुप्रशस्तं बहुस्तुतम् । किमर्थम् । ऊतये अस्माकं रक्षणाय ॥ ॥ १२ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Let our voices, our praises, go towards, go to Agni, whose flame devours, who is beautiful, to be seen by all. So also let our sacrifices go towards him with the oblation, of the nature of ghee and the like. What kind of Agni? Rich in wealth, that is, possessed of abundant wealth, and much praised. For what purpose? For our protection.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- गिरः (*giraḥ*)—praises
- शीरशोचिषं (*śīraśociṣaṃ*)—to Agni, who has flames that devour wood and the like
- दर्शतं (*darśataṃ*)—and has an attractive form
- अच्छ (*accha*)—towards him
- यंतु (*yaṃtu*)—let them go and reach
- यज्ञासः (*yajñāsaḥ*)—our sacrifices also
- नमसा (*namasā*)—in the form of our oblation
- पुरुवसुं (*puruvasuṃ*)—who has exceedingly abundant wealth
- पुरुप्रशस्तं (*purupraśastaṃ*)—and is abundantly **NOTE** praised, Agni
- ऊतये (*ūtaye*)—for our protection
- अच्छ (*accha*)—let them go and reach towards him

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Let our praises go and reach towards Agni, who has flames that devour wood and the like, and has an attractive form. Let our sacrifices also, in the form of our oblation, go and reach towards Agni, who has exceedingly abundant wealth and is abundantly praised, for our protection.

##### English translation
Let our voices come near the beautiful (Agni), him who bears devouring flames ; let our sacrifices with our oblations come near him, for our protection, who is rich·in wealth and rich in praise.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

At the time of reciting the Prātaranuvāka mantras, the six Ṛks beginning अच्छा नः (*acchā naḥ*) are employed for the Ṛks in the Bṛhatī metre, as is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrautasūtra अच्छा नः शीरशोचिषमिति षट् (*acchā naḥ śīraśociṣamiti ṣaṭ*) (8. 4-13) **NOTE**. On the tenth day of the sacrifice

### Printed p. 73

at the time of the morning pressing, in place of the trca beginning अच्छा वो अग्निं (*acchā vo agniṃ*), the trca beginning अच्छा नः (*acchā naḥ*) is to be recited by the priest called Acchāvāka, as is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrautasūtra अच्छा नः शीरशोचिषं प्रति प्र तायु वो धृषदिति तृचौ (*acchā naḥ śīraśociṣaṃ prati pra tāyu vo dhṛṣaditi tṛcau*) (Ā. 8-12) **NOTE**.

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निं सू॒नुं सह॑सो जा॒तवे॑दसं दा॒नाय॒ वार्या॑णाम् ।  
> द्वि॒ता यो भूद॒मृतो॒ मर्त्ये॒ष्वा होता॑ मं॒द्रत॑मो वि॒शि ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निम् । सूनुम् । सहसः । जातऽवेदसम् । दानाय । वार्याणाम् ।  
> द्विता । यः । भूत् । अमृतः । मर्त्येषु । आ । होता । मंद्रऽतमः । विशि ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अग्निं सहसः सूनुं बलस्य पुत्रं जातवेदसं जातधनं वार्याणां वरणीयानां गवादिधनानां दानाय गिरः अच्छ यंत्वित्यनुवर्तते । यः अग्निः अमृतः अमरणधर्मा देवेषु भवति सः मर्त्येष्वा । आकारश्चार्थे । मनुष्येषु चाभूत् अभवदित्येवं द्विता द्वैधं भवति । देवेष्वमृतत्वमस्य प्रसिद्धं मनुष्येषु कीदृशोऽभूदिति उच्यते । विशि विक्षु यजमानरूपासु प्रजासु होता होमनिष्पादकः मंद्रतमः मादयितृतमश्च भवति । अच्छ यन्विति समन्वयः । अथवा योऽमृतो द्विता द्वित्वं द्वैधं द्विप्रकारोऽभूत् । कथम् । मर्त्येषु सामान्येन दाहपाकादिसाधनोऽभवदित्येतत् प्रसिद्धं विशि यजमानरूपायां तु होता मंद्रतमः अभवदित्येवं द्वित्वम् ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Agni, the son of strength, Jātavedas, that is, one whose wealth is born, for the giving of desirable wealth such as cows: the words "let our voices go towards" continue from the preceding Ṛk. The Agni who is immortal, of an undying nature, among the gods, and who among mortals also became so, in this way becomes twofold. His immortality among the gods is well known; it is now said what kind of being he became among men. Among the people, the sacrificers, he is the Hotṛ, the accomplisher of the oblation, and the most exhilarating. The construction is: let them go towards. Or else: the immortal one became twofold, of two kinds. How? Among mortals in general it is well known that he became the means of burning, cooking and the like; but among the people who are sacrificers he became the Hotṛ, the most exhilarating. Thus the twofoldness.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मंद्रतमः (*maṃdratamaḥ*)—who gives delight
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Agni
- विशि (*viśi*)—among men who are sacrificers
- होता (*hotā*)—as the accomplisher of the oblation
- मर्त्येषु आ (*martyeṣu ā*)—among the other men

### Printed p. 74

as the means of cooking;
- द्विता (*dvitā*)—of two kinds
- अमृतः (*amṛtaḥ*)—as one possessed of the nature of mortality, or **NOTE**
- भूत् (*bhūt*)—who is immortal among the gods and a performer of sacrifice and the like among men, such
- सहसः सूनुं (*sahasaḥ sūnuṃ*)—the son of strength
- जातवेदसं (*jātavedasaṃ*)—who knows all things
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- वार्याणां (*vāryāṇāṃ*)—of desired riches
- दानाय (*dānāya*)—for the giving (may our praises go and reach him)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May our praises go and reach, for the giving and attaining of desired riches, Agni, son of strength, who knows all things, who gives delight, who is in two ways, as the accomplisher of the oblation among men who are sacrificers and as the means of cooking among the other men, and who shines as the immortal among the gods.

##### English translation
(Let them come near) Agni, Jatavedas, son of strength, for the giving of all desirable good things ; who is doubly immortal as (perpetually burning) amongst mortals, and as the supremely exhilarating ministrant-priest amongst the sacrificers.

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निं वो॑ देवय॒ज्यया॒ग्निं प्र॑य॒त्य॑ध्व॒रे ।  
> अ॒ग्निं धी॒षु प्र॑थ॒मम॒ग्निमर्व॑त्य॒ग्निं क्षैत्रा॑य॒ साध॑से ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निम् । वः । देवऽयज्यया । अग्निम् । प्रऽयति । अध्वरे ।  
> अग्निम् । धीषु । प्रथमम् । अग्निम् । अर्वति । अग्निम् । क्षैत्राय । साधसे ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे यजमानाः युष्माकं देवयज्यया देवयागेन निमित्तेन देवयागार्थम् अग्निं स्तौमीति शेषः । तथा अग्निम् अध्वरे यागे प्रयति प्रकर्षेण गच्छति प्रवृत्ते सति स्तौमि प्रथमम् इतरदेवेभ्यः । तथा अग्निम् अर्वति आगते भ्रातृव्ये स्तौमि यज्ञविघ्नपरिहारार्थम् । तथा क्षैत्राय क्षेत्राय क्षेत्रसंबंधिने साधसे साधनाय क्षेत्रलाभाय स्तौमि । यज्ञांते क्षेत्रलाभरूपाय फलाय च स्तौमि । एवमादौ मध्येऽंते च सर्वदा स्तौमीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O sacrificers, by your divine sacrifice, that is, for the sake of the sacrifice to the gods, I praise Agni; so the sentence is completed. So also, when the sacrifice proceeds, that is, has set out and is in progress, I praise Agni, first before the other gods. So also, when the rival has come, I praise Agni, for the removal of the obstacles of the sacrifice. So also, for what belongs to the field, for the accomplishing, that is, for the gaining of a field, I praise him; and at the end of the sacrifice I praise him for the fruit in the form of the gaining of a field. The meaning is that I praise him always, at the beginning, in the middle and at the end.

### Printed p. 75

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O sacrificers) वः (*vaḥ*)—your
- देवयज्यया (*devayajyayā*)—for the sacrifice directed to the gods
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—I praise Agni
- अध्वरे (*adhvare*)—when the sacrifice
- प्रयति (*prayati*)—is being performed
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—I praise Agni
- धीषु (*dhīṣu*)—among the wise gods
- प्रथमं अग्निं (*prathamaṃ agniṃ*)—I praise Agni, the foremost
- अर्वति (*arvati*)—at the time of the enemy's approach also
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—I praise Agni
- क्षैत्राय (*kṣaitrāya*)—for what is related to the field
- साधसे (*sādhase*)—for the accomplishing of gain
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—I praise Agni

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O sacrificers, at the beginning of your sacrifice directed to the gods, I praise Agni. While the sacrifice is being performed, I praise Agni. I praise Agni, the foremost among the wise gods. At the time of the enemy's approach also, I praise Agni. For the accomplishing of gain related to the field, I praise Agni.

##### English translation
I praise Agni, (O sacrificers), for the inauguration of your divine offering; (I praise him) when the sacrifice is proceeding: (I praise) Agni first of the gods, at our rites; (I praise) Agni when the enemy approaches ; (I praise)Agni for the attainment of land.

#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निरि॒षां स॒ख्ये द॑दातु न॒ ईशे॒ यो वार्या॑णाम् ।  
> अ॒ग्निं तो॒के तन॑ये॒ शश्व॑दीमहे॒ वसुं॒ संतं॑ तनू॒पाम् ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निः । इषाम् । सख्ये । ददातु । नः । ईशे । यः । वार्याणाम् ।  
> अग्निम् । तोके । तनये । शश्वत् । ईमहे । वसुम् । संतम् । तनूऽपाम् ॥ १३ ॥

### Printed p. 76

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)
