Translation of the Kannada: This sūkta beginning विशोविशो वः (*viśoviśo vaḥ*) is the fifth sūkta in the eighth Maṇḍala. It has fifteen Ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: विशोविशो वः पंचोनाग्नेयं त्वनुष्टुबुम्मखास्त्र्यचाश्चत्वारोऽन्त्यास्तिस्रोऽनुष्टुभ आर्क्षस्य श्रुतर्वणो दानस्तुतिः (*viśoviśo vaḥ paṃconāgneyaṃ tvanuṣṭubummakhāstryacāścatvāro 'ntyāstisro 'nuṣṭubha ārkṣasya śrutarvaṇo dānastutiḥ*). The seer of this sūkta is the one named Gopavana. Of the first three tṛcas of this sūkta (a tṛca is three Ṛks) the first Ṛk is Anuṣṭubh, and the second and third Ṛks are in the Gāyatrī metre. The three Ṛks from the thirteenth onwards are in the Anuṣṭubh metre. Agni is the deity of this sūkta and of the next sūkta. For the last three Ṛks of this sūkta the praise of the gift of the king named Śrutarvā is the deity. It is to be used for the Ājya-śastra mantras in the Vyoma, Viśvadeva and Pañcaśāradīya rites, as it is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta-sūtra विशोविशो वोः अथितिमिथ्याज्यं (*viśoviśo voḥ athitimithyājyaṃ*) (Ā. 9-8). **NOTE**

Sūkta 74. Maṇḍala 8, Anuvāka 8, Sūkta 74. Aṣṭaka 6, Adhyāya 5, Vargas 21, 22, 23. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 15. Seer: Gopavana Ātreya. Deity: 1-12 Agni; 13-15 the praise of the gift of Śrutarvan Ārkṣa. Metre: 1, 4, 7, 10, 13-15 Anuṣṭubh; 2, 3, 5, 6, 8, 9, 11, 12 Gāyatrī.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वि॒शोवि॑शो वो॒ अति॑थिं वाज॒यंतः॑ पुरुप्रि॒यम् ।  
> अ॒ग्निं वो॒ दुर्यं॒ वच॑ स्तु॒षे शू॒षस्य॒ मन्म॑भिः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> विशःऽविशः । वः । अतिथिम् । वाजऽयंतः । पुरुऽप्रियम् ।  
> अग्निम् । वः । दुर्यम् । वचः । स्तुषे । शूषस्य । मन्मऽभिः ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 116

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अन्य ऋत्विजो यजमानाश्च वः यूयं वाजयंतः अन्नमिच्छंतः विशोविशः सर्वस्याः प्रजायाः अतिथिं पूज्यं पुरुप्रियं बहुप्रियम् अग्निं स्तुत्या परिचरतेति शेषः । अहं च वः युष्मदर्थं दुर्यं गुहा हितं वचः स्तुषे शूषस्य सुखस्य लाभाय । कैः साधनैः । मन्मभिः मननीयैः स्तोत्रैः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

He addresses the other priests and sacrificers: you, desiring food, serve with praise Agni, the guest, the one to be honoured, of every people, beloved of many. And I, for you, praise the homely speech, for the gain of happiness, with what means: with hymns worthy of reflection.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वः (*vaḥ*)—you (O priests)
- वाजयंतः (*vājayaṃtaḥ*)—desirous of food
- विशोविशः (*viśoviśaḥ*)—for every human people
- अतिथिं (*atithiṃ*)—the one to be honoured
- पुरुप्रियं (*purupriyaṃ*)—the one dear to many
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—serve Agni
- वः (*vaḥ*)—for you
- शूषस्य (*śūṣasya*)—for happiness
- मन्मभिः (*manmabhiḥ*)—with hymns of meditative nature
- दुर्यं (*duryaṃ*)—relating to the house
- वचः (*vacaḥ*)—the speech of praise
- स्तुषे (*stuṣe*)—I recite

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O priests, you, desiring food, serve Agni, who is to be honoured by every human people and is dear to many. For your happiness, with hymns of meditative nature, I recite the speech of praise relating to the house.

##### English translation
Food-desiring (priests, worship) Agni, who is the guest of all man kind, beloved of many ; I address to him in your behalf a domestic homage with hymns, for the attainment of happiness ;

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यं जना॑सो ह॒विष्मं॑तो मि॒त्रं न स॒र्पिरा॑सुतिम् ।  
> प्र॒शंसं॑ति॒ प्रश॑स्तिभिः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यम् । जनासः । हविष्मंतः । मित्रम् । न । सर्पिःऽआसुतिम् ।  
> प्रऽशंसंति । प्रशस्तिऽभिः ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 117

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यम् अग्निं जनासः जना यजमानाः हविष्मंतः संतः मित्रं न मित्रमिवादित्यमिव सखायमिव वा सर्पिरासुतिं सर्पिरासूयते हूयते यस्मिंस्तादृशं प्रशंसंति स्तुवंति प्रशस्तिभिः स्तुतिभिः स्तुव इति शेषः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Agni whom the people, the sacrificers, having oblations, praise with praises, like a friend, that is, like Mitra (the sun) or like a companion, in whom clarified butter is poured and offered; I praise him, the verb being understood.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मित्रं न (*mitraṃ na*)—as one serves a friend
- हविष्मंतः (*haviṣmaṃtaḥ*)—possessing oblations
- जनासः (*janāsaḥ*)—the sacrificers
- सर्पिरासुतिं (*sarpirāsutiṃ*)—having oblations offered with praises
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Agni
- प्रशस्तिभिः (*praśastibhiḥ*)—with words of praise
- प्रशंसंति (*praśaṃsaṃti*)—they praise (that Agni I praise)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The sacrificers, possessing oblations, praise and honour with words of praise, as they serve their friends, that Agni who has the oblations offered with praises; that Agni I too praise.

##### English translation
(That Agni), to whom clarified butter is offered, whom men, bearing oblations, worship with praises as a friend ;

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पन्यां॑सं जा॒तवे॑दसं॒ यो दे॒वता॒त्युद्य॑ता ।  
> ह॒व्यान्यैर॑यद्दि॒वि ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> पन्यांसम् । जातऽवेदसम् । यः । देवऽताति । उत्ऽयता ।  
> हव्यानि । ऐरयत् । दिवि ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पन्यांसम् अतिशयेन स्तोतारं साधु कृतमिति यजमानं स्तुवंतं जातवेदसं जातधनं जातविद्यं वा स्तुव इति शेषः । यः अग्निः देवताति देवतातौ यज्ञे उद्यता उद्यतानि हव्यानि हवींषि दिवि ऐरयत् प्रेरयति दिवि देवेभ्यः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

पन्यांसं (*panyāṃsaṃ*) is the greatest praiser, one who praises the sacrificer, saying "well done"; जातवेदसं (*jātavedasaṃ*) is one in whom wealth is born, or one by whom knowledge is born; I praise him, the verb being understood. The Agni who, in the sacrifice (देवताति (*devatāti*)), sends the oblations that are offered up to heaven, for the gods.

### Printed p. 118

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Agni
- देवताति (*devatāti*)—in the sacrifice addressed to the gods
- उद्यता (*udyatā*)—offered
- हव्यानि (*havyāni*)—the oblations
- दिवि (*divi*)—to the world of the gods
- ऐरयत् (*airayat*)—sends and dispatches, such a one
- पन्यांसं (*panyāṃsaṃ*)—one who praises the sacrificer who worships him
- जातवेदसं (*jātavedasaṃ*)—and the one who knows everything, that Agni I praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The Agni who carries to the world of the gods the oblations offered in the sacrifice addressed to the gods, who praises the sacrificer who worships him, and who knows everything, that Agni I praise.

##### English translation
Jatavedas, the earnest praiser of his worshipper, who sends to heaven the oblations presented in the sacrifice.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आग॑न्म वृत्र॒हंत॑मं॒ ज्येष्ठ॑म॒ग्निमान॑वम् ।  
> यस्य॑ श्रु॒तर्वा॑ बृ॒हन्ना॒र्क्षो अनी॑क॒ एध॑ते ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । अगन्म । वृत्रहन्ऽतमम् । ज्येष्ठम् । अग्निम् । आनवम् ।  
> यस्य । श्रुतर्वा । बृहन् । आर्क्षः । अनीके । एधते ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वृत्रहंतमं पापानामतिशयेन हंतारं ज्येष्ठं प्रशस्यम् आनवं मनुष्यसंबंधिनं तेषां हितकारिणम् अग्निम् आगन्म आगता वयम् । पूजार्थं बहुवचनम् । यस्य अग्नेः अनीके ज्वालासंघे बृहन् महान् आर्क्षः ऋक्षपुत्रः श्रुतर्वा नाम राजा एधते वर्धते । कर्म करोतीत्यर्थः । तमग्निमागन्मेति समन्वयः । एवं श्रुतर्वाणं भिक्षयागतो गोपवनोऽग्निं स्तौति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

We have come to Agni, the greatest destroyer of the sins, the most excellent, the one belonging to men (आनवं (*ānavaṃ*)), their benefactor; the plural is for honour. In the flame-host of which Agni the mighty Ārkṣa, son of Ṛkṣa, the king named Śrutarvā, thrives; that is, he performs the rite. So the connection is: we have come to that Agni. Thus Gopavana, who has come to Śrutarvā for alms, praises Agni.

### Printed p. 119

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यस्य (*yasya*)—of which Agni
- अनीके (*anīke*)—with the help of the army in the form of flames
- बृहन् (*bṛhan*)—endowed with greatness
- आर्क्षः (*ārkṣaḥ*)—the son of Ṛkṣa
- श्रुतर्वा (*śrutarvā*)—the king named Śrutarvan
- एधते (*edhate*)—thrives, such a one
- वृत्रहंतमं (*vṛtrahaṃtamaṃ*)—the destroyer of sinners
- ज्येष्ठं (*jyeṣṭhaṃ*)—the most worthy of praise
- आनवं (*ānavaṃ*)—the benefactor of men
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—to Agni
- आ अगन्म (*ā aganma*)—we have come

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We have come near to Agni, the destroyer of sinners, the most worthy of praise, the benefactor of men, with the help of whose army in the form of flames the king, endowed with greatness, the son of Ṛkṣa, named Śrutarvan, thrives.

##### English translation
We have come to that most excellent Agni, mightiest destroyer of the wicked, the benefactor of men, in whose army (of rays) Srutarvan the mighty son of Riksha, waxes great.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒मृतं॑ जा॒तवे॑दसं ति॒रस्तमां॑सि दर्श॒तम् ।  
> घृ॒ताह॑वन॒मीड्य॑म् ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अमृतम् । जातऽवेदसम् । तिरः । तमांसि । दर्शतम् ।  
> घृतऽआहवनम् । ईड्यम् ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> स एवागत्य स्तौति । अमृतम् अमरणं जातवेदसं जाततेजआद्युपलक्षणधनं तमांसि तिरः दर्शतं नाशयंतमित्यर्थः। घृताहवनम् । घृतमाहूयते यत्र तम् । ईड्यं स्तुत्यम्। ईदृशमागन्मेति संबंधः ॥ ॥ २१ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The same, having come, praises: अमृतम् (*amṛtam*) is the one who does not die; जातवेदसं (*jātavedasaṃ*) is the one in whom wealth, marked by born brilliance and the like, is born; तमांसि तिरः दर्शतं (*tamāṃsi tiraḥ darśataṃ*) means the one who destroys the darkness. घृताहवनम् (*ghṛtāhavanam*) is the one in whom ghee is poured; ईड्यं (*īḍyaṃ*) is the one worthy of praise. The connection is: we have come to such a one.

### Printed p. 120

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अमृतं (*amṛtaṃ*)—the one free from the nature of death
- जातवेदसं (*jātavedasaṃ*)—the one who knows everything
- तमांसि (*tamāṃsi*)—the black darknesses
- तिरः दर्शतं (*tiraḥ darśataṃ*)—the one who destroys
- घृताहवनं (*ghṛtāhavanaṃ*)—the one to whom the oblation in the form of ghee is offered
- ईड्यं (*īḍyaṃ*)—the one to be honoured; we have approached such an Agni

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We have approached Agni, who is free from the nature of death, who knows everything, who destroys the black darknesses, who is honoured with the oblation in the form of ghee, and who is worthy of praise.

##### English translation
(We have come) to the mortal Jatavedas, who shows light across the darkness, well worthy of praise, and  receiving the offerings of ghi ;

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स॒बाधो॒ यं जना॑ इ॒मे॒३॒॑ऽग्निं ह॒व्येभि॒रीळ॑ते ।  
> जुह्वा॑नासो य॒तस्रु॑चः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सऽबाधः । यम् । जनाः । इमे । अग्निम् । हव्येभिः । ईळते ।  
> जुह्वानासः । यतऽस्रुचः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> इमे सबाधः बाधसहिता अध्वर्य्वादयः यम् अग्निं हव्येभिः हविर्भिः ईळते स्तुवंति । कीदृशा जनाः। जुह्वानासः यागं कुर्वाणाः यतस्रुचः तदर्थं धृतस्रुग्दंडाः । तमागन्मेति समन्वयः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

These Adhvaryus and others, सबाधः (*sabādhaḥ*), together with obstruction, praise with oblations the Agni whom they praise. What kind of people: जुह्वानासः (*juhvānāsaḥ*), performing the sacrifice; यतस्रुचः (*yatasrucaḥ*), holding the ladle-handles for it. The connection is: we have come to him. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- जुह्वानासः (*juhvānāsaḥ*)—those who perform the sacrifice
- यतस्रुचः (*yatasrucaḥ*)—those who hold up the ladle full of ghee
- सबाधः (*sabādhaḥ*)—those who are gathered together
- इमे जनाः (*ime janāḥ*)—these Adhvaryus
- यं अग्निं (*yaṃ agniṃ*)—which Agni
- हव्येभिः (*havyebhiḥ*)—with oblations
- ईळते (*īḷate*)—they praise (we have approached that Agni)

### Printed p. 121

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We have approached that Agni whom these Adhvaryus, who perform the sacrifice, who hold up the ladle full of ghee and who are gathered together, praise with oblations.

##### English translation
That Agni, whom these crowding worshippers honour with oblations offering to him with up-lifted ladles.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इ॒यं ते॒ नव्य॑सी म॒तिरग्ने॒ अधा॑य्य॒स्मदा ।  
> मंद्र॒ सुजा॑त॒ सुक्र॒तोऽमू॑र॒ दस्माति॑थे ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इयम् । ते । नव्यसी । मतिः । अग्ने । अधायि । अस्मत् । आ ।  
> मंद्र । सुऽजात । सुक्रतो इति सुऽक्रतो । अमूर । दस्म । अतिथे ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने इयम् इदानीं क्रियमाणा नव्यसी नवतरा स्तुतिः ते तव स्वभूता अस्मत् अस्मासु अधायि धृताभूत् । वयं तव स्तुतिं कुर्म इत्यर्थः । हे मंद्र मोदमान सुजात शोभनजनन सुक्रतो शोभनकर्मन् अमूर अमूढ दस्म दर्शनीय अतिथे अतिथिवत् पूज्य इत्यग्नेर्विशेषणानि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, this newest praise, now being made, belonging to you, has been set among us (अधायि (*adhāyi*)); the meaning is that we make praise of you. The words मंद्र (*maṃdra*) (rejoicing), सुजात (*sujāta*) (of good birth), सुक्रतो (*sukrato*) (of good works), अमूर (*amūra*) (not bewildered), दस्म (*dasma*) (beautiful to behold) and अतिथे (*atithe*) (to be honoured like a guest) are epithets of Agni.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मंद्र (*maṃdra*)—one who gives joy
- सुजात (*sujāta*)—one of excellent birth
- सुक्रतो (*sukrato*)—one of excellent works
- अमूर (*amūra*)—the wise one
- दस्म (*dasma*)—one of attractive form
- अतिथे (*atithe*)—the one to be honoured
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- ते (*te*)—addressed to you
- नव्यसी (*navyasī*)—new
- इयं मतिः (*iyaṃ matiḥ*)—this praise
- अस्मत् (*asmat*)—by us
- आ अधायि (*ā adhāyi*)—has been set for your praise

### Printed p. 122

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, who give joy, are of excellent birth, are the doer of excellent works, are wise, of attractive form, and to be honoured, this new praise addressed to you has been set by us for your praise (has been made).

##### English translation
This new hymn has been made by us for you, O joyful, well-born Agni, glorious in deeds, unbewildered, beautiful, the guest (of man);

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सा ते॑ अग्ने॒ शंत॑मा॒ चनि॑ष्ठा भवतु प्रि॒या ।  
> तया॑ वर्धस्व॒ सुष्टु॑तः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सा । ते । अग्ने । शम्ऽतमा । चनिष्ठा । भवतु । प्रिया ।  
> तया । वर्धस्व । सुऽस्तुतः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने सा अस्माभिः क्रियमाणा स्तुतिः शंतमा अत्यंतं सुखकरा चनिष्ठा अतिशयेनान्नवती ते तव प्रिया भवतु । तया स्तुत्या सुष्टुतः सुष्ठु स्तुतः सन् वर्धस्व प्रवृद्धो भव ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, that praise made by us, most pleasant (शंतमा (*śaṃtamā*)) and most rich in food (चनिष्ठा (*caniṣṭhā*)), let it be dear to you. Being well praised by that praise, grow, become greatly increased.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- सा (*sā*)—the praise made by us
- शंतमा (*śaṃtamā*)—most productive of happiness
- चनिष्ठा (*caniṣṭhā*)—endowed with abundant food
- ते (*te*)—to you
- प्रिया (*priyā*)—productive of what is dear
- भवतु (*bhavatu*)—let it be
- तया (*tayā*)—by that same praise
- सुष्टुतः (*suṣṭutaḥ*)—you, well praised
- वर्धस्व (*vardhasva*)—attain increase

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, let the praise made by us be most productive of happiness, endowed with abundant food and productive of what is dear to you. Well praised by that same praise, attain increase.

### Printed p. 123

##### English translation
Agni, may it be dear to you, most pleasant and most agreeable—well praised by it, do you wax great.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सा द्यु॒म्नैर्द्यु॒म्निनी॑ बृ॒हदुपो॑प॒ श्रव॑सि॒ श्रवः॑ ।  
> दधी॑त वृत्र॒तूर्ये॑ ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सा । द्युम्नैः । द्युम्निनी । बृहत् । उपऽउप । श्रवसि । श्रवः ।  
> दधीत । वृत्रऽतूर्ये ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सा अस्माभिः क्रियमाणा स्तुतिः द्युम्नैः द्योतमानैरन्नैः अस्मभ्यं प्रदेयैः द्युम्निनी अन्नवती श्रवसि पूर्वस्मिन् विद्यमानेऽन्ने पुनरपि बृहत् महत् श्रवः अन्नम् उपोप दधीत पुनरुपरि धारयतु । कुत्रेति उच्यते । वृत्रतूर्ये संग्रामे । शत्रोः संबंधीति यावत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

That praise made by us, with shining foods (द्युम्नैः (*dyumnaiḥ*)) to be given to us, is rich in food (द्युम्निनी (*dyumninī*)); to the food (श्रवसि (*śravasi*)) that already exists, again may it add great food (श्रवः (*śravaḥ*)), and again and again place more. Where? It is said: in the battle (वृत्रतूर्ये (*vṛtratūrye*)), that is, the one connected with the enemy. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सा (*sā*)—this praise made and recited by us
- द्युम्नैः (*dyumnaiḥ*)—with splendid food-wealths
- द्युम्निनी (*dyumninī*)—the one that joins new food
- श्रवसि (*śravasi*)—with our food that is behind
- वृत्रतूर्ये (*vṛtratūrye*)—in battle, seized from enemies
- बृहत् (*bṛhat*)—abundant
- श्रवः (*śravaḥ*)—new food
- उपोप (*upopa*)—in great measure
- दधीत (*dadhīta*)—let it join and increase

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May this praise made and recited by us, which joins new food with splendid food-wealths, add abundantly and increase the new food, abundant and seized from enemies in battle, together with our food that is behind.

### Printed p. 124

##### English translation
May this (hymn) the rich source of wealth, heap  abundance o nour abundance (with stores won from our enemies) in battle.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अश्व॒मिद्गां र॑थ॒प्रां त्वे॒षमिंद्रं॒ न सत्प॑तिम् ।  
> यस्य॒ श्रवां॑सि॒ तूर्व॑थ॒ पन्यं॑पन्यं च कृ॒ष्टयः॑ ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अश्वम् । इत् । गाम् । रथऽप्राम् । त्वेषम् । इंद्रम् । न । सत्ऽपतिम् ।  
> यस्य । श्रवांसि । तूर्वथ । पन्यम्ऽपन्यम् । च । कृष्टयः ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> गां गंतारम् अश्वमित् । इच्छब्द इवार्थे। अश्वमिव । तं यथा स्तुवते तथेत्यर्थः। रथप्रां रथानामस्मदीयानां पूरयितारं धनैः तथा त्वेषं दीप्तमग्निं सत्पतिं सतां पालकम् इंद्रं न इंद्रमिवेमं कृष्टयः मनुष्याः परिचरतेति शेषः । यस्य अग्नेर्बलेन श्रवांसि अन्नानि शत्रुसंबंधीनि तूर्वथ तथा पन्यंपन्यं च यद्यत् स्तुत्यं धनमस्ति तदपि तूर्वथ हिंस्थ ॥ ॥ २२ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

गां (*gāṃ*) means the one who goes; अश्वमित् (*aśvamit*): the word इत् (*it*) is in the sense of "like", that is, like a horse; as men praise him, so. रथप्रां (*rathaprāṃ*) is the one who fills our chariots with riches; likewise the shining (त्वेषं (*tveṣaṃ*)) Agni, the protector of the good (सत्पतिं (*satpatiṃ*)), like Indra: men (कृष्टयः (*kṛṣṭayaḥ*)), serve this one, the verb being understood. By whose strength you overcome (तूर्वथ (*tūrvatha*)) the foods (श्रवांसि (*śravāṃsi*)) belonging to the enemy, and likewise whatever praiseworthy wealth (पन्यंपन्यं (*panyaṃpanyaṃ*)) there is, that too you destroy.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- कृष्टयः (*kṛṣṭayaḥ*)—O men
- यस्य (*yasya*)—by the power of which Agni
- श्रवांसि (*śravāṃsi*)—the foods belonging to the enemy
- तूर्वथ (*tūrvatha*)—you seize and fill yourselves
- पन्यं पन्यं च (*panyaṃ panyaṃ ca*)—and the valuable riches too you seize and fill yourselves, such a one
- अश्वमित् (*aśvamit*)—like a horse
- गां (*gāṃ*)—the one who moves about
- रथप्रां (*rathaprāṃ*)—the one who fills our chariots with enemy wealth
- त्वेषं (*tveṣaṃ*)—the radiant one
- सत्पतिं (*satpatiṃ*)—the protector of good men
- इंद्रं न (*iṃdraṃ na*)—the one honoured like Indra, serve that Agni

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O men, serve that Agni, by whose power you seize and fill yourselves with the foods belonging to the enemy, and seize and fill yourselves with their valuable riches too, who moves about like a horse,

### Printed p. 125

who fills our chariots with the enemy's wealth, who is radiant, the protector of good men, and honoured like Indra.

##### English translation
(Worship), you men, the bright (Agni), who goes like a horse and fills our chariots (with spoil), who protects the good like Indra, and by whose might you ravage the stores ( of your enemies ) and all their wonderful (wealth).

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यं त्वा॑ गो॒पव॑नो गि॒रा चनि॑ष्ठदग्ने अंगिरः ।  
> स पा॑वक श्रुधी॒ हव॑म् ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यम् । त्वा । गोपवनः । गिरा । चनिष्ठत् । अग्ने । अंगिरः ।  
> सः । पावक । श्रुधि । हवम् ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने यं त्वा त्वां गोपवनः ऋषिः गिरा स्तुत्या चनिष्ठत् अतिशयेनान्नप्रदातारमकरोत् सः तादृशाग्ने अंगिरः सर्वत्र गंतरंगिरसां मध्य एक वा पावक शोधक हवं गोपवनस्य श्रुधि शृणु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, you whom the seer Gopavana by his praise has made the greatest giver of food, such a one, O Agni, अंगिरः (*aṃgiraḥ*), O purifier (पावक (*pāvaka*)), hear the call (हवं (*havaṃ*)) of Gopavana. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अंगिरः (*aṃgiraḥ*)—the one famed by the name Aṅgiras
- पावक (*pāvaka*)—the purifier
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- यं (*yaṃ*)—which
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- गोपवनः (*gopavanaḥ*)—the seer named Gopavana
- गिरा (*girā*)—by his praise
- चनिष्ठत् (*caniṣṭhat*)—made the excellent giver of food
- सः (*saḥ*)—such you
- हवं (*havaṃ*)—the praise of that Gopavana
- श्रुधि (*śrudhi*)—hear

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, famed by the name Aṅgiras and the purifier, you whom the seer named Gopavana has made by his praise the excellent giver of food, such as you, hear the praise of that Gopavana.

### Printed p. 126

##### English translation
Agni, Angiras, whom Gopavana by his praise has made the especial giver of food,—O purifier, hear his prayer.

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यं त्वा॒ जना॑स॒ ईळ॑ते स॒बाधो॒ वाज॑सातये ।  
> स बो॑धि वृत्र॒तूर्ये॑ ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यम् । त्वा । जनासः । ईळते । सऽबाधः । वाजऽसातये ।  
> सः । बोधि । वृत्रऽतूर्ये ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने यं त्वा त्वां जनासः जनाः स्तोतारो वा वाजसातये अन्नस्य लाभाय सबाधः निर्बंधरूपबाधोपेताः संतः ईळते स्तुवंति सः त्वं वृत्रतूर्ये वैरिनाशनाय पापक्षयाय वा बोधि बुध्यस्व । अथवा वृत्रतूर्ये संग्रामे बोधि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, you whom the people, or the praisers, for the gain of food (वाजसातये (*vājasātaye*)), being (सबाधः (*sabādhaḥ*)) afflicted by the obstruction of compulsion, praise; such a one, you, in the battle (वृत्रतूर्ये (*vṛtratūrye*)), for the destruction of the foe or for the destruction of sin, be aware (बोधि (*bodhi*)). Or else, be aware in the battle. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Agni) यं (*yaṃ*)—which
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- जनासः (*janāsaḥ*)—all the praisers
- सबाधः (*sabādhaḥ*)—gathered together
- वाजसातये (*vājasātaye*)—for the gain of food
- ईळते (*īḷate*)—praise
- सः (*saḥ*)—such you
- वृत्रतूर्ये (*vṛtratūrye*)—that in battle our enemies must be destroyed
- बोधि (*bodhi*)—know

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, you whom all the praisers, gathered together, praise for the gain of food, such as you, know that you must destroy our enemies in battle.

##### English translation
O you whom the crowding worshippers praise for the obtainment of food, attend to them for the destruction of their enemies.

### Printed p. 127

#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒हं हु॑वा॒न आ॒र्क्षे श्रु॒तर्व॑णि मद॒च्युति॑ ।  
> शर्धां॑सीव स्तुका॒विनां॑ मृ॒क्षा शी॒र्षा च॑तु॒र्णाम् ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अहम् । हुवानः । आर्क्षे । श्रुतर्वणि । मदऽच्युति ।  
> शर्धांसिऽइव । स्तुकाऽविनाम् । मृक्षा । शीर्षा । चतुर्णाम् ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अहम् ऋषिः हुवानः हूयमानो यज्ञदिदृक्षार्थं श्रुतर्वणि एतन्नाम्नि राजनि मदच्युति शत्रूणां मदस्य च्यावयितरि स्तुकाविनाम् । स्तुकाविन ऊर्णायवः । स्तुकः केशसंघातः । तद्वतां शर्धांसीव उच्छ्रितानि लोमानीव तानि यथा स्पृशंति तद्वत् वृक्षा वृक्षाणि । वृश्च्यंत इति वृक्षाः केशाः । तद्वंति वृक्षाणि शीर्षा शीर्षाणि शिरांसि । केषाम् । चतुर्णां श्रुतर्वणा प्रदत्तानामश्वानां शिरांस्युन्मृजामीति शेषः । अथवा वृक्षा वृक्षेण । व्रश्चनसाधनत्वाद्वृक्षो हस्तः । तेनोन्मृजामि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

I, the seer, being invited (हुवानः (*huvānaḥ*)) in order to see the sacrifice, before the king named Śrutarvan, the one who makes the pride of enemies fall (मदच्युति (*madacyuti*)). स्तुकाविनाम् (*stukāvinām*): the owners of it are the woolly (sheep); स्तुकः (*stukaḥ*) is a mass of hair. As one touches the raised hairs (शर्धांसि (*śardhāṃsi*)) of those that have it, so I stroke (मृक्षा (*mṛkṣā*)) the heads (शीर्षा (*śīrṣā*)) of the four horses given by Śrutarvan; the वृक्षाः (*vṛkṣāḥ*) are hairs (that which is cut off), and the heads have them. Or else मृक्षा (*mṛkṣā*) is "with the hand", the hand being the instrument of cutting; with it I stroke.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- आर्क्षे (*ārkṣe*)—the son of Ṛkṣa
- मदच्युति (*madacyuti*)—the destroyer of the pride of enemies
- श्रुतर्वणि (*śrutarvaṇi*)—before King Śrutarvan
- हवानः (*havānaḥ*)—invited
- अहं (*ahaṃ*)—I
- स्तुकाविनां (*stukāvināṃ*)—of the plentifully grown sheep
- शर्धांसीव (*śardhāṃsīva*)—as one strokes the wool
- चतुर्णां (*caturṇāṃ*)—of the four horses given to me by Śrutarvan
- शीर्षा (*śīrṣā*)—the heads
- मृक्षा (*mṛkṣā*)—I stroke with my hand **NOTE**

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

I, invited before King Śrutarvan, the son of Ṛkṣa and the destroyer of the pride of enemies, stroke with my hand the heads of the four horses given to me by Śrutarvan, as one strokes the wool of plentifully grown sheep.

##### English translation
Summoned before Shrutarvan, the son of Riksha, the humbler of the

### Printed p. 128

pride of his enemies, (I stroke) with my hand the heads of the four horses (which he has given me), as (men stroke) the long wool of rams.

#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> मां च॒त्वार॑ आ॒शवः॒ शवि॑ष्ठस्य द्रवि॒त्नवः॑ ।  
> सु॒रथा॑सो अ॒भि प्रयो॒ वक्ष॒न्वयो॒ न तुग्र्य॑म् ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> माम् । चत्वारः । आशवः । शविष्ठस्य । द्रवित्नवः ।  
> सुऽरथासः । अभि । प्रयः । वक्षन् । वयः । न । तुग्र्यम् ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मां शविष्ठस्य अतिशयेनान्नवतः श्रुतर्वणो राज्ञः संबंधिनः चत्वारः आशवः अश्वाः द्रवित्नवः गमनशीलाः सुरथासः शोभनरथा अश्वाः प्रयः अन्नं शत्रूणां प्रति अभि वक्षन् अभिवहंति । वयो’ न तुग्र्यम्। भुज्युं यथाश्विभ्यां प्रेरिताश्चतस्रो नावः स्वगृहं प्रापयन् तद्वदिति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The four swift horses (आशवः (*āśavaḥ*), द्रवित्नवः (*dravitnavaḥ*)) belonging to King Śrutarvan, the most strong-in-food (शविष्ठस्य (*śaviṣṭhasya*)), the horses with good chariots (सुरथासः (*surathāsaḥ*)), carry me towards the food of the enemies (प्रयः (*prayaḥ*)). As (वयो न तुग्र्यम् (*vayo na tugryam*)) the four ships sent by the two Aśvins carried Bhujyu to his own house, so.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वयः (*vayaḥ*)—the four ships given by the Aśvins
- तुग्र्यं न (*tugryaṃ na*)—as they carried Bhujyu and brought him home
- शविष्ठस्य (*śaviṣṭhasya*)—given by Śrutarvan, who has wealth of food
- द्रवित्नवः (*dravitnavaḥ*)—swift
- सुरथासः (*surathāsaḥ*)—yoked to an excellent chariot
- चत्वारः आशवः (*catvāraḥ āśavaḥ*)—the four horses
- मां (*māṃ*)—me
- प्रयः अभि (*prayaḥ abhi*)—towards the food belonging to the enemies
- वक्षन् (*vakṣan*)—carry

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As the four ships given by the Aśvins carried Bhujyu and brought him home, so the four horses, given by Śrutarvan, who has wealth of food, swift and yoked to an excellent chariot, carry me towards the food belonging to the enemies

##### English translation
Four swift horses of that most mighty king, yoked to a splendid car, bear me forth to seize the substance (of my enemies), as the ships bore home the son of Tugra.

### Printed p. 129

#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स॒त्यमित्त्वा॑ महेनदि॒ परु॒ष्ण्यव॑ देदिशम् ।  
> नेमा॑पो अश्व॒दात॑रः॒ शवि॑ष्ठादस्ति॒ मर्त्यः॑ ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सत्यम् । इत् । त्वा । महेऽनदि । परुष्णि । अव । देदिशम् ।  
> न । ईम् । आपः । अश्वऽदातरः । शविष्ठात् । अस्ति । मर्त्यः ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे महेनदि परुष्णि एतन्नामिके त्वा त्वां सत्यमित् सत्यमेव अव देदिशम् आदिशामि वदामि । नद्याकारेण संबोध्यापः संबोधयति । हे आपः ईम् अस्मात् शविष्ठात् बलवत्तमाच्छ्रुतर्वणोऽधिकः कश्चित् अश्वदातरः अश्वानां दातृतमः मर्त्यः न अस्ति । परुष्ण्यास्तीरे राज्ञोऽश्वमेधप्रतिग्रहात्तां संबोध्य ब्रूते ॥ ॥ २३ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O great river Parushṇī of that name, I declare to you the truth (सत्यमित् (*satyamit*)), I proclaim it. Addressing the waters in the form of a river, he addresses them: O waters, no mortal is a greater giver of horses than this most mighty Śrutarvan. Because the king accepted the horse-sacrifice gift on the bank of the Parushṇī, he addresses her.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- परुष्णि (*paruṣṇi*)—O river of the name Parushṇī
- महेनदि (*mahenadi*)—O river of great nature
- त्वा (*tvā*)—before you
- सत्यमित् (*satyamit*)—in truth
- अव देदिशं (*ava dediśaṃ*)—I declare
- आपः (*āpaḥ*)—O river waters
- शविष्ठात् (*śaviṣṭhāt*)—more than this most mighty
- ईं (*īṃ*)—Śrutarvan
- अश्वदातरः (*aśvadātaraḥ*)—who gives horses
- मर्त्यः (*martyaḥ*)—a man
- न अस्ति (*na asti*)—there is none at all in the world

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O river of the name Parushṇī, of great nature, I declare before you in truth: O river waters, there is no man in this world who gives horses more than this most mighty King Śrutarvan.

##### English translation
Verily I address you, O great river Parushni ; O waters, there is no mortal who gives horses more liberally than this most mighty (monarch)

### Printed p. 130

## Sūkta 75 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ‘युक्ष्वा हि' इति षोडशर्च षष्ठं सूक्तमांगिरसस्य विरूपस्यार्षं गायत्रमाग्नेयम् । तथा चानुक्रमणिका-– ‘युक्ष्वा हि षोळश विरूपः' इति । दशरात्रे तृतीयेऽहनीदं सूक्तमाज्यशस्त्रम् । सूत्रितं च -– तृतीये युक्ष्वा हीत्याज्यम्' (आश्व. श्रौ. ७. १०) इति । प्रातरनुवाकेऽप्याग्नेये गायत्रे छंदस्याश्विनशस्त्रे चेदं सूक्तम् । सूत्रितं च --युक्ष्वा हि प्रेष्ठं वः' ( आश्व. श्रौ. ४, १३) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta युक्ष्वा हि (*yukṣvā hi*) has sixteen Ṛks and is the sixth sūkta; it is the vision of Virūpa Āṅgirasa, in the Gāyatrī metre, addressed to Agni. So the Anukramaṇikā says: युक्ष्वा हि षोळश विरूपः (*yukṣvā hi ṣoḷaśa virūpaḥ*). On the third day of the Daśarātra this sūkta is the Ājya-śastra; it is set out in the sūtra तृतीये युक्ष्वा हीत्याज्यं (*tṛtīye yukṣvā hītyājyaṃ*) (Āśv. Śrau. 7. 10). Also, in the Prātaranuvāka, in the Āgneya Gāyatra metre, and in the Āśvina-śastra, this sūkta is used; it is set out in the sūtra युक्ष्वा हि प्रेष्ठं वः (*yukṣvā hi preṣṭhaṃ vaḥ*) (Āśv. Śrau. 4. 13).

Translation of the Kannada: This sūkta beginning युक्ष्वा हि (*yukṣvā hi*) is the sixth sūkta in the eighth Anuvāka. It has sixteen Ṛks. The seer of this sūkta is the one named Virūpa Āṅgirasa; Agni is the deity; the metre is Gāyatrī. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: युक्ष्वा हि षोळश विरूपः (*yukṣvā hi ṣoḷaśa virūpaḥ*). It is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta-sūtra तृतीये युक्ष्वा हीत्याज्यं (*tṛtīye yukṣvā hītyājyaṃ*) (Ā. 7-10) that this sūkta is used on the third day of the sacrifice called Daśarātra for the Ājya-śastra mantras; and it is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta-sūtra युक्ष्वा हि प्रेष्ठं वः (*yukṣvā hi preṣṭhaṃ vaḥ*) (Ā. 4-13) that at the time of reciting the Prātaranuvāka mantras this sūkta is used for the Ṛks in the Gāyatrī metre related to the Āgneya rite, and for the Āśvina-śastra mantras.

Sūkta 75. Maṇḍala 8, Anuvāka 8, Sūkta 75. Aṣṭaka 6, Adhyāya 5, Vargas 24, 25, 26. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 16. Seer: Virūpa. Deity: Agni. Metre: Gāyatrī.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यु॒क्ष्वा हि दे॑व॒हूत॑माँ॒ अश्वाँ॑ अग्ने र॒थीरि॑व ।  
> नि होता॑ पू॒र्व्यः स॑दः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> युक्ष्व । हि । देवऽहूतमान् । अश्वान् । अग्ने । रथीःऽइव ।  
> नि । होता । पूर्व्यः । सदः ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 131

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने देवहूतमान् देवानामाह्वातृतमान् अश्वान् युक्ष्व योजय रथे। रथीरिव यथा रथी स्वाश्वानिष्टदेशगमनाय योजयति तद्वत् । तथा कृत्वा होता त्वं पूर्व्यः मुख्यः सन् नि षदः उपविश च ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, yoke to the chariot the horses that are the best summoners of the gods (देवहूतमान् (*devahūtamān*)), as a charioteer (रथीरिव (*rathīriva*)) yokes his own horses to go to the desired place. Having done so, you, the Hotṛ, being the chief (पूर्व्यः (*pūrvyaḥ*)), sit down.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- रथीरिव (*rathīriva*)—as the driver of a chariot yokes horses to his chariot
- देवहूतमान् (*devahūtamān*)—those who call the gods in the best manner
- अश्वान् (*aśvān*)—the horses
- युक्ष्व हि (*yukṣva hi*)—yoke to the chariot
- होता (*hotā*)—you in the form of the Hotṛ
- पूर्व्यः (*pūrvyaḥ*)—the chief of the sacrifice
- नि षदः (*ni ṣadaḥ*)—sit down on the seat

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, as the driver of a chariot yokes horses to his chariot, yoke to the chariot the horses that call and bring the gods in the best manner. You, in the form of the Hotṛ, the chief of the sacrifice, sit down on the seat.

##### English translation
Agni, like a charioteer yoke your good-invoking steeds ; seat yourself first, the invoker.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒त नो॑ देव दे॒वाँ अच्छा॑ वोचो वि॒दुष्ट॑रः ।  
> श्रद्विश्वा॒ वार्या॑ कृधि ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत । नः । देव । देवान् । अच्छ । वोचः । विदुःऽतरः ।  
> श्रत् । विश्वा । वार्या । कृधि ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे देव अग्ने उत अपि च नः अस्मान् देवान् अच्छा वोचः अभिब्रूयाः । सम्यगनुष्ठितवंत इति । तथा विदुष्टरः विद्वत्तमान् वोचः । तथा कृत्वा विश्वा सर्वाणि वार्या वरणीयानि धनानि देवसंबंधीनि श्रत् सत्यानि कृधि कुर्वस्माकम् । अथवास्मदीयानि सर्वाणि वरणीयानि हवींषि श्रत् सत्यानि कुरु । देवान् प्रापयेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O divine Agni, proclaim (वोचः (*vocaḥ*)) us to the gods, as having duly performed, and as the most knowing (विदुष्टरः (*viduṣṭaraḥ*)). Having done so, make all desirable (वार्या (*vāryā*)) riches belonging to the gods true (श्रत् (*śrat*)) for us. Or else: make all our desirable oblations true, that is, make them reach the gods.

### Printed p. 132

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देव (*deva*)—O Agni, of the nature of a god
- उत (*uta*)—and
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- देवान् अच्छ (*devān accha*)—before the gods
- विदुष्टरः (*viduṣṭaraḥ*)—as of exceeding understanding
- वोचः (*vocaḥ*)—proclaim with praise
- विश्वा (*viśvā*)—all
- वार्या (*vāryā*)—the desired riches
- श्रत् (*śrat*)—as of the nature of truth
- कृधि (*kṛdhi*)—make

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, of the nature of a god, proclaim us with praise before the gods as of exceeding understanding. Make all the riches, according to our desire, fit for our enjoyment.

##### English translation
Divine (Agni), proclaim us to the gods as profoundly skilled ; assure to us all desirable things ;

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वं ह॒ यद्य॑विष्ठ्य॒ सह॑सः सूनवाहुत ।  
> ऋ॒तावा॑ य॒ज्ञियो॒ भुवः॑ ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वम् । ह । यत् । यविष्ठ्य । सहसः । सूनो इति । आऽहुत ।  
> ऋतऽवा । यज्ञियः । भुवः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने यविष्ठ्य युवतम सहसः सूनो बलस्य पुत्र आहुत सर्वतो हुत आहूत वा त्वं यत् यदा ह खलु ऋतावा सत्यवान् यज्ञियः यज्ञार्हश्च भुवः भवसि तदा वार्याणि श्रत्कुर्विति संबंधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, youngest (यविष्ठ्य (*yaviṣṭhya*)), son of strength (सहसः सूनो (*sahasaḥ sūno*)), invoked from every side (आहुत (*āhuta*)), when you become truthful (ऋतावा (*ṛtāvā*)) and worthy of the sacrifice (यज्ञियः (*yajñiyaḥ*)), then make the desirable things true; this is the connection.

### Printed p. 133

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यविष्ठ्य (*yaviṣṭhya*)—most youthful
- सहसः सूनो (*sahasaḥ sūno*)—son of strength
- आहुत (*āhuta*)—one who is called upon everywhere; O Agni, such as you are
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- यद्ध (*yaddha*)—by whose power of yours
- ऋतावा (*ṛtāvā*)—truthful
- यज्ञियः (*yajñiyaḥ*)—possessed of sacrifice
- भुवः (*bhuvaḥ*)—you are (by that very power fulfil our desired ends)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, most youthful, son of strength, invoked everywhere, by that power of yours by which you are truthful and possessed of sacrifice, by that very power fulfil our desired ends.

##### English translation
Since you are truthful and worthy of sacrifice, O most youthful, son of strength and everywhere honoured with offerings.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒यम॒ग्निः स॑ह॒स्रिणो॒ वाज॑स्य श॒तिन॒स्पतिः॑ ।  
> मू॒र्धा क॒वी र॑यी॒णाम् ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अयम् । अग्निः । सहस्रिणः । वाजस्य । शतिनः । पतिः ।  
> मूर्धा । कविः । रयीणाम् ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अयमग्निः शतिनः सहस्रिणः चोक्तसंख्योपेतस्य वाजस्य अन्नस्य पतिः स्वामी मूर्धा शिरोवदुन्नतः श्रेष्ठः कविः मेधावी रयीणां धनानामपि पतिरिति शेषः । तदुभयं प्रयच्छत्वित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Agni is the lord of food (वाजस्य (*vājasya*)) that has the stated numbers, a hundredfold and a thousandfold. He is the head, that is, exalted like a head, the foremost; the seer, that is, the wise one. He is also the lord of riches, which is to be supplied. The sense is: may he grant both food and wealth.

### Printed p. 134

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अयं अग्निः (*ayaṃ agniḥ*)—this well-known Agni
- शतिनः (*śatinaḥ*)—of hundredfold number
- सहस्रिणः (*sahasriṇaḥ*)—of thousandfold number
- वाजस्य (*vājasya*)—of food
- पतिः (*patiḥ*)—is the lord
- मूर्धा (*mūrdhā*)—is the foremost
- कविः (*kaviḥ*)—is the wise one
- रयीणां (*rayīṇāṃ*)—is the giver of all riches

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This well-known Agni is the lord of food, hundredfold and thousandfold, the foremost, the wise one, and the giver of all riches.

##### English translation
This Agni is the lord of hundredfold and thousandfold food; he is the head, the seer, (the lord) of wealth.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the Darśapūrṇamāsa sacrifices, when an offering is made to Agni, this Ṛk अयमग्निः (*ayamagniḥ*) is to be recited as an optional yājyā mantra, as explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra अयमग्निः सहस्रिण इति वेदं विष्णुर्वि चक्रमे (*ayamagniḥ sahasriṇa iti vedaṃ viṣṇurvi cakrame*) (Ā. 1-6).

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तं ने॒मिमृ॒भवो॑ य॒था न॑मस्व॒ सहू॑तिभिः ।  
> नेदी॑यो य॒ज्ञमं॑गिरः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तम् । नेमिम् । ऋभवः । यथा । आ । नमस्व । सहूतिऽभिः ।  
> नेदीयः । यज्ञम् । अंगिरः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अंगिरः त्वं सहूतिभिः समानाह्वानैरन्यैर्देवैः सह नेदीयः अंतिकतमं यज्ञम् आ नमस्व' आनमय । ऋभवः नेमिं रथमिव ॥ ॥ २४ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aṅgiras, together with the other gods who share your invocation, bend towards us the sacrifice that is nearest, as the Ṛbhus bend the felly of a chariot.

### Printed p. 135

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अंगिरः (*aṃgiraḥ*)—O Agni, of the form of Aṅgiras
- ऋभवः (*ṛbhavaḥ*)—the Ṛbhus
- नेमिं यथा (*nemiṃ yathā*)—as they bend the felly of a chariot wheel
- सहूतिभिः (*sahūtibhiḥ*)—with the other deities who have the invocation together with you
- नेदीयः (*nedīyaḥ*)—the nearest
- तं यज्ञं (*taṃ yajñaṃ*)—towards that same sacrifice
- आ नमस्व (*ā namasva*)—come bending

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, of the form of Aṅgiras, as the Ṛbhus bend the felly of a chariot wheel, so come bending, together with the other deities who share your invocation, towards the nearest sacrifice.

##### English translation
O Angiras, with the deities associated in the invocation, draw this offering near you as the Ribhus (bend) the circumference of a wheel.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तस्मै॑ नू॒नम॒भिद्य॑वे वा॒चा वि॑रूप॒ नित्य॑या ।  
> वृष्णे॑ चोदस्व सुष्टु॒तिम् ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तस्मै । नूनम् । अभिऽद्यवे । वाचा । विऽरूप । नित्यया ।  
> वृष्णे । चोदस्व । सुऽस्तुतिम् ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे विरूप नानारूपैतन्नामक महर्षे त्वं तस्मै प्रसिद्धाय अभिद्यवे अभिगतदीप्तये वृष्णे वर्षकायाग्नये नित्यया उत्पत्तिरहितया वाचा मंत्ररूपया सुष्टुतिं नूनम् इदानीं चोदस्व स्तुहीत्येवमृषिः स्वात्मानं ब्रवीति यजमानो वा होतारं विरूपम् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Virūpa, great sage of that name, now send forth a good praise, in the form of a mantra and a voice that has no origin, to that well-known Agni, the radiant one, the showerer. In this way the seer speaks to himself; or else the sacrificer speaks to the Hotṛ, Virūpa.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विरूप (*virūpa*)—O sage named Virūpa
- तस्मै (*tasmai*)—to him who is well-known
- अभिद्यवे (*abhidyave*)—who has widespread radiance
- वृष्णे (*vṛṣṇe*)—who grants desired ends, to that Agni
- नित्यया (*nityayā*)—that has no origin

### Printed p. 136

- वाचा (*vācā*)—with a voice in the form of a mantra
- सुष्टुतिं (*suṣṭutiṃ*)—excellent praise
- नूसं (*nūsaṃ*)—now
- चोदस्व (*codasva*)—recite

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O sage named Virūpa, now recite excellent praise, with a voice that is constant and in the form of a mantra, to Agni, the well-known, of widespread radiance, the granter of desired ends.

##### English translation
Virupa, with constant voice address you praise to this well-pleased showerer (of blessings).

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> कमु॑ ष्विदस्य॒ सेन॑या॒ग्नेरपा॑कचक्षसः ।  
> प॒णिं गोषु॑ स्तरामहे ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> कम् । ऊं इति । स्वित् । अस्य । सेनया । अग्नेः । अपाकऽचक्षसः ।  
> पणिम् । गोषु । स्तरामहे ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अस्य अग्नेः अपाकचक्षसः अनल्पचक्षसोऽग्नेः सेनया ज्वालारूपया गोषु निमित्तेषु कमु ष्वित् कं खलु पणिं स्तरामहे । स्तरणं हिंसनम् । इदानीं बलिनमभिभवेमेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

By the army of this Agni of vast radiance, an army in the form of flames, on account of the cows, which enemy, the Paṇi, shall we overthrow? Overthrowing (स्तरणं (*staraṇaṃ*)) is killing. The meaning is: now may we overcome the strong one.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अपाकचक्षसः (*apākacakṣasaḥ*)—of exceedingly increased radiance
- अस्य अग्नेः (*asya agneḥ*)—of this Agni
- सेनया (*senayā*)—with the help of the army in the form of flames
- गोषु (*goṣu*)—to obtain cows
- कमु स्वित् पणिं (*kamu svit paṇiṃ*)—which enemy in the form of a Paṇi indeed
- स्तरामहे (*starāmahe*)—should we conquer

### Printed p. 137

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

With the help of the flame-like army of this Agni, of exceedingly increased radiance, we shall be able to conquer all enemies. Now, to obtain cows, which enemy shall we face and defeat? Who is such an enemy in the form of a Paṇi?

##### English translation
What strong enemy shall we overthrow, to win cows by the help of the host of this Agni of unmeasured radiance ?

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> मा नो॑ दे॒वानां॒ विशः॑ प्रस्ना॒तीरि॑वो॒स्राः ।  
> कृ॒शं न हा॑सु॒रघ्न्याः॑ ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मा । नः । देवानाम् । विशः । प्रस्नातीःऽइव । उस्राः ।  
> कृशम् । न । हासुः । अघ्न्याः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> देवानां सर्वेषां विशः प्रजाभूतान् परिचारकान् नः अस्मान् मा हासीत् अग्निर्मा परित्यजतु । प्रस्नातीः उस्राः इव पयः क्षरंतीर्गाव इव । ता यथा न मुंचंति । उभयमपि न परित्यजत्वित्यर्थः । किमिव । कृशम् अल्पं स्ववत्सम् अघ्न्याः गावो यथा न हासुः न परित्यजंति तद्वत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

May Agni not forsake us, the subjects and servants of all the gods. As cows that yield abundant milk do not let go, so may he not abandon either. How? As cows (अघ्न्याः (*aghnyāḥ*)) do not abandon their own weak, small calf, so may he not abandon us.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अघ्न्याः (*aghnyāḥ*)—the cows
- कृशं (*kṛśaṃ*)—their young calves
- न हासुः (*na hāsuḥ*)—as they do not abandon, so
- देवानां (*devānāṃ*)—of all the gods
- विशः (*viśaḥ*)—who are subjects
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- प्रस्नातीः (*prasnātīḥ*)—that give milk in abundance
- उस्राः इव (*usrāḥ iva*)—Agni, who is beneficent like milch cows
- मा (*mā*)—may not forsake

### Printed p. 138

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As cows do not abandon their young calves, so may Agni, who is beneficent like the milch cows that give milk in abundance, not forsake us, who are subjects of all the gods.

##### English translation
May he not ( forsake ) us, the licgemen of the gods, as the milk-streaming cows (forske not) ; the cows abandon not a little (calf). **NOTE**

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> मा नः॑ समस्य दू॒ढ्य१॒ः॑ परि॑द्वेषसो अंह॒तिः ।  
> ऊ॒र्मिर्न नाव॒मा व॑धीत् ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मा । नः । समस्य । दुःऽध्यः । परिऽद्वेषसः । अंहतिः ।  
> ऊर्मिः । न । नावम् । आ । वधीत् ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> समस्य सर्वस्य परिद्वेषसः परितो द्विषतः दूढ्यः पापबुद्धेः अंहतिः हननं मा आ वधीत् मा हिंस्यात् । नावम् ऊर्मिः समुद्रतरंग इव । स यथा तां पीडयति तद्वन्मा वधीदित्यर्थः । अत्र ‘मा नः सर्वस्य दुर्धियः' (निरु. ५. २३) इत्यादि निरुक्तं द्रष्टव्यम् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

May the destruction, the harm, of every evil-minded one who hates us on all sides not strike us down, as a wave of the sea strikes a ship; as the wave oppresses the ship, so may it not strike us. Here the Nirukta on मा नः सर्वस्य दुर्धियः (*mā naḥ sarvasya durdhiyaḥ*) (Ni. 5. 23) and so on is to be seen.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऊर्मिः (*ūrmiḥ*)—the waves of the sea
- नावं न (*nāvaṃ na*)—as they strike a boat and destroy it
- समस्य (*samasya*)—of all people
- परिद्वेषसः (*paridveṣasaḥ*)—of the enemies who surround us
- दूर्ध्यः (*dūrdhyaḥ*)—of the evil-minded
- अंहतिः (*aṃhatiḥ*)—the inclination to harm
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- मा आ वधीत् (*mā ā vadhīt*)—may it not destroy

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As the waves of the sea strike a boat and destroy it, so may the evil-minded inclination to harm of all the enemies who surround us not destroy us.

### Printed p. 139

##### English translation
Let not the onset of any evil-minded adversary harm us as the wave (overwhelms) a ship.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

The word सोमस्यशब्दवु (*somasyaśabdavu*) **NOTE** is regarded as a pronoun. समं (*samaṃ*) means "all". Yāska gives the etymology: समस्य । सममिति परिग्रहार्थीयं सर्वनामानुदात्तं (*samasya | samamiti parigrahārthīyaṃ sarvanāmānudāttaṃ*) (Ni. 5-27); that is, the word समं (*samaṃ*) is used in the sense of "taking in, including"; it is well known as a pronoun; its accent is wholly anudātta. Having said this, and taking the present Ṛk itself as the example in support of it, he gives its explanation: मा नः सर्वस्य दुर्धियः पापधियः सर्वतो द्वेषसो अंहतिरूर्मिरिव नावमावधीत् । ऊर्मिरूर्णोतेर्नौः प्रणोत्तव्या भवति । नमतेर्वा । तत्कथमनुदात्तप्रकृति नानु स्यात् दृष्टव्ययं तु भवति ॥ (*mā naḥ sarvasya durdhiyaḥ pāpadhiyaḥ sarvato dveṣaso aṃhatirūrmiriva nāvamāvadhīt | ūrmirūrṇoternauḥ praṇottavyā bhavati | namatervā | tatkathamanudāttaprakṛti nānu syāt dṛṣṭavyayaṃ tu bhavati ‖*) (Ni. 5-27) **NOTE**.

May the evil intent of enemy people, that is, of those who act with a sinful mind towards us, not harm us. As waves of water strike all round a boat that is on the water, so may the sinful mind of wicked people not harm us: thus an illustration is given for the waves of water and the sinful minds of wicked people. The word ऊर्मि (*ūrmi*) is formed from the root ऊर्णोति (*ūrṇoti*), which means "to cover". Because the waves repeatedly cover the shore, they have the name ऊर्मि (*ūrmi*). The word नौः (*nauḥ*) is formed from the root नयति (*nayati*), which means "to rise" or "to float" **NOTE**; that is, it is drawn or moves over the water, and so a boat has the name नौः (*nauḥ*). Or else this word may be formed from the root नमति (*namati*), which means "to bend". Now, the pronominal word समं (*samaṃ*) has been said to be wholly anudātta; is it not contradictory for a noun or pronoun to have the designation wholly anudātta? The answer is that this word समं (*samaṃ*), like a subanta word, is used in all seven cases, taking various forms with the endings. In the present Ṛk the genitive singular समस्य (*samasya*) is used. In the same way, in the Ṛk उतो समस्मिन्नाशितीहि नो वसो वाजी सुशिस्र गोमति (*uto samasminnāśitīhi no vaso vājī suśisra gomati*) (Ṛ. Saṃ. 8-1-8) the locative singular समस्मिन् (*samasmin*) is used. This is the main purport of the explanation.

### Printed p. 140

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नम॑स्ते अग्न॒ ओज॑से गृ॒णंति॑ देव कृ॒ष्टयः॑ ।  
> अमै॑र॒मित्र॑मर्दय ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नमः । ते । अग्ने । ओजसे । गृणंति । देव । कृष्टयः ।  
> अमैः । अमित्रम् । अर्दय ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने देव ते तुभ्यं नमः गृणंति नमस्कारशब्दमुंचारयंति । किमर्थम् । ओजसे बलाय । के। कृष्टयः मनुष्या यजमानाः । अतोऽहमपि गृणामीत्यर्थः । तथा अमैः बलैः अमित्रं शत्रुम् अर्दय नाशय ॥ ॥ २५ ॥
> ग्राम्येणाग्निना वैतानिकस्य संसर्गेऽग्नये संवर्गायेष्टिः कार्या । तत्र ‘कुवित्सु नः' इत्यनुवाक्या ‘ मा नो अस्मिन्' इति याज्या । सूत्रितं च -- कुवित्सु नो गविष्टये मा नो अस्मिन्महाधने' (आश्व. श्रौ. ३. १३) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O divine Agni, men, that is, the sacrificers, utter the word of homage to you, for strength; therefore I too utter it. And with your forces destroy the enemy. For the Saṃvarga iṣṭi: when the Vaitānika fire is brought into contact with the domestic fire, an iṣṭi to Agni Saṃvarga is to be performed. In it कुवित्सु नः (*kuvitsu naḥ*) is the anuvākyā and मा नो अस्मिन् (*mā no asmin*) the yājyā. It is also laid down in the sūtra: कुवित्सु नो गविष्टये मा नो अस्मिन्महाधने (*kuvitsu no gaviṣṭaye mā no asminmahādhane*) (Āśv. Śrau. 3. 13).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देव (*deva*)—of divine nature
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- ते (*te*)—to you
- कृष्टयः (*kṛṣṭayaḥ*)—men
- ओजसे (*ojase*)—for the acquisition of strength
- नमः (*namaḥ*)—praise accompanied by homage
- गृणंति (*gṛṇaṃti*)—recite
- अम्यैः (*amyaiḥ*)—with your forces
- अमित्रं (*amitraṃ*)—the enemy
- अर्दय (*ardaya*)—destroy

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, of divine nature, all men recite praise accompanied by homage to you for the acquisition of strength. (In the same way I too praise you.) With your forces destroy the enemies.

##### English translation
Divine Agni, men utter your praises for the attainment of strength ; by strength destroy the enemy.

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> कु॒वित्सु नो॒ गवि॑ष्ट॒येऽग्ने॑ सं॒वेषि॑षो र॒यिम् ।  
> उरु॑कृदु॒रु ण॑स्कृधि ॥ ११ ॥

### Printed p. 141

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> कुवित् । सु । नः । गोऽइष्टये । अग्ने । सम्ऽवेषिषः । रयिम् ।  
> उरुऽकृत् । उरु । नः । कृधि ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने त्वं नः अस्माकं गविष्टये गवामेषणाय कुवित् बहु रयिं धनं संवेषिषः संप्रापय । उरुकृत् त्वं नः अस्मान् उरु कृधि कुरु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, bring us much wealth, in order to seek cows for us. You, maker of wide room, make us wide, that is, give us room.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- गविष्टये (*gaviṣṭaye*)—cows
- कुवित् (*kuvit*)—very abundant
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—wealth
- सु संवेषिषः (*su saṃveṣiṣaḥ*)—provide
- उरुकृत् (*urukṛt*)—you, the one who causes increase
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- उरु (*uru*)—as prosperous
- कृधि (*kṛdhi*)—make

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, provide us with very abundant wealth to obtain cows. You, who cause increase, make us prosperous.

##### English translation
Agni, send us abundance of wealth to satisfy our desires ; giver of free space, grant us abundant room.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the Saṃvarga iṣṭi this Ṛk कुवित्स नः (*kuvitsa naḥ*) is to be recited as the anuvākyā mantra, and the next Ṛk, मा नो अस्मिन् (*mā no asmin*), as the yājyā mantra, as explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra कुवित्स नो गविष्टये मा नो अस्मन्महाधने (*kuvitsa no gaviṣṭaye mā no asmanmahādhane*) (Ā. 3-13).

### Printed p. 142

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> मा नो॑ अ॒स्मिन्म॑हाध॒ने परा॑ वर्ग्भार॒भृद्य॑था ।  
> सं॒वर्गं॒ सं र॒यिं ज॑य ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मा । नः । अस्मिन् । महाऽधने । परा । वर्क् । भारऽभृत् । यथा ।  
> सम्ऽवर्गम् । सम् । रयिम् । जय ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> नः अस्मान् अस्मिन् महाधने संग्रामे मा परा वर्क् मा परित्याक्षीः । भारभृद्यथा । भारवाही यथा भारमंते परित्यजति तद्वत् । संवर्गं शत्रुभ्यः सहाच्छिद्यमानं रयिं धनं सं जय अस्मदर्थम् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Do not abandon us in this great-wealth battle, as a bearer of a burden abandons the burden at the end. Win for us the wealth that is gathered together (संवर्गं (*saṃvargaṃ*)), snatched away from the enemies.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- भारभृत् (*bhārabhṛt*)—(O Agni) one who carries a burden
- यथा (*yathā*)—as he abandons his burden, so
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- महाधने (*mahādhane*)—that is fought for abundant wealth
- अस्मिन् (*asmin*)—in this battle
- मा परा वर्क् (*mā parā vark*)—do not abandon
- संवर्गं (*saṃvargaṃ*)—gathered by the enemies
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—wealth
- स जय (*sa jaya*)—win for us

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, as one who carries a burden abandons his burden, so do not abandon us in this battle fought for abundant wealth. Win for us the wealth gathered by the enemies.

##### English translation
Leave us not in this conflict as a bearer his burden ; win for us the plundered wealth of our foes.

#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒न्यम॒स्मद्भि॒या इ॒यमग्ने॒ सिष॑क्तु दु॒च्छुना॑ ।  
> वर्धा॑ नो॒ अम॑व॒च्छवः॑ ॥ १३ ॥

### Printed p. 143

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अन्यम् । अस्मत् । भियै । इयम् । अग्ने । सिसक्तु । दुच्छुना ।  
> वर्ध । नः । अमऽवत् । शवः ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने त्वदीया इयं दुच्छुना बाधकसंहतिः अस्मत् अन्यम् अस्तोतारं भियै भयाय सिषक्तु सेवताम् । त्वं च नः अस्माकम् अमवत् बलोपेतं शवः वेगं वर्ध वर्धय संग्रामे ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, may this harassing host of yours attach itself, for fear, to one other than us, one who is not a praiser. And you, increase our strength endowed with force, our impetus, in the battle.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- दुच्छुना (*ducchunā*)—that causes harm
- इयं (*iyaṃ*)—this power
- अस्मत् (*asmat*)—than us
- अस्यं (*asyaṃ*)—of another enemy
- भियै (*bhiyai*)—as a cause of fear
- सिषक्तु (*siṣaktu*)—may it be employed
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- अमुवत् (*amuvat*)—powerful
- शवः (*śavaḥ*)—impetus
- वर्ध (*vardha*)—increase

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, may this harmful power be employed as a cause of fear to an enemy other than us. Increase our powerful impetus.

##### English translation
Agni, may your plagues pursue some other to terrify him ; increase our vigorous strength is battle.

#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यस्याजु॑षन्नम॒स्विनः॒ शमी॒मदु॑र्मखस्य वा ।  
> तं घेद॒ग्निर्वृ॒धाव॑ति ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यस्य । अजुषत् । नमस्विनः । शमीम् । अदुःऽमखस्य । वा ।  
> तम् । घ । इत् । अग्निः । वृधा । अवति ॥ १४ ॥

### Printed p. 144

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यस्य नमस्विनः नमस्कारवतः अदुर्मखस्य वा अदुष्टयागस्य वा शमीं कर्म अजुषत् असेवत तं घेत् तमेव यजमानं संग्रामे अग्निर्विधावति विशेषेण गच्छति । अतो नमोयुक्ता अदुर्मखाश्च भवामेति भावः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Whose act of rite, the act of a man full of homage or of one whose sacrifice is faultless, Agni has accepted, that very sacrificer Agni specially goes to, to help him, in the battle. Therefore let us be filled with homage and faultless in sacrifice: this is the meaning.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नमस्विनः (*namasvinaḥ*)—full of homage
- वा (*vā*)—or
- अदुर्मुखस्य (*adurmukhasya*)—who performs a sacrifice free of sin
- यस्य (*yasya*)—of whatever sacrificer
- शमीां (*śamīāṃ*)—the rite
- अजुषत् (*ajuṣat*)—Agni has accepted
- तं घेत् (*taṃ ghet*)—that very sacrificer
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- वृथा (*vṛthā*)—exceedingly
- अवति (*avati*)—protects

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Agni exceedingly protects that very sacrificer, full of homage or performing a sacrifice free of sin, whose rite Agni has accepted.

##### English translation
Agni especiallv protects (in battle) that praiser or zealous sacrificer whose offerings he has attended.

#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पर॑स्या॒ अधि॑ सं॒वतोऽव॑राँ अ॒भ्या त॑र ।  
> यत्रा॒हमस्मि॒ ताँ अ॑व ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> परस्याः । अधि । सम्ऽवतः । अवरान् । अभि । आ । तर ।  
> यत्र । अहम् । अस्मि । तान् । अव ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने परस्याः अन्यस्याः संवतः सेनायाः अवरान् अन्यानस्मदीयान् अभि अभिमुखम् आ सर्वतः तर तारय । वैरिसेना अस्मद्भटैः पराभावयेत्यर्थः । यत्र येष्वस्मदीयपरिजनमध्ये अहमस्मि स्वामी तानव रक्ष ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, carry our own people, the lower ones (अवरान् (*avarān*)), from every side towards the front across the other host; let the enemy hosts be defeated by our warriors. Protect those among whom, in the midst of my own people, I am, the master.

### Printed p. 145

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यत्र (*yatra*)—to whatever army (O Agni)
- अहं (*ahaṃ*)—I
- अस्मि (*asmi*)—am the lord
- अवरान् (*avarān*)—the people of the army who depend on me
- परस्याः अधि (*parasyāḥ adhi*)—belonging to the enemy
- संवतः (*saṃvataḥ*)—from the army
- अभि आ तर (*abhi ā tara*)—protect all around
- तान् (*tān*)—them
- अव (*ava*)—guard

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, those people of the army who depend on me, the army of which I am the lord, protect all around from the army that belongs to the enemy. Guard them.

##### English translation
Deliver us wholly from the hostile army, shield those among whom I am (lord).

#### Ṛk 16

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वि॒द्मा हि ते॑ पु॒रा व॒यमग्ने॑ पि॒तुर्यथाव॑सः ।  
> अधा॑ ते सु॒म्नमी॑महे ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> विद्म । हि । ते । पुरा । वयम् । अग्ने । पितुः । यथा । अवसः ।  
> अध । ते । सुम्नम् । ईमहे ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने पितुः पालकस्य ते तव अवसः अवो रक्षणं पुरा यथा तथेदानीमपीति विद्म । अधः तत् ते तव सुम्नं सुखम् ईमहे याचामहे । अथवा पितुर्यथेति दृष्टांतः । पितुः पालनं पुत्रो यथा वेत्ति तथेत्यर्थः ॥ ॥ २६ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, we know now, as before, the protection of you, the guardian; therefore we ask of you happiness. Or else पितुर्यथा (*pituryathā*) is an illustration: as a son knows the protection of his father, so we know yours.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- पितुः (*pituḥ*)—of the guardian
- ते (*te*)—your
- अवसः (*avasaḥ*)—protection
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we
- पुरा यथा (*purā yathā*)—as we knew it in former times
- विद्म हि (*vidma hi*)—we know also now
- अध (*adha*)—therefore
- ते (*te*)—from you
- सुम्नं (*sumnaṃ*)—happiness
- ईमहे (*īmahe*)—we ask

### Printed p. 146

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, as we knew in former times the protection of you, the guardian, so we know it also now. Therefore we ask happiness of you.

##### English translation
We know your protection, Agni, as of a father in former times, therefore we (again) desire of you that happiness.

The seventieth sūkta. **NOTE**

## Sūkta 76 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ‘इमं नु मायिनम्' इति द्वादशर्चं सप्तमं सूक्तम् । कुरुसुतिर्नाम काण्व ऋषिः । गायत्री छंदः । इंद्रो देवता । तथा चानुक्रांतम्- ‘ इमं नु द्वादश कुरुसुतिः काण्वः' इति । व्यूढे दशरात्रे चतुर्थेऽहनि मरुत्वतीय आद्यस्तृचः । सूत्रितं च--' इमं नु मायिनं हुवे त्यमु वः सत्रासाहम्' ( आश्व. श्रौ. ८. ८) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The seventh sūkta, of twelve Ṛks, beginning इमं नु मायिनम् (*imaṃ nu māyinam*). The seer is Kurusuti of the Kāṇva line, the metre Gāyatrī, the deity Indra. So the Anukramaṇikā says: इमं नु द्वादश कुरुसुतिः काण्वः (*imaṃ nu dvādaśa kurusutiḥ kāṇvaḥ*). In the Vyūḍha Daśarātra, on the fourth day, in the Marutvatīya, the first tṛca is taken from it. This is laid down in the sūtra इमं नु मायिनं हुवे त्यमु वः सत्रासाहम् (*imaṃ nu māyinaṃ huve tyamu vaḥ satrāsāham*) (Āśv. Śrau. 8. 8).

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta beginning इमं नु मायिनं (*imaṃ nu māyinaṃ*) is the seventh sūkta in the eighth anuvāka. It has twelve Ṛks. The seer of this sūkta is Kurusuti, born in the Kaṇva lineage; Indra is the deity; the metre is Gāyatrī. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: इमं नु द्वादश कुरुसुतिः काण्वः (*imaṃ nu dvādaśa kurusutiḥ kāṇvaḥ*). In the ten-day sacrifice called Vyūḍha, on the fourth day, among the Marutvatīya śastra mantras, the first three Ṛks of this sūkta, beginning इमं नु मायिनं (*imaṃ nu māyinaṃ*), are to be recited as the opening tṛca, as is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra इमं नु मायिनं हुवे त्वमु वः सत्रासाहं (*imaṃ nu māyinaṃ huve tvamu vaḥ satrāsāhaṃ*) (Ā. 8-8). **NOTE**

##### Particulars

Sūkta 76.

Maṇḍala 8. Anuvāka 8. Sūkta 76.

Aṣṭaka 6. Adhyāya 5. Vargas 27, 28.

Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 12.

Seer: Kurusuti Kāṇva.

Deity: Indra.

Metre: Gāyatrī.

### Printed p. 147

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इ॒मं नु मा॒यिनं॑ हुव॒ इंद्र॒मीशा॑न॒मोज॑सा ।  
> म॒रुत्वं॑तं॒ न वृं॒जसे॑ ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इमम् । नु । मायिनम् । हुवे । इंद्रम् । ईशानम् । ओजसा ।  
> मरुत्वंतम् । न । वृंजसे ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> इमं मायिनं प्रज्ञावंतम् ओजसा स्वबलेन ईशानं सर्वस्य स्वामिनं मरुत्वंतं न । नेति संप्रत्यर्थे । मरुद्भिस्तद्वंतमिदानीम् इंद्रं वृंजसे शत्रूणां छेदनाय हुवे आह्वयामि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

I invoke this Indra now, the wise one, lord of all by his own might, accompanied by the Maruts, for the cutting down of enemies. The word न (*na*) is in the sense of "now"; the one possessed of Maruts is Indra.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मायिनं (*māyinaṃ*)—the wise one
- ओजसा नु (*ojasā nu*)—by his own strength
- ईशानं (*īśānaṃ*)—the one who is the lord
- मरुत्वंतं (*marutvaṃtaṃ*)—the one who is accompanied by the Maruts
- इमुं इंद्रं (*imuṃ iṃdraṃ*)—this Indra
- न (*na*)—now
- वृंजसे (*vṛṃjase*)—for the destruction of enemies
- हुवे (*huve*)—I invoke with prayer

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This Indra, the wise one, the lord by his own strength, accompanied by the Maruts, I now invoke with prayer for the destruction of enemies.

##### English translation
I invoke now for the destruction of my enemies the wise Indra attended by the Maruts, ruling all by his power.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒यमिंद्रो॑ म॒रुत्स॑खा॒ वि वृ॒त्रस्या॑भिन॒च्छिरः॑ ।  
> वज्रे॑ण श॒तप॑र्वणा ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 148

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अयम् । इंद्रः । मरुत्ऽसखा । वि । वृत्रस्य । अभिनत् । शिरः ।  
> वज्रेण । शतऽपर्वणा ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अयमिंद्रो मरुत्सखा मरुद्युक्तः वृत्रस्य वि अभिनत् व्यच्छिनत् शिरः वज्रेण शतपर्वणा शतसंधिना ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Indra, the friend of the Maruts, united with the Maruts, split the head of Vṛtra with the hundred-jointed thunderbolt (शतसंधिना (*śatasaṃdhinā*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मरुत्सखा (*marutsakhā*)—friend of the Maruts
- आयं इंद्रः (*āyaṃ iṃdraḥ*)—this Indra (with the help of the Maruts)
- शतपर्वणा (*śataparvaṇā*)—with a hundred joints (edges)
- वज्रेण (*vajreṇa*)—with the thunderbolt
- वृत्रस्य (*vṛtrasya*)—of the demon Vṛtra
- शिरः (*śiraḥ*)—the head
- व्यभिसत् (*vyabhisat*)—has split

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This Indra, the friend of the Maruts, with the help of the Maruts has split the head of the demon Vṛtra with the thunderbolt of a hundred joints (edges).

##### English translation
Indra, attended by the Maruts, has cleft the head of Vritra with his hundred jointed thunderbolt.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वा॒वृ॒धा॒नो म॒रुत्स॒खेंद्रो॒ वि वृ॒त्रमै॑रयत् ।  
> सृ॒जन्स॑मु॒द्रिया॑ अ॒पः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ववृधानः । मरुत्ऽसखा । इंद्रः । वि । वृत्रम् । ऐरयत् ।  
> सृजन् । समुद्रियाः । अपः ॥ ३ ॥

### Printed p. 149

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अयम् इंद्रः वावृधानः वर्धमानः मरुत्सखा मरुत्सहायः वृत्रं मेघं वि ऐरयत् विदारितवान् । किं कुर्वन् । समुद्रियाः । समुद्रमंतरिक्षम् । तत्संबंधिन्यः अपः सृजन् ।
> षष्ठेऽहनि मरुत्वतीये ‘अयं ह येन ' इति मरुत्वन्निविद्धानीयः । सूत्रितं च – 'अयं ह येन वा इदमुप नो हरिभिः सुतम् ' ( आश्व. श्रौ. ८. ८) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Indra, growing, the ally of the Maruts, tore apart Vṛtra, the cloud. What was he doing? Letting loose the waters belonging to the samudra, the atmosphere. On the sixth day, in the Marutvatīya, the verse अयं ह येन (*ayaṃ ha yena*) serves as the मरुत्वन्निविद्धानीयः (*marutvannividdhānīyaḥ*). It is also laid down in the sūtra अयं ह येन वा इदमुप नो हरिभिः सुतम् (*ayaṃ ha yena vā idamupa no haribhiḥ sutam*) (Āśv. Śrau. 8. 8).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मरुत्सखा (*marutsakhā*)—who has the help of the Maruts
- वावृधानः (*vāvṛdhānaḥ*)—who has grown
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- समुद्रियाः (*samudriyāḥ*)—belonging to the atmosphere
- अपः (*apaḥ*)—the waters
- सृजन् (*sṛjan*)—releasing
- वृत्रं (*vṛtraṃ*)—the cloud
- व्यैरयत् (*vyairayat*)—has split

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Indra, who has the help of the Maruts and has grown, has split the cloud, releasing the waters that belong to the atmosphere.

##### English translation
Indra, increasing in might, attended by the Maruts, has torn Vritra asunder, letting loose the waters of the firmament.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒यं ह॒ येन॒ वा इ॒दं स्व॑र्म॒रुत्व॑ता जि॒तम् ।  
> इंद्रे॑ण॒ सोम॑पीतये ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अयम् । ह । येन । वै । इदम् । स्वः । मरुत्वता । जितम् ।  
> इंद्रेण । सोमऽपीतये ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अयं ह' खल्विंद्रः येन वै येन खलु मरुत्वता मरुद्भिर्युक्तेन इंद्रेण इदं स्वः स्वर्गाख्यं स्थानमिदं स्वः सर्वं कर्म वा यद्वेदं सर्वं जगत् जितम् । किमर्थम् । सोमपीतये सोमपानाय ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Indra is the one by whom, accompanied by the Maruts, this स्वः (*svaḥ*), the place called heaven, or else all rites, or, as another explanation, the whole world, has been won. For what purpose? For the drinking of Soma.

### Printed p. 150

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मरुत्वता (*marutvatā*)—accompanied by the Maruts
- येन (*yena*)—by whom
- इंद्रेण वै (*iṃdreṇa vai*)—by Indra himself
- सोमपीतये (*somapītaye*)—for the drinking of Soma
- इदं स्वः (*idaṃ svaḥ*)—this heaven
- जितं (*jitaṃ*)—was won
- आयं ह (*āyaṃ ha*)—this is that very Indra

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This is that very Indra, accompanied by the Maruts, by whom this heaven was won for the drinking of Soma.

##### English translation
This is that Indra, by whom assisted by the Maruts, yonder heaven was conquered, to quaff the Soma.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> म॒रुत्वं॑तमृजी॒षिण॒मोज॑स्वंतं विर॒प्शिन॑म् ।  
> इंद्रं॑ गी॒र्भिर्ह॑वामहे ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मरुत्वंतम् । ऋजीषिणम् । ओजस्वंतम् । विऽरप्शिनम् ।  
> इंद्रम् । गीःऽभिः । हवामहे ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मरुत्वंतं मरुद्भिस्तद्वंतम् ऋजीषिणम् । अभिषुतशेष ऋजीषः । स च तृतीयसवने पुनः सूयते । तद्वंतम् ओजस्वंतम् । ओजो नामाष्टमी दशा । शरीरवृद्ध्युपेतमित्यर्थः । विरप्शिनम् । महन्नामैतत् । महांतमेवंमहानुभावम् इंद्रं गीर्भिः स्तुतिभिः हवामहे आह्वयामः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

We call with praises Indra, who is accompanied by the Maruts and has the ऋजीष (*ṛjīṣa*), the remainder of the pressed Soma, which is pressed again at the third pressing. He is endowed with strength: strength (ओजो (*ojo*)) is the name of the eighth stage of life; the meaning is that he is endowed with bodily growth. विरप्शिनम् (*virapśinam*) is a word meaning great: Indra, so great and of such majesty.

### Printed p. 151

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मरुत्वंतं (*marutvaṃtaṃ*)—accompanied by the Maruts
- ऋजीषिणं (*ṛjīṣiṇaṃ*)—possessed of the Soma called ṛjīṣa
- ओजस्वंतं (*ojasvaṃtaṃ*)—endowed with strength
- विरप्शिनं (*virapśinaṃ*)—of great form
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- गीर्भिः (*gīrbhiḥ*)—with praises
- हवामहे (*havāmahe*)—we call, praying

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We call with praises, praying, Indra, who is accompanied by the Maruts, who has the Soma obtained from the ṛjīṣa, who is endowed with strength and of great form.

##### English translation
We invoke with our praises the mighty Indra, accompanied by the Maruts, the vigorous accepter of the residue of the oblation.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्रं॑ प्र॒त्नेन॒ मन्म॑ना म॒रुत्वं॑तं हवामहे ।  
> अ॒स्य सोम॑स्य पी॒तये॑ ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्रम् । प्रत्नेन । मन्मना । मरुत्वंतम् । हवामहे ।  
> अस्य । सोमस्य । पीतये ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मरुत्वंतम् इंद्रं प्रत्नेन पुराणेन मन्मना मननीयेन स्तोत्रेण हवामहे अस्य सोमस्य पीतये पानाय ॥ ॥ २७ ॥
> पंचमेऽहनि मरुत्वतीये ‘मरुत्वाँ इंद्र मीढ्वः' इति तृचो निविद्धानीयः । सूत्रितं च- ‘ मरुत्वाँ इंद्र मीढ्वस्तमिंद्रं वाजयामसि' (आश्व. श्रौ. ८. ८) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

We call Indra, who is accompanied by the Maruts, with an ancient and meditative hymn, to drink this Soma. On the fifth day, in the Marutvatīya, the triplet beginning मरुत्वाँ इंद्र मीढ्वः (*marutvām̐ iṃdra mīḍhvaḥ*) is to be recited among the nividdhānīya verses. It is also stated in the sūtra: मरुत्वाँ इंद्र मीढ्वस्तमिंद्रं वाजयामसि (*marutvām̐ iṃdra mīḍhvastamiṃdraṃ vājayāmasi*) (Āśv. Śrau. 8. 8).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मरुत्वंतं (*marutvaṃtaṃ*)—accompanied by the Maruts
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- प्रत्नेन (*pratnena*)—with an ancient
- मन्मना (*manmanā*)—meditative hymn
- अस्य सोमस्य (*asya somasya*)—of this Soma
- पीतये (*pītaye*)—for the drinking
- हवामहे (*havāmahe*)—we call

### Printed p. 152

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We call Indra, who is accompanied by the Maruts, with an ancient and meditative hymn, to drink this Soma.

##### English translation
With an ancient hymn we invoke Indra with the Maruts, to drink this Soma.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> म॒रुत्वाँ॑ इंद्र मीढ्वः॒ पिबा॒ सोमं॑ शतक्रतो ।  
> अ॒स्मिन्य॒ज्ञे पु॑रुष्टुत ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मरुत्वान् । इंद्र । मीढ्वः । पिब । सोमम् । शतक्रतो इति शतऽक्रतो ।  
> अस्मिन् । यज्ञे । पुरुऽस्तुत ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे मीढ्वः फलस्य वृष्टेर्वा सेक्तः शतक्रतो बहुकर्मन् इंद्र त्वं मरुत्वान् सोमं पिब अस्मिन् यज्ञे हे पुरुष्टुत बहुभिराहूत ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O showerer (mīḍhvaḥ) of fruit or of rain, O Śatakratu, of many works, Indra, accompanied by the Maruts, drink the Soma at this sacrifice. O Purustuta, invoked by many.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पुरुष्टुत (*puruṣṭuta*)—worthy of the praise of many
- मीढ्वः (*mīḍhvaḥ*)—who pours down rain
- शतक्रतो (*śatakrato*)—doer of many works
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- मरुत्वान् (*marutvān*)—you who are accompanied by the Maruts
- अस्मिन् यज्ञे (*asmin yajñe*)—at this sacrifice
- सोमं (*somaṃ*)—the Soma
- पिब (*piba*)—drink

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, worthy of the praise of many, who pours down rain, doer of many works, you who are accompanied by the Maruts, drink the Soma at this sacrifice.

### Printed p. 153

##### English translation
Indra, Satakratu, showerer (of blessings), drink the Soma at this offering, accompanied by the Maruts, O invoked of many.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

On the fifth day of the sacrifice, when the Marutvatīya śastra mantras are recited, this triplet beginning मरुत्वा इंद्र मीढ्वः (*marutvā iṃdra mīḍhvaḥ*) is to be included among the nividdhānīya mantras and recited, as is explained by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, in the sūtra मरुत्वा इंद्र मीढ्वस्तमिंद्रं वाजयामसि (*marutvā iṃdra mīḍhvastamiṃdraṃ vājayāmasi*) (Ā. 8-8).

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तुभ्येदिं॑द्र म॒रुत्व॑ते सु॒ताः सोमा॑सो अद्रिवः ।  
> हृ॒दा हू॑यंत उ॒क्थिनः॑ ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तुभ्य । इत् । इंद्र । मरुत्वते । सुताः । सोमासः । अद्रिऽवः ।  
> हृदा । हूयंते । उक्थिनः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अद्रिवः वज्रवन् इंद्र मरुत्वते तुभ्येत् तुभ्यमेव सोमासः सुताः अभिषुताः । ते च उक्थिनः शस्त्रवंतः हृदा मनसा भक्त्या हूयंते त्वदर्थम् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, wielder of the thunderbolt (adrivaḥ), accompanied by the Maruts, for you alone the Soma juices are pressed. And the reciters of recitations (ukthinaḥ), possessed of śastras, call them for you with the heart, that is, with the mind, in devotion.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- आद्रिवः (*ādrivaḥ*)—bearer of the thunderbolt weapon
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- मरुत्वते (*marutvate*)—accompanied by the Maruts
- तुभ्येत् (*tubhyet*)—for you alone
- सोमासः (*somāsaḥ*)—these Soma juices
- सुताः (*sutāḥ*)—are pressed
- उक्थिनः (*ukthinaḥ*)—these Soma juices, accompanied by praises in the form of ukthas
- हृदा (*hṛdā*)—with a heart full of devotion
- हूयंते (*hūyaṃte*)—are offered

### Printed p. 154

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, bearer of the thunderbolt weapon, for you who are accompanied by the Maruts these Soma juices are pressed. These Soma juices, accompanied by praises in the form of ukthas, are offered with a heart full of devotion.

##### English translation
Thunderer Indra, to you with the Maruts are these Soma-libations effused,—they are offered to you in faith, with recited hymns.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पिबेदिं॑द्र म॒रुत्स॑खा सु॒तं सोमं॒ दिवि॑ष्टिषु ।  
> वज्रं॒ शिशा॑न॒ ओज॑सा ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> पिब । इत् । इंद्र । मरुत्ऽसखा । सुतम् । सोमम् । दिविष्टिषु ।  
> वज्रम् । शिशानः । ओजसा ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र मरुत्सखा त्वं सुतम् अभिषुतं सोमं पिब । किमर्थम् । दिविष्टिषु अस्माकमह्नामभिगमनेषु दिवः स्वर्गस्य वैषणेषु निमित्तेषु । पीत्वा च ओजसा बलेन सोमपानजनितेन वज्रं शिशानः तीक्ष्णीकुर्वन् । शत्रूंजहीति भावः ॥
> चतुर्विंशेऽहनि प्रातःसवने ब्राह्मणाच्छंसिशस्त्रे ‘उत्तिष्ठन्' इति तृचः षळहस्तोत्रियः । सूत्रितं च - उत्तिष्ठन्नोजसा सह भिंधि विश्वा अप द्विष इति ब्राह्मणाच्छंसिनः ' (आश्व. श्रौ. ७. २) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, friend of the Maruts, drink the Soma that is pressed. For what purpose? In the diviṣṭi, that is, on the approaches of our days of sacrifice, which are the occasions for the attainment of heaven. Having drunk, sharpen your thunderbolt with the strength produced by the drinking of Soma; the meaning is: slay the enemies. On the twenty-fourth day, at the morning pressing, in the śastra of the Brāhmaṇācchaṃsin, the triplet उत्तिष्ठन् (*uttiṣṭhan*) is the ṣaḷahastotriya. It is also stated in the sūtra: उत्तिष्ठन्नोजसा सह भिंधि विश्वा अप द्विष इति ब्राह्मणाच्छंसिनः (*uttiṣṭhannojasā saha bhiṃdhi viśvā apa dviṣa iti brāhmaṇācchaṃsinaḥ*) (Āśv. Śrau. 7. 2).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- मरुत्सखा (*marutsakhā*)—you who are accompanied by the Maruts
- दिविष्टिषु (*diviṣṭiṣu*)—on the days of our sacrifice
- सुतं (*sutaṃ*)—pressed
- सोमं (*somaṃ*)—this Soma
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—for the sake of heaven
- पिब (*piba*)—drink
- ओजसा (*ojasā*)—by the strength of your Soma-drinking
- वज्रं (*vajraṃ*)—the thunderbolt weapon
- शिशानः (*śiśānaḥ*)—having made it sharp, conquer the enemies

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you who are accompanied by the Maruts, drink for the sake of heaven this Soma, pressed and made ready on the days of our sacrifice. Having made the thunderbolt weapon sharp by the strength of your Soma-drinking, conquer the enemies.

### Printed p. 155

##### English translation
Drink, Indra, with your friends the Maruts, this Soma, effused on the recurring sacred days, and sharpen your thunderbolt with ( renewed ) vigour.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒त्तिष्ठ॒न्नोज॑सा स॒ह पी॒त्वी शिप्रे॑ अवेपयः ।  
> सोम॑मिंद्र च॒मू सु॒तम् ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत्ऽतिष्ठन् । ओजसा । सह । पीत्वी । शिप्रे इति । अवेपयः ।  
> सोमम् । इंद्र । चमू इति । सुतम् ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वं पीत्वी पीत्वा ओजसा बलेन सह उत्तिष्ठन् शिप्रे हनू अवेपयः अकंपयः । मदावेशादिति भावः । किं पीत्वा । चमू चम्वोरधिषवणफलकयोः सुतं सोमम् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, having drunk, rising up together with strength, you shook your two jaws (śipre); that is, because of the intoxication. What did you drink? The Soma pressed in the two camū, that is, on the two pressing boards.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- चमू (*camū*)—in the camū vessels
- सुतं (*sutaṃ*)—pressed and offered
- सोमं (*somaṃ*)—the Soma
- पीत्वी (*pītvī*)—having drunk
- ओजसा सह (*ojasā saha*)—together with that strength
- उत्तिष्ठन् (*uttiṣṭhan*)—rising up
- शिप्रे (*śipre*)—your cheeks
- अवेपयः (*avepayaḥ*)—you shook

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, having drunk the Soma pressed and offered in the camū vessels, rising up together with that strength, you shook your cheeks.

### Printed p. 156

##### English translation
Rising up in your strength, Indra, you shook your jaws, when you had quaffed the Soma pressed between the two boards.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

On the day of the sacrifice called the twenty-fourth, at the time of the morning pressing, in the śastra mantras recited by the priest called Brāhmaṇācchaṃsin, the triplet beginning उत्तिष्ठन् (*uttiṣṭhan*) is to be recited as the ṣaḷahastotriya triplet, as is explained by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, in the sūtra उत्तिष्ठन्नोजसा सह भिंधि विश्वा अप द्विष इति ब्राह्मणाच्छंसिनः (*uttiṣṭhannojasā saha bhiṃdhi viśvā apa dviṣa iti brāhmaṇācchaṃsinaḥ*) (A. 7-2).**NOTE**

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अनु॑ त्वा॒ रोद॑सी उ॒भे क्रक्ष॑माणमकृपेताम् ।  
> इंद्र॒ यद्द॑स्यु॒हाभ॑वः ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अनु । त्वा । रोदसी इति । उभे इति । क्रक्षमाणम् । अकृपेताम् ।  
> इंद्र । यत् । दस्युऽहा । अभवः ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र क्रक्षमाणं शत्रून् विलिखंतं त्वा त्वाम् उभे रोदसी उभे अपि द्यावापृथिव्यौ अनु अकृपेताम् अनुकल्पयेताम् । यत् यदा दस्युहा अभवः भवसि तदा ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, when you become the slayer of the Dasyus, both heaven and earth, both Rodasī, followed you, who were tearing the enemies apart (krakṣamāṇam), and gave their consent.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- यत् (*yat*)—when
- दस्युहा (*dasyuhā*)—as the destroyer of enemies
- अभवः (*abhavaḥ*)—you become, then
- क्रक्षमाणं (*krakṣamāṇaṃ*)—tearing the enemies apart
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- उभे रोदसी (*ubhe rodasī*)—both heaven and earth
- अनु अकृपेतां (*anu akṛpetāṃ*)—may they follow

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, when you become the destroyer of enemies, then may both heaven and earth follow you, who tear the enemies apart.

### Printed p. 157

##### English translation
Let heaven and earth follow you Indra, as you smite; when you beat down the Dasyu.

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वाच॑म॒ष्टाप॑दीम॒हं नव॑स्रक्तिमृत॒स्पृश॑म् ।  
> इंद्रा॒त्परि॑ त॒न्वं॑ ममे ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वाचम् । अष्टाऽपदीम् । अहम् । नवऽस्रक्तिम् । ऋतऽस्पृशम् ।  
> इंद्रात् । परि । तन्वम् । ममे ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अष्टापदीम् । अष्टाभिर्दिग्भिर्विदिग्भिः साष्टापदी । नवस्रक्तिम् उपरि स्थितेनादित्येन नवस्रक्तिम् । आसु दिक्षु व्याप्तमित्यर्थः । ऋतस्पृशं यज्ञस्पृशं वाचं स्तुतिम् अहं परिपूर्णत् इंद्रात् तन्वं तनुं न्यूनां सती परि ममे । अन्यूनेयत्तां करोमीत्यर्थः । कार्येस्न्  न स्वरूपं स्तुत्या विषयीकर्तुमशक्यत्वादिति भावः ॥ ॥ २८ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The speech (vāc) is ashṭāpadī, "eight-footed", because it is accompanied by the eight directions and intermediate directions; it is navasrakti, "nine-edged", because it is pervaded by the sun standing above; the meaning is that it extends over these directions. I measure out this speech, which touches the sacrifice (ṛta), that is, the hymn of praise, as being less than the full Indra; the meaning is: I make it of a measure that falls short. The sense is that his own nature cannot be made the object of praise by a hymn.**NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अष्टापदीं (*aṣṭāpadīṃ*)—pervading the eight directions and intermediate directions
- नवस्रक्तिं (*navasraktiṃ*)—even though it is to pervade also the ninth, the solar orb
- ऋतस्पृशं (*ṛtaspṛśaṃ*)—connected with the sacrifice
- वाचं (*vācaṃ*)—my hymn
- अहं (*ahaṃ*)—I
- इंद्रात् (*iṃdrāt*)—than the perfect Indra
- तस्नं (*tasnaṃ*)—as lacking
- परि ममे (*pari mame*)—I regard

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Even though this hymn of mine, connected with the sacrifice, pervades the eight directions and intermediate directions, and pervades also the ninth, the solar orb, I regard it as unable to praise fully the complete excellence of the perfect Indra.

### Printed p. 158

##### English translation
I make this sacrificial hymn, reaching to the eight points (of the sky) and rising to a ninth (the sun in the zenith), though it is less than ( the dimensions of) Indra.

## Sūkta 77 (Maṇḍala 8)
