> हे सोम पीतस्त्वं नः अस्मान् मा सं वीविजः । चलितांगान् मा कार्षीः । हे राजन् सोम अस्मान् मा वि बीभिषथाः भीतान् मा कुरु । नः अस्माकं हार्दि हृदयं त्विषा दीप्त्या मा वधीः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Soma, having been drunk, do not make us tremble (मा सं वीविजः (*mā saṃ vīvijaḥ*)), that is, do not make our limbs shaky. O king Soma, do not frighten us (मा वि बीभिषथाः (*mā vi bībhiṣathāḥ*)), do not make us afraid. Do not smite (मा वधीः (*mā vadhīḥ*)) our heart (हार्दि (*hārdi*)) with your splendour (त्विषा (*tviṣā*), brightness).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सोम (*soma*)—O god Soma, drunk by us
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- मा सं वीविजः (*mā saṃ vīvijaḥ*)—do not make us quake and tremble
- राजन् (*rājan*)—O lordly Soma
- मा वि बीभिषथाः (*mā vi bībhiṣathāḥ*)—do not frighten
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- हार्दि (*hārdi*)—heart
- त्विषा (*tviṣā*)—by your fierce radiance
- मा वधीः (*mā vadhīḥ*)—do not make it burn

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O god Soma, drunk by us, do not make us quake and tremble. O lordly Soma, do not frighten us; do not make our heart burn by your fierce radiance.

##### English translation
O Soma, cause us not to tremble, frighten us not, O king ; smite not our hearts with your brightness

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अव॒ यत्स्वे स॒धस्थे॑ दे॒वानां॑ दुर्म॒तीरीक्षे॑ ।  
> राज॒न्नप॒ द्विषः॑ सेध॒ मीढ्वो॒ अप॒ स्रिधः॑ सेध ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अव । यत् । स्वे । सधऽस्थे । देवानाम् । दुःऽमतीः । ईक्षे ।  
> राजन् । अप । द्विषः । सेध । मीढ्वः । अप । स्रिधः । सेध ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> स्वे सधस्थे स्वकीये सहस्थाने गृहे देवानां दुर्मतीः दुर्मतयो न प्रविशंत्विति । यत् यदा अव ईक्षे अहं त्वं वेक्षसे तदा हे राजन् द्विषः अस्मद्वेप्ष्टॄन अप सेध। हे मीड्वः सोमरसस्य सेक्तः स्रिधः हिंसकान् अप सेध । हिंधीत्यर्थः ॥ ॥ ३४ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In my own (स्वे (*sve*)) seat, that is, in my own house, so that the evil-minded thoughts (दुर्मतीः (*durmatīḥ*)) of the gods may not enter, when I watch (ईक्षे (*īkṣe*)), then, O king, drive away the haters (द्विषः (*dviṣaḥ*)), those who hate us. O showerer (मीढ्वः (*mīḍhvaḥ*)), pourer of the Soma juice, drive away the harmers (स्रिधः (*sridhaḥ*)). **NOTE**

### Printed p. 196

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- राजन् (*rājan*)—O lordly god Soma
- स्वे (*sve*)—belonging to me
- सदस्थे (*sadasthe*)—in the house
- देवानां (*devānāṃ*)—towards the gods
- दुर्मतीः (*durmatīḥ*)—so that those of evil mind may not enter
- यत् (*yat*)—when
- अवेक्षे (*avekṣe*)—I keep watch, then
- द्विषः (*dviṣaḥ*)—such enemies
- अप सेध (*apa sedha*)—drive far away
- मीढ्वः (*mīḍhvaḥ*)—O Soma, who pour out desired objects
- स्रिधः (*sridhaḥ*)—such harmers
- अप सेध (*apa sedha*)—drive far away

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O lordly god Soma, when I keep watch so that those of evil mind who slight the gods may not enter my house, then drive such enemies far away. O Soma, who pour out desired objects, drive such harmers far away.

##### English translation
When in my house I watch against the enemies of the gods, then, O king drive away those who hate us,—O showerer of blessings, drive away those who would harm us.

## Sūkta 80 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ‘नह्यन्यम्' इति दशर्चमेकादशं सूक्तम् । अत्रेयमनुक्रमणिका--' न ह्यन्यं दशैकद्यूर्नौधसो गायत्रेऽंत्या दैवी त्रिष्टुप्' इति । एकद्यूर्नाम नोधसः पुत्र ऋषिः । अंत्या त्रिष्टुप् । सा च देवदेवत्या शिष्टा गायत्र्य ऐंद्र्यः । द्वितीये पर्याये मैत्रावरुणशस्त्र आदितोऽष्टर्चः । सूत्रितं च - ' नह्यन्यं बळाकरमित्यष्टौ ' (आश्व. श्रौ. ६. ४) इति । महाव्रते निष्केवल्ये गायत्रतृचाशीतावाद्या विनियुक्ता । तथा च पंचमारण्यके शौनकः -' नह्यन्यं बळाकरमित्येतां प्रत्यवदधाति' (ऐ. आ. ५, २. ३) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning नह्यन्यं (*nahyanyaṃ*) has ten Ṛks and is the eleventh sūkta. The Anukramaṇikā says: न ह्यन्यं दशैकद्यूर्नौधसो गायत्रेऽंत्या दैवी त्रिष्टुप् (*na hyanyaṃ daśaikadyūrnaudhaso gāyatre 'ṃtyā daivī triṣṭup*). Ekadyū is the name of the seer, a son of Nodhas. The last Ṛk is in Triṣṭubh, and it has the Devas as its deity; the remaining Ṛks are Gāyatrī, addressed to Indra. In the second paryāya, in the Maitrāvaruṇa śastra, the first eight Ṛks are used. This is stated in the sūtra: नह्यन्यं बळाकरमित्यष्टौ (*nahyanyaṃ baḷākaramityaṣṭau*) (Āśv. Śrau. 6. 4). In the Mahāvrata, in the Niṣkevalya, the first of the Gāyatra tṛca is employed. **NOTE** So also Śaunaka in the Fifth Āraṇyaka: नह्यन्यं बळाकरमित्येतां प्रत्यवदधाति (*nahyanyaṃ baḷākaramityetāṃ pratyavadadhāti*) (Ai. Ā. 5, 2. 3) **GLOSS**

This sūkta, beginning नह्यन्यं (*nahyanyaṃ*), is the eleventh sūkta in the eighth Anuvāka. It has ten Ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: नह्यन्यं दशैकद्यूर्नौधसो गायत्रेऽन्त्या दैवी त्रिष्टुप् (*nahyanyaṃ daśaikadyūrnaudhaso gāyatre 'ntyā daivī triṣṭup*). The seer of this sūkta is Ekadyū, the son of the seer Nodhas; Indra is the deity; for the tenth Ṛk the Devas are the deity;

### Printed p. 197

the last Ṛk of this sūkta is in Triṣṭubh metre. The remaining Ṛks are in Gāyatrī metre. The use of the first eight Ṛks of this sūkta for the śastra mantras recited by the priest called Maitrāvaruṇa in the second paryāya is explained by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra in the sūtra नह्यन्यं बळाकरमित्यष्टौ (*nahyanyaṃ baḷākaramityaṣṭau*) (Ā. Sū. 6-4). The use of the first Ṛk of this sūkta for the Gāyatrī-metre Ṛks, when the Niṣkevalya śastra mantras are recited in the Mahāvratesṭi, is indicated by the sage Śaunaka in the Fifth Āraṇyaka of the Aitareya Āraṇyaka by the sentence नह्यन्यं बळाकरमित्येतां प्रत्यवदधाति (*nahyanyaṃ baḷākaramityetāṃ pratyavadadhāti*) (Ai. Ā. 5-2-3).

Sūkta 80

Maṇḍala 8, Anuvāka 8, Sūkta 80

Aṣṭaka 6, Adhyāya 5, Varga 35, 36

Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 10

Seer: Ekadyūrnaudhasaḥ

Deity: Ṛks 1-9 Indra; Ṛk 10 the Devas

Metre: Ṛks 1-9 Gāyatrī; Ṛk 10 Triṣṭubh

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न॒ह्य१॒॑न्यं ब॒ळाक॑रं मर्डि॒तारं॑ शतक्रतो ।  
> त्वं न॑ इंद्र मृळय ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नहि । अन्यम् । बळा । अकरम् । मर्डितारम् । शतक्रतो इति शतऽक्रतो ।  
> त्वम् । नः । इंद्र । मृळय ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे शतक्रतो त्वत्तः अन्यं मर्ङितारं सुखयितारं बळा बट् सत्यं न अकरं न करोमि । तस्माद्धे इंद्र त्वं नः अस्मान् मृळय ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Śatakratu, I do not make any other than you a comforter, a giver of happiness (बळा (*baḷā*) means truth). Therefore, O Indra, make us happy.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शतक्रतो (*śatakrato*)—O Indra, doer of many deeds
- अन्यं (*anyaṃ*)—other than you
- मर्डितारं (*marḍitāraṃ*)—one who gives us happiness
- बळा (*baḷā*)—truly
- न अकरं (*na akaraṃ*)—I cannot see **NOTE**
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- मृळय (*mṛḷaya*)—make happy

### Printed p. 198

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, doer of many deeds, other than you, one who gives us happiness, I cannot truly see. O Indra, make us happy.

##### English translation
Other then you, Satakratu, I know no bestower of happiness ; Indra, you make us happy.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यो नः॒ शश्व॑त्पु॒रावि॒थामृ॑ध्रो॒ वाज॑सातये ।  
> स त्वं न॑ इंद्र मृळय ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । नः । शश्वत् । पुरा । आविथ । अमृध्रः । वाजऽसातये ।  
> सः । त्वम् । नः । इंद्र । मृळय ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यः अमृधः अहिंसकः नः अस्मान् वाजसातये अन्नलाभाय पुरा पूर्वम् आविथ रक्षितवान् । हे इंद्र स त्वं नः अस्मान् शश्वत् सदा मृळय सुखय ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

You who are harmless (अमृधः (*amṛdhaḥ*)) and have protected (आविथ (*āvitha*)) us before, for the gain of food (वाजसातये (*vājasātaye*)), O Indra, being that same one, make us happy (मृळय (*mṛḷaya*)) always (शश्वत् (*śaśvat*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- अमृध्रः (*amṛdhraḥ*)—harmless
- यः (*yaḥ*)—you who
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- वाजसातये (*vājasātaye*)—for the gain of food
- पुरा (*purā*)—in former times
- आविथ (*āvitha*)—have protected
- सः (*saḥ*)—such
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- शश्वत् (*śaśvat*)—always
- मृळय (*mṛḷaya*)—make happy

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you, the harmless one, who have protected us in former times for the gain of food, make us happy always.

##### English translation
O you, the invulnerable, who have always in former times protected us for the battle, you **NOTE**, Indra, make us happy.

### Printed p. 199
#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> किमं॒ग र॑ध्र॒चोद॑नः सुन्वा॒नस्या॑वि॒तेद॑सि ।  
> कु॒वित्स्विं॑द्र णः॒ शकः॑ ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> किम् । अंग । रध्रऽचोदनः । सुन्वानस्य । अविता । इत् । असि ।  
> कुवित् । सु । इंद्र । नः । शकः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वं रध्रचोदनः । रध्रं राधकं चोदयतीति रध्रचोदनः । तादृशस्त्वं सुन्वानस्य अवितेदसि रक्षक एव भवसि । अतः नः अस्माकं कुवित् बहु सु सुष्ठु शकः अशकः शक्तो भव। बहु धनं कुर्वित्वर्थः । अस्मान् वा बहु कुर्विति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, you are रध्रचोदनः (*radhracodanaḥ*): he who impels the one who accomplishes (रध्रं राधकं चोदयतीति (*radhraṃ rādhakaṃ codayatīti*)). Being such, you are surely the protector of the one who presses Soma. Therefore, for us, become exceedingly (कुवित् (*kuvit*), सु (*su*)) capable; the meaning is: make much wealth, or make us many. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- रध्रचोदनः (*radhracodanaḥ*)—you, who are the stimulator of your worshipper
- सुन्वानस्य (*sunvānasya*)—of him who presses and offers Soma to you
- अंग (*aṃga*)—surely
- अविता इत् असि (*avitā it asi*)—you are indeed the protector
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- कुवित् (*kuvit*)—abundantly
- सु (*su*)—exceedingly
- शकः (*śakaḥ*)—become a powerful helper

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you who stimulate your worship, are surely the protector of him who presses Soma and offers it to you. Become to us a helper, abundantly and exceedingly powerful.

##### English translation
Director of the worshipper, you are the guardian of the offerer; help us mightily.
#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्र॒ प्र णो॒ रथ॑मव प॒श्चाच्चि॒त्संत॑मद्रिवः ।  
> पु॒रस्ता॑देनं मे कृधि ॥ ४ ॥

### Printed p. 200

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्र । प्र । नः । रथम् । अव । पश्चात् । चित् । संतम् । अद्रिऽवः ।  
> पुरस्तात् । एनम् । मे । कृधि ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र नः रथं प्र अव प्ररक्ष । कीदृशं रथम् । पश्चाच्चित्संतम् । चिदप्यर्थे । अस्मत्समानरथानां पश्चाद्भूतमपि एनं मे रथं हे अद्रिवः वज्रवन्निंद्र पुरस्तात् वर्तमानं कृधि कुरु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, protect our chariot fully (प्र अव प्ररक्ष (*pra ava prarakṣa*)). What kind of chariot? One that is behind (पश्चाच्चित्संतम् (*paścāccitsaṃtam*)); here चित् (*cit*) is in the sense of "even". Even though it is behind chariots equal to ours, this chariot of mine, O Indra, wielder of the thunderbolt (अद्रिवः वज्रवन् (*adrivaḥ vajravan*)), make present in front (पुरस्तात् वर्तमानं कृधि कुरु (*purastāt vartamānaṃ kṛdhi kuru*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- पश्चाच्चित्संतं (*paścāccitsaṃtaṃ*)—even though fallen far behind
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- रथं (*rathaṃ*)—chariot
- प्र अव (*pra ava*)—protect fully
- अद्रिवः (*adrivaḥ*)—O Indra, bearer of the thunderbolt
- मे (*me*)—my
- एनं (*enaṃ*)—this chariot
- पुरस्तात् (*purastāt*)—as being ahead of the other chariots
- कृधि (*kṛdhi*)—make

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, even though our chariot has fallen far behind the other chariots, protect our chariot fully. O Indra, bearer of the thunderbolt, make this chariot of mine to be ahead of the other chariots.

##### English translation
Indra, protect our chariot; though now left behind, set it in the front, O thunderer.
#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> हंतो॒ नु किमा॑ससे प्रथ॒मं नो॒ रथं॑ कृधि ।  
> उ॒प॒मं वा॑ज॒यु श्रवः॑ ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> हंतो इति । नु । किम् । आससे । प्रथमम् । नः । रथम् । कृधि ।  
> उपऽमम् । वाजऽयु । श्रवः ॥ ५ ॥

### Printed p. 201

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हंतेत्येतदादि मुख्यकृदामंत्रितेन समानम् । हंत इंद्र नु इदानीं किं त्वं तूष्णीम् आससे । तत्र किं करोमीति चेदुच्यते । नः रथं प्रथमं सर्वेषां मुख्यं कृधि कुरु । वाजयु अस्माकममिच्छत् श्रवः अन्नं हविर्लक्षणम् उपमम् । अंतिकनामैतत् । तवांतिकभूतं वर्तत इति शेषः । यस्मादेवं तस्माद्रथमस्मदीयं प्रथमं कृधीति ॥ ॥ ३५ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The verse beginning with हंत (*haṃta*) is introduced by Sāyaṇa as the same as a vocative construction. **NOTE** "Come now, O Indra, why do you sit silent now?" If it is asked what he should do, it is said: make our chariot first, the foremost of all (प्रथमं सर्वेषां मुख्यं कृधि कुरु (*prathamaṃ sarveṣāṃ mukhyaṃ kṛdhi kuru*)). The श्रवः (*śravaḥ*), the food in the form of an oblation, desiring us (वाजयु अस्माकममिच्छत् (*vājayu asmākamamicchat*)), is उपमम् (*upamam*); this is a word meaning "near": it is near you, this being the word to be supplied. Since it is so, make our chariot first.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हंतो (*haṃto*)—O Indra
- नु (*nu*)—now
- किं (*kiṃ*)—why
- आससे (*āsase*)—do you sit silent?
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- रथं (*rathaṃ*)—chariot
- प्रथमं (*prathamaṃ*)—as the foremost among the other chariots
- कृधि (*kṛdhi*)—make
- वाजयुं (*vājayuṃ*)—desiring food for us
- श्रवः (*śravaḥ*)—this food in the form of an oblation
- उपमं (*upamaṃ*)—is quite near you

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, why are you still silent? Make our chariot the best (the foremost) of all the other chariots. This food in the form of an oblation, desiring food for us, is near you.

##### English translation
Up, why do you sit still? Make our chariot the first; our food-seeking offering is near you.
#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अवा॑ नो वाज॒युं रथं॑ सु॒करं॑ ते॒ किमित्परि॑ ।  
> अ॒स्मान्सु जि॒ग्युष॑स्कृधि ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अव । नः । वाजऽयुम् । रथम् । सुऽकरम् । ते । किम् । इत् । परि ।  
> अस्मान् । सु । जिग्युषः । कृधि ॥ ६ ॥

### Printed p. 202

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र नः वाजयुम् अन्नेच्छुं रथम् अव रक्ष संग्रामे । ते तव किमित् किमपि सर्वकर्तव्यजातं परि परितः सुकरं सुखेन कर्तव्यम्। तव कर्तुमशक्यं न किंचिदस्ति । यस्मादेवं तस्मात् सु जिग्युषः सुष्ठु जेतॄन् कुरु संग्रामे ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, protect in battle our chariot, which desires food (वाजयुम् अन्नेच्छुं (*vājayum annecchuṃ*)). Whatever is to be done by you, all of it, on every side (परि परितः (*pari paritaḥ*)), is easy, to be done with ease (सुकरं सुखेन कर्तव्यम् (*sukaraṃ sukhena kartavyam*)); there is nothing that you cannot do. Since it is so, make us well victorious (सु जिग्युषः सुष्ठु जेतॄन् (*su jigyuṣaḥ suṣṭhu jetṝn*)) in battle.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra) नः (*naḥ*)—our
- वाजयुं (*vājayuṃ*)—desiring food
- रथं (*rathaṃ*)—chariot
- अव (*ava*)—protect
- परि (*pari*)—on all sides
- ते (*te*)—to you
- किं इत् (*kiṃ it*)—every task
- सुकरं (*sukaraṃ*)—is easy
- अस्मान् (*asmān*)—us
- सु (*su*)—exceedingly
- जिग्युषः (*jigyuṣaḥ*)—so that we may be victorious
- कृधि (*kṛdhi*)—make

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, protect our chariot, which desires food. All tasks are easy for you in all places. Make us excellent and victorious.

##### English translation
Protect our food-seeking chariot; everything is easy for you to do; make us completely victorious.
#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्र॒ दृह्य॑स्व॒ पूर॑सि भ॒द्रा त॑ एति निष्कृ॒तम् ।  
> इ॒यं धीरृ॒त्विया॑वती ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्र । दृह्यस्व । पूः । असि । भद्रा । ते । एति । निःऽकृतम् ।  
> इयम् । धीः । ऋत्वियऽवती ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वं दृह्यस्व दृढो भव संग्रामे । त्वं पूरसि पुरमसि । यथा पुरमविचलितं तद्वत्त्वमसि । अथवा। अस्मदीये यज्ञे दृढो भव । त्वं पुनरन्ययज्ञजिगमिषुर्मा भूः । त्वं पूः पूरकः कामानामसि । किमत्र विद्यत इति चेदुच्यते । निष्कृतं निष्कर्तारं ते त्वां भद्रा कल्याणी इयं धीः स्तुतिः क्रिया वा ऋत्वियावती । ऋतुशब्दः कालोपलक्षकः । स्वकालोपेता सती एति गच्छति । यद्वा । ते निष्कृतं स्थानमेति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, be firm (दृह्यस्व दृढो भव (*dṛhyasva dṛḍho bhava*)) in battle. You are पू (*pū*), a city (पुरमसि (*puramasi*)); as a city is unshaken, so are you. Or else: be firm at our sacrifice, and do not wish to go to another sacrifice. You are पूः पूरकः कामानामसि (*pūḥ pūrakaḥ kāmānāmasi*), the fulfiller of desires. If it is asked what there is here: to you, the one who sets right (निष्कृतं निष्कर्तारं (*niṣkṛtaṃ niṣkartāraṃ*)), this auspicious (भद्रा कल्याणी (*bhadrā kalyāṇī*)) धीः (*dhīḥ*), a hymn of praise or a rite, comes (एति गच्छति (*eti gacchati*)), being furnished with its proper season (स्वकालोपेता सती (*svakālopetā satī*)); the word ऋतु (*ṛtu*) indicates time. Or else: it comes to your place (ते निष्कृतं स्थानमेति (*te niṣkṛtaṃ sthānameti*)).
### Printed p. 203

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- पूः (*pūḥ*)—like a city with a fort
- असि (*asi*)—you are firm
- दृह्यस्व (*dṛhyasva*)—stand unshaken in battle
- निष्कृतं (*niṣkṛtaṃ*)—who is seen as rejecting the enemies
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- ऋत्वियावती (*ṛtviyāvatī*)—that which is to be ... in due season, **NOTE** and
- भद्रा (*bhadrā*)—auspicious
- इयं धीः (*iyaṃ dhīḥ*)—this hymn of praise
- एति (*eti*)—approaches

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you are firm like a city with a fort. Stand unshaken in battle. This hymn of praise, auspicious and **NOTE** in due season, approaches you, who are seen rejecting the enemies.

##### English translation
Indra, be firm (in battle), you are (strong as) a city; to you, the repeller (of enemies), comes this auspicious sacrifice, offered in due season.
#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> मा सी॑मव॒द्य आ भा॑गु॒र्वी काष्ठा॑ हि॒तं धन॑म् ।  
> अ॒पावृ॑क्ता अर॒त्नयः॑ ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मा । सीम् । अवद्ये । आ । भाक् । उर्वी । काष्ठा । हितम् । धनम् ।  
> अपऽआवृक्ताः । अरत्नयः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मा अस्मान् सीं सर्वतः अवद्यः निंदा आ भाक् माभजतु । प्राप्नोतु न कुतश्चित् । पापरहितान् कुर्वित्यर्थः। किंच उर्वी काष्ठा बह्वंतरालमाज्यंतः । ‘आज्यंतोऽपि काष्ठोच्यते क्रांत्वा स्थिता भवति' (निरु. २. १५) इति यास्कः । तत्र हितं निहितं शत्रुसंबंधि धनम् अस्माकं भवत्वित्यर्थः । अरत्नयः अरममाणाः शत्रवः अपावृक्ताः संत्विति शेषः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

May reproach (अवद्यः निंदा (*avadyaḥ niṃdā*)) not come upon us from any side (सीं सर्वतः (*sīṃ sarvataḥ*)); the meaning is: make us free of sin. Further, उर्वी काष्ठा (*urvī kāṣṭhā*) is the wide space, the end of the course (बह्वंतरालमाज्यंतः (*bahvaṃtarālamājyaṃtaḥ*)). Yāska says (Ni. 2. 15): आज्यंतोऽपि काष्ठोच्यते क्रांत्वा स्थिता भवति (*ājyaṃto 'pi kāṣṭhocyate krāṃtvā sthitā bhavati*) **GLOSS**. There the wealth belonging to the enemy that is stored (हितं निहितं शत्रुसंबंधि धनम् (*hitaṃ nihitaṃ śatrusaṃbaṃdhi dhanam*)) is to be ours. The enemies, अरत्नयः अरममाणाः (*aratnayaḥ aramamāṇāḥ*), are to be driven away (अपावृक्ताः (*apāvṛktāḥ*)).
### Printed p. 204

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सीं (*sīṃ*)—everywhere
- अवद्यः (*avadyaḥ*)—reproach
- मा आ भाक् (*mā ā bhāk*)—may it not approach us
- काष्ठा (*kāṣṭhā*)—the final goal of the battle
- उर्वी (*urvī*)—is still very far away
- हितं (*hitaṃ*)—that which is in the enemies
- धनं (*dhanaṃ*)—wealth, may it come to us
- अरत्नयः (*aratnayaḥ*)—enemies who cause sorrow
- अपावृक्ताः (*apāvṛktāḥ*)—may they go far away

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May reproach not approach us from any side. The final goal of the battle is still very far from us. May the wealth that is in the enemies come to us. May our enemies, who cause sorrow, go far away.

##### English translation
Let not reproach reach us; far off is the goal; there is the wealth stored; may our enemies be excluded.
#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तु॒रीयं॒ नाम॑ य॒ज्ञियं॑ य॒दा कर॒स्तदु॑श्मसि ।  
> आदित्पति॑र्न ओहसे ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तुरीयम् । नाम । यज्ञियम् । यदा । करः । तत् । उश्मसि ।  
> आत् । इत् । पतिः । नः । ओहसे ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वं यज्ञियं यज्ञसंबंधि तुरीयं चतुर्थं नाम यदा करः करोषि तदुश्मसि कामयामहे । आदित् अनंतरमेव नामकामानंतरमेव पतिः पालकस्त्वं नः अस्मान् ओहसे वहसि प्रापयसि । नक्षत्रनाम गुह्यं नाम प्रकाशं नामेति त्रीणि नामानि सोमयाजीति तुरीयं नाम तच्च यज्ञियम् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, when you make (करः करोषि (*karaḥ karoṣi*)) the fourth (तुरीयं चतुर्थं (*turīyaṃ caturthaṃ*)) name (नाम (*nāma*)) relating to the sacrifice (यज्ञियं यज्ञसंबंधि (*yajñiyaṃ yajñasaṃbaṃdhi*)), that we desire (तदुश्मसि कामयामहे (*taduśmasi kāmayāmahe*)). Immediately afterwards, after the desire for the name, you, the protector (पतिः पालकस्त्वं (*patiḥ pālakastvaṃ*)), carry us (ओहसे वहसि प्रापयसि (*ohase vahasi prāpayasi*)), bring us to our goal. There are three names: नक्षत्रनाम गुह्यं नाम प्रकाशं नाम (*nakṣatranāma guhyaṃ nāma prakāśaṃ nāma*); the fourth name is that of the Soma-sacrificer (सोमयाजीति तुरीयं नाम (*somayājīti turīyaṃ nāma*)), and that is the one relating to the sacrifice.

### Printed p. 205

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra) यज्ञियं (*yajñiyaṃ*)—that which relates to the sacrifice
- तुरीयं (*turīyaṃ*)—the fourth, called Somayājin
- नाम (*nāma*)—name
- यदा (*yadā*)—when
- करः (*karaḥ*)—you assume
- तत् (*tat*)—then that
- उश्मसि (*uśmasi*)—we desire
- आत् इत् (*āt it*)—immediately after that
- पतिः (*patiḥ*)—you, the protector
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- ओहसे (*ohase*)—you help us to attain our desired end

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, the name that relates to the sacrifice, the fourth among the four names, Nakṣatra-nāma, Guhya-nāma, Prakāśa-nāma and Somayājī, namely the fourth name Somayājī: whenever you assume it, we desire it. Immediately after that, you, the protector, help us to attain our desired end.

##### English translation
When you assume your sacrificial fourth name, we long for it; then you forthwith carry us as a protector.
#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अवी॑वृधद्वो अमृता॒ अमं॑दीदेक॒द्यूर्दे॑वा उ॒त याश्च॑ देवीः ।  
> तस्मा॑ उ॒ राधः॑ कृणुत प्रश॒स्तं प्रा॒तर्म॒क्षू धि॒याव॑सुर्जगम्यात् ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अवीवृधत् । वः । अमृताः । अमंदीत् । एकऽद्यूः । देवाः । उत । याः । च । देवीः ।  
> तस्मै । ऊं इति । राधः । कृणुत । प्रऽशस्तम् । प्रातः । मक्षु । धियाऽवसुः । जगम्यात् ॥ १० ॥

### Printed p. 206

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> इयं वैश्वदेवी । हे देवाः हे अमृताः अमरणाः वः युष्मानयम् अवीवृधत् वर्धयति स्तुत्या अमंदीत् तर्पयति सोमेन एकद्यूः ऋषिरहम् । उत अपि च हे देवीः देव्यो देवपत्न्यः याश्च यूयं स्थ युष्मानप्यवीवृधदमंदीच्च । तस्मै राधः धनं प्रशस्तं प्रवृद्धं कृणुत कुरुन । उ इति पूरण एवकारार्थो वा । प्रातः प्रातरेव मक्षु क्षिप्रं धियावसुः कर्मधन इंद्रः जगम्यात् आगच्छतु । इंद्रस्य देवस्वामित्वादाधिक्यद्योतनाय पुनरभिधानम् ॥ ॥ ३६ ॥ ॥ ८ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Ṛk is addressed to the Viśvedevas (इयं वैश्वदेवी (*iyaṃ vaiśvadevī*)). O gods, O immortals (अमृताः अमरणाः (*amṛtāḥ amaraṇāḥ*)), this seer, Ekadyu, has made you grow by praise (अवीवृधत् वर्धयति स्तुत्या (*avīvṛdhat vardhayati stutyā*)) and has gratified you with Soma (अमंदीत् तर्पयति सोमेन (*amaṃdīt tarpayati somena*)). Also, O goddesses, the wives of the gods, whoever you are, he has made you too grow and has gratified you. For him make wealth, राधः धनं (*rādhaḥ dhanaṃ*), excellent and abundant (प्रशस्तं प्रवृद्धं (*praśastaṃ pravṛddhaṃ*)). The word उ (*u*) is a filler or has the sense of "indeed". May Indra, धियावसुः कर्मधन (*dhiyāvasuḥ karmadhana*), whose wealth is rite, come quickly, in the very morning (प्रातः प्रातरेव मक्षु क्षिप्रं (*prātaḥ prātareva makṣu kṣipraṃ*)). Indra is named again to show his pre-eminence, since he is lord of the gods (इंद्रस्य देवस्वामित्वादाधिक्यद्योतनाय पुनरभिधानम् (*iṃdrasya devasvāmitvādādhikyadyotanāya punarabhidhānam*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवाः (*devāḥ*)—O gods
- उत (*uta*)—and
- याः च देवीः (*yāḥ ca devīḥ*)—O goddesses, wives of the gods, whoever you are
- अमृताः (*amṛtāḥ*)—free from the nature of death
- वः (*vaḥ*)—you
- एकद्युः (*ekadyuḥ*)—the seer named Ekadyu
- अवीवृधं (*avīvṛdhaṃ*)—has made you grow by praise and the like
- अमंदीत् (*amaṃdīt*)—has also made you gratified by offerings of Soma and the rest
- तस्मै (*tasmai*)—for him
- राधः (*rādhaḥ*)—wealth
- प्रशस्तं (*praśastaṃ*)—so that it grows excellently
- कृणुत (*kṛṇuta*)—make
- धियावसुः (*dhiyāvasuḥ*)—Indra, whose wealth is rite
- मक्षु (*makṣu*)—quickly
- प्रातः (*prātaḥ*)—in the morning itself
- जगम्यात् (*jagamyāt*)—may he come

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O gods and goddesses, the seer named Ekadyu has made you, free from death, grow by praise and the like. He has also made you gratified by offerings of Soma and the rest. Make his wealth grow excellently. May Indra, whose wealth is rite, come quickly in the very morning.

##### English translation
O immortal gods and all you goddesses, Ekadyu has honoured you (with his praise) and rejoiced you (with his Soma-offerings); make his substance abundant; and may (Indra), who rewards pious acts with wealth, come speedily in the morning.
### Printed p. 207
## Sūkta 81 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> नवमेऽनुवाके त्रयोदश सूक्तानि । तत्र ‘आ तू न इंद्र' इति नवर्चं प्रथमं सूक्तं कण्वपुत्रस्य कुसीदिन आर्षं गायत्रमैंद्रम् । तथा चानुक्रम्यते--- आ तू नो नव कुसीदी काण्वः' इति । महाव्रते निष्केवल्ये गायत्रतृचाशीतावेतदादिके द्वे सूक्ते । तथैव पंचमारण्यके सूत्रितं च शौनकेन - ‘आ तू न इंद्र क्षुमंतमिति सूक्ते सूददोहाः' (ऐ. आ. ५. २. ३) इति । द्वितीये पर्याये मैत्रावणशस्त्र आद्यस्तृचः स्तोत्रियः । सूत्रितं च--- आ तू न इंद्र क्षुमंतमा प्र द्रव परावतः ' ( आश्व. श्रौ. ६. ४ ) इति ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the ninth Anuvāka there are thirteen sūktas. Among them the first is the sūkta of nine Ṛks beginning आ तू न इंद्र (*ā tū na iṃdra*); its seer is Kusīdin, the son of Kaṇva; it is in the Gāyatrī metre and addressed to Indra. So the Anukramaṇikā says: आ तू नो नव कुसीदी काण्वः (*ā tū no nava kusīdī kāṇvaḥ*). In the Mahāvrata, in the Niṣkevalya śastra, this sūkta and the next, two sūktas beginning with it, are used for the Gāyatrī tṛca. So Śaunaka states in the Fifth Āraṇyaka: आ तू न इंद्र क्षुमंतमिति सूक्ते सूददोहाः (*ā tū na iṃdra kṣumaṃtamiti sūkte sūdadohāḥ*) (Ai. Ā. 5. 2. 3). In the second paryāya, in the Maitrāvaruṇa śastra, the first tṛca is the Stotriya. It is stated in the sūtra: आ तू न इंद्र क्षुमंतमा प्र द्रव परावतः (*ā tū na iṃdra kṣumaṃtamā pra drava parāvataḥ*) (Āśv. Śrau. 6. 4).

##### Anuvāda

In the ninth Anuvāka of the eighth Maṇḍala there are thirteen sūktas. Among them the sūkta आ तू न इंद्र (*ā tū na iṃdra*) is the first. It has nine Ṛks. The seer of this sūkta is Kusīdī, the son of Kaṇva; Indra is the deity; the metre is Gāyatrī. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: आ तू नो नव कुसीदी काण्वः (*ā tū no nava kusīdī kāṇvaḥ*). In the Mahāvrateṣṭi, in the recitation of the Niṣkevalya śastra mantras, this sūkta and the following one are used for the Gāyatrī-metre Ṛks; this is indicated in the Fifth Āraṇyaka of the Aitareya Āraṇyaka by the sage Śaunaka in the sentence आ तू न इंद्र क्षुमंतमिति सूक्ते सूददोहाः (*ā tū na iṃdra kṣumaṃtamiti sūkte sūdadohāḥ*) (Ai. Ā. 5-2-3). In the second paryāya, the first three Ṛks of this sūkta are to be recited as the Stotriya tṛca in the śastra mantras recited by the priest called Maitrāvaruṇa; this is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra आ तू न इंद्र क्षुमंतमा प्र द्रव परावतः (*ā tū na iṃdra kṣumaṃtamā pra drava parāvataḥ*) (Ā. 6-44). **NOTE**
Sūkta 81
Maṇḍala 8, Anuvāka 8, Sūkta 81 **NOTE**
Aṣṭaka 6, Adhyāya 5, Varga 37, 38
Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 9
Seer: Kusīdī Kāṇvaḥ
Deity: Indraḥ
Metre: Gāyatrī
#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ तू न॑ इंद्र क्षु॒मंतं॑ चि॒त्रं ग्रा॒भं सं गृ॑भाय ।  
> म॒हा॒ह॒स्ती दक्षि॑णेन ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 208

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । तु । नः । इंद्र । क्षुऽमंतम् । चित्रम् । ग्राभम् । सम् । गृभाय ।  
> महाऽहस्ती । दक्षिणेन ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र महाहस्ती महाहस्तवान् त्वं तु तदानीमेव नः अस्मभ्यं दातुं क्षुमंतं शब्दवंतम् । स्तुत्यमित्यर्थः । चित्रं चायनीयं ग्राभं ग्राहकं ग्रहणार्हं वा धनं दक्षिणेन हस्तेन  आ सं गृभाय आभिमुख्येन संगृहाण ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, you of the great hand (महाहस्ती महाहस्तवान् (*mahāhastī mahāhastavān*)), at that very moment, to give to us, take up with your right hand the wealth that is क्षुमंतं शब्दवंतम् (*kṣumaṃtaṃ śabdavaṃtam*), that is, praiseworthy; marvellous (चित्रं चायनीयं (*citraṃ cāyanīyaṃ*)); ग्राभं ग्राहकं ग्रहणार्हं वा (*grābhaṃ grāhakaṃ grahaṇārhaṃ vā*), that which grasps or is fit to be grasped. Take it up facing us (आभिमुख्येन संगृहाण (*ābhimukhyena saṃgṛhāṇa*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- महाहस्ती (*mahāhastī*)—you, who have a great (large) hand
- तु (*tu*)—at once
- नः (*naḥ*)—to give to us
- क्षुमंतं (*kṣumaṃtaṃ*)—that which is praiseworthy
- चित्रं (*citraṃ*)—that which is attractive
- ग्राभं (*grābhaṃ*)—that which is fit to be grasped, the wealth
- दक्षिणेन (*dakṣiṇena*)—with the right hand
- आ सं गृभाय (*ā saṃ gṛbhāya*)—take hold of

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you who have a great hand, take hold with your right hand of the wealth that is praiseworthy, attractive and fit to be grasped, to give it to us at once.

##### English translation
Indra, lord of the mighty hand, seize for us with your right hand marvellous praise-exciting (riches), worthy to be seized.
#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वि॒द्मा हि त्वा॑ तुविकू॒र्मिं तु॒विदे॑ष्णं तु॒वीम॑घम् ।  
> तु॒वि॒मा॒त्रमवो॑भिः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> विद्म । हि । त्वा । तुविऽकूर्मिम् । तुविऽदेष्णम् । तुविऽमघम् ।  
> तुविऽमात्रम् । अवःऽभिः ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 209

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वा त्वां विद्म हि जानीमः खलु । कीदृशमिति । तुविकूर्मिं बहुकर्माणं तुविदेष्णं बहुप्रदेयं तुविमघं बहुधनं तुविमात्रं बहुप्रमाणम् अवोभिः युक्तम् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, we indeed know you (त्वा त्वां विद्म हि जानीमः खलु (*tvā tvāṃ vidma hi jānīmaḥ khalu*)). Of what kind? तुविकूर्मिं बहुकर्माणं (*tuvikūrmiṃ bahukarmāṇaṃ*), of many deeds; तुविदेष्णं बहुप्रदेयं (*tuvideṣṇaṃ bahupradeyaṃ*), with many gifts to give; तुविमघं बहुधनं (*tuvimaghaṃ bahudhanaṃ*), of much wealth; तुविमात्रं बहुप्रमाणम् (*tuvimātraṃ bahupramāṇam*), of great measure; अवोभिः युक्तम् (*avobhiḥ yuktam*), furnished with protections.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra) त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- तुविकूर्मिं (*tuvikūrmiṃ*)—as one of many kinds of deeds
- तुविदेष्णं (*tuvideṣṇaṃ*)—as one who gives abundantly
- तुविमुघं (*tuvimughaṃ*)—as one who has abundant wealth
- तुविमात्रं (*tuvimātraṃ*)—as one of vast measure
- अवोभिः (*avobhiḥ*)—as one endowed with protection
- विद्म हि (*vidma hi*)—we do know

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, we do know you as one of many kinds of deeds, one who gives abundantly, one who has abundant wealth, one of vast measure, and one endowed with protection.

##### English translation
We know you the achiever of many great deeds, the bestower of many gifts, the lord of much wealth, vast in size, and full of protection (for your worshippers).
#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न॒हि त्वा॑ शूर दे॒वा न मर्ता॑सो॒ दित्सं॑तम् ।  
> भी॒मं न गां वा॒रयं॑ते ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नहि । त्वा । शूर । देवाः । न । मर्तासः । दित्संतम् ।  
> भीमम् । न । गाम् । वारयंते ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे शूर इंद्र त्वा त्वां दित्संतं दातुमिच्छंतं देवाः नहि वारयंते न निवारयंति । तथा मर्तासः मर्त्या अपि न वारयंते । भीमं न गां भयजनकं वृषभं यवसे प्रवृत्तमिव । तं यथा वारयितुं न शक्नुवंति तद्वत् ॥
> प्रथमे पर्यायेऽच्छावाकशस्त्रे ‘एतो न्विंद्रम्' इति तृचः । तथा च सूत्रितम् - ‘एतो न्विंद्रं स्तवामेशानं मा नो अस्मिन्मघवन्' (आश्व. श्रौ. ६. ४) इति ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O hero Indra, when you wish to give (दित्संतं दातुमिच्छंतं (*ditsaṃtaṃ dātumicchaṃtaṃ*)), the gods do not restrain you, and mortals too do not restrain you. As a fearsome bull (भीमं न गां भयजनकं वृषभं (*bhīmaṃ na gāṃ bhayajanakaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ*)) that has set out for pasture cannot be restrained, so it is with you. In the first paryāya, in the Acchāvāka śastra, there is the tṛca एतो न्विंद्रम् (*eto nviṃdram*). So it is stated in the sūtra: एतो न्विंद्रं स्तवामेशानं मा नो अस्मिन्मघवन् (*eto nviṃdraṃ stavāmeśānaṃ mā no asminmaghavan*) (Āśv. Śrau. 6. 4).
### Printed p. 210

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शूर (*śūra*)—O Indra, the valiant
- भीमं (*bhīmaṃ*)—fearsome
- गां न (*gāṃ na*)—as a bull cannot be restrained
- दित्संतं (*ditsaṃtaṃ*)—who are desirous of giving
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- देवाः (*devāḥ*)—the gods
- नहि वारयंते (*nahi vārayaṃte*)—cannot restrain
- मर्तासः (*martāsaḥ*)—men also
- न (*na*)—cannot restrain

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O valiant Indra, as a fearsome bull cannot be restrained, so the gods cannot restrain you who are desirous of giving. Men also cannot restrain you.

##### English translation
Hero, when you desire to give, neither gods nor men can stay you, as (they cannot stay) a terrible bull.
#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> एतो॒ न्विंद्रं॒ स्तवा॒मेशा॑नं॒ वस्वः॑ स्व॒राज॑म् ।  
> न राध॑सा मर्धिषन्नः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । इत । ऊं इति । नु । इंद्रम् । स्तवाम । ईशानम् । वस्वः । स्वऽराजम् ।  
> न । राधसा । मर्धिषत् । नः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अस्मदीया जनाः एतो आगच्छतैव नु क्षिप्रम्। किं कर्तुम् । स्तवाम इंद्रम् । कीदृशं तम् । वस्वः वसुनः धनस्य ईशानं स्वामिनं स्वराजं स्वयमेव राजमानं स्वर्गे राजमानं वा । नः अस्मान् इंद्रेणानुगृहीतान् राधसा धनेनान्यो धनी न मर्धिषत् न बाधताम् । आढ्यानामस्मत्समानानामप्याढ्यत्वाय स्तवामेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O our people, come at once, quickly. To do what? To praise Indra. Of what kind is he? वस्वः वसुनः धनस्य ईशानं स्वामिनं (*vasvaḥ vasunaḥ dhanasya īśānaṃ svāminaṃ*), lord of wealth; स्वराजं स्वयमेव राजमानं (*svarājaṃ svayameva rājamānaṃ*), shining of himself, or shining in heaven (स्वर्गे राजमानं वा (*svarge rājamānaṃ vā*)). May no other wealthy man oppress us, who are favoured by Indra, with wealth (राधसा धनेनान्यो धनी न मर्धिषत् न बाधताम् (*rādhasā dhanenānyo dhanī na mardhiṣat na bādhatām*)). The meaning is: we praise him for the prosperity even of the wealthy who are like ourselves.

### Printed p. 211

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O people) वस्वः (*vasvaḥ*)—of all wealth
- ईशानं (*īśānaṃ*)—who is the lord
- स्वराजं (*svarājaṃ*)—who is self-resplendent
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- स्तवाम (*stavāma*)—let us praise
- नु (*nu*)—quickly
- एतो (*eto*)—be pleased to come
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- राधसा (*rādhasā*)—by pride of wealth
- न मर्धिषत् (*na mardhiṣat*)—let no one harm

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O people, let us praise Indra, who is lord of all wealth and self-resplendent; be pleased to come quickly. Let no one harm us by the pride of his wealth.

##### English translation
Hasten hither, let us glorify Indra the lord of wealth, the self-resplendent, let none vex us by his wealth.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

That in the first paryāya, in the śastra mantras recited by the priest called Acchāvāka, three Ṛks beginning with एतो न्विंद्रं (*eto nviṃdraṃ*) are to be recited, is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra एतो न्विंद्रं स्तवामेशानं मा नो अस्मिन्मघवन् (*eto nviṃdraṃ stavāmeśānaṃ mā no asminmaghavan*) (Ā. 6-4).
#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र स्तो॑ष॒दुप॑ गासिष॒च्छ्रव॒त्साम॑ गी॒यमा॑नम् ।  
> अ॒भि राध॑सा जुगुरत् ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । स्तोषत् । उप । गासिषत् । श्रवत् । साम । गीयमानम् ।  
> अभि । राधसा । जुगुरत् ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पूर्वमंत्रे स्तवामेत्युक्तम् । तदेव स्तोत्रमिंद्रः प्र स्तोषत् । प्रस्तुत्वा च गासिषत् उपगानं च करोतु । तदर्थं गीयमानं साम स्तोत्रं श्रवत् शृणोतु । राधसा धनेन च युक्तोऽस्मान अभि जुगुरत् अभिगृणातु स्वीकुर्विति ॥ ॥ ३७ ॥ (ग-पुस्तकेऽस्य मंत्रस्य भाष्यमेवं विद्यते-सोता इंद्रं प्रस्तोतु। उपगासिषदुपगानं च करोतु । गीयमानं साम श्रवत् शृणोतु । राधसा धनेन प्रसाधनेन वा युक्तोऽस्मानभिजुगुरदभिगृणातु स्वीकुर्विति ।)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the previous mantra स्तवाम (*stavāma*) was said. May Indra himself praise that very hymn (प्र स्तोषत् (*pra stoṣat*)), and having praised, may he also sing the accompanying chant (गासिषत् उपगानं (*gāsiṣat upagānaṃ*)). For that purpose may he hear (श्रवत् शृणोतु (*śravat śṛṇotu*)) the sāman hymn that is being sung. Endowed with wealth (राधसा धनेन च युक्तोऽस्मान (*rādhasā dhanena ca yukto 'smāna*)), may he take us up, approve us (अभि जुगुरत् अभिगृणातु स्वीकुर्विति (*abhi jugurat abhigṛṇātu svīkurviti*)). In the Ga manuscript the bhāṣya of this mantra reads: may the Soma-presser praise Indra (सोता इंद्रं प्रस्तोतु (*sotā iṃdraṃ prastotu*)) and sing the accompaniment; may Indra hear the sāman being sung; endowed with wealth or with accomplishment (राधसा धनेन प्रसाधनेन वा (*rādhasā dhanena prasādhanena vā*)), may he take us up and approve us.
### Printed p. 212

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra)
- प्र स्तोषत् (*pra stoṣat*)—may he praise excellently
- उप गासिषत् (*upa gāsiṣat*)—may he sing along with us
- गीयमानं (*gīyamānaṃ*)—that which is being sung by us
- सामु (*sāmu*)—the hymn in the form of a sāman
- श्रवत् (*śravat*)—may he hear
- राधसा (*rādhasā*)—endowed with wealth, us
- अभि जुगुरत् (*abhi jugurat*)—may he accept

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Indra also praise excellently along with us. May he sing along with us. May he hear the hymn in the form of a sāman that is being sung by us. May he accept us, who are endowed with wealth.

##### English translation
May (Indra) sing the prelude, may he sing the accompaniment, may he listen to our hymn as it is chanted; may he, endowed with wealth, accept us favourably.
#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ नो॑ भर॒ दक्षि॑णेना॒भि स॒व्येन॒ प्र मृ॑श ।  
> इंद्र॒ मा नो॒ वसो॒र्निर्भा॑क् ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । नः । भर । दक्षिणेन । अभि । सव्येन । प्र । मृश ।  
> इंद्र । मा । नः । वसोः । निः । भाक् ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र नः अस्मभ्यम् आ भर । आहृत्य दक्षिणेन सव्येन च हस्तेनोभाभ्यां हस्ताभ्याम् अभि प्र मृश । प्रयच्छेत्यर्थः। नः अस्मान् वसोः धनात् मा निर्भाक् मा निर्भाक्षीः ॥
> द्वितीये पर्यायेऽच्छावाकशस्त्रे ‘उप क्रमस्व' इति तृचः । सूत्रितं च--- उप क्रमस्वा भर धृषता तदस्मै नव्यम्' (आश्व. श्रौ. ६. ४) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, bring to us (आ भर आहृत्य (*ā bhara āhṛtya*)): having brought it, with the right hand and with the left, with both hands, give it (अभि प्र मृश प्रयच्छ (*abhi pra mṛśa prayaccha*)). Do not deprive us of wealth (वसोः धनात् मा निर्भाक् मा निर्भाक्षीः (*vasoḥ dhanāt mā nirbhāk mā nirbhākṣīḥ*)). In the second paryāya, in the Acchāvāka śastra, there is the tṛca उप क्रमस्व (*upa kramasva*). So it is stated in the sūtra: उप क्रमस्वा भर धृषता तदस्मै नव्यम् (*upa kramasvā bhara dhṛṣatā tadasmai navyam*) (Āśv. Śrau. 6. 4).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- दक्षिणेन (*dakṣiṇena*)—with the right hand
- आ भर (*ā bhara*)—bring wealth
- सव्येन (*savyena*)—with the left hand
- अभि प्र मृश (*abhi pra mṛśa*)—give to us
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- वसोः (*vasoḥ*)—from wealth
- मा निर्भाक् (*mā nirbhāk*)—do not make separated
### Printed p. 213

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, bring wealth to us with the right hand. Give to us with the left hand. Do not make us separated from wealth.

##### English translation
Bring us (gifts) with your right hand, and with your left bestow them on us; exclude us not, Indra, from wealth.
#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उप॑ क्रम॒स्वा भ॑र धृष॒ता धृ॑ष्णो॒ जना॑नाम् ।  
> अदा॑शूष्टरस्य॒ वेदः॑ ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उप । क्रमस्व । आ । भर । धृषता । धृष्णो इति । जनानाम् ।  
> अदाशूःऽतरस्य । वेदः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वम् उप क्रमस्व धनं प्रत्युपगच्छ । प्रवृत्तो भव वा दातुम् । हे धृष्णो धर्षक शत्रूणां धृषता धृष्टेन चेतसा युक्तः सन् आ भर आहर च । कस्य धनमाहरेति उच्यते । जनानां मध्ये अदाशूष्टरस्य अत्यंतमदातृतमस्य वेदः धनम् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, approach the wealth (उप क्रमस्व धनं प्रत्युपगच्छ (*upa kramasva dhanaṃ pratyupagaccha*)), or be ready to give (प्रवृत्तो भव वा दातुम् (*pravṛtto bhava vā dātum*)). O bold one (धृष्णो धर्षक शत्रूणां (*dhṛṣṇo dharṣaka śatrūṇāṃ*)), assailant of enemies, being furnished with a bold mind (धृषता धृष्टेन चेतसा युक्तः सन् (*dhṛṣatā dhṛṣṭena cetasā yuktaḥ san*)), bring it (आ भर आहर (*ā bhara āhara*)). If it is asked whose wealth he should bring, it is said: among people, the wealth of him who is most utterly ungiving (जनानां मध्ये अदाशूष्टरस्य अत्यंतमदातृतमस्य वेदः धनम् (*janānāṃ madhye adāśūṣṭarasya atyaṃtamadātṛtamasya vedaḥ dhanam*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- धृष्णो (*dhṛṣṇo*)—O Indra, crusher of enemies
- उप क्रमस्व (*upa kramasva*)—approach the enemy's wealth
- जनानां (*janānāṃ*)—among men
- अदाशूष्टरस्य (*adāśūṣṭarasya*)—of him who does not give
- वेदः (*vedaḥ*)—wealth
- धृषता (*dhṛṣatā*)—by your firm strength
- आ भर (*ā bhara*)—bring

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, crusher of enemies, approach the enemy's wealth. Bring by your firm strength the wealth of him among men who does not give.
### Printed p. 214

##### English translation
Come hither, and bring us, daring one, with your resolute (mind), the wealth of him who is pre-eminently a niggard amongst men.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

That in the second paryāya, in the śastra mantras recited by the priest called Acchāvāka, three Ṛks beginning with उप क्रमस्व (*upa kramasva*) are to be recited, is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra उप क्रमस्वा भर धृषता तदस्मै नव्यं (*upa kramasvā bhara dhṛṣatā tadasmai navyaṃ*) (Ā. 6-4).
#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्र॒ य उ॒ नु ते॒ अस्ति॒ वाजो॒ विप्रे॑भिः॒ सनि॑त्वः ।  
> अ॒स्माभिः॒ सु तं स॑नुहि ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्र । यः । ऊं इति । नु । ते । अस्ति । वाजः । विप्रेभिः । सनित्वः ।  
> अस्माभिः । सु । तम् । सनुहि ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र यः वाजः अन्नं विप्रेभिः मेधाविभिः सनित्वः संभजनीयः ते तव अस्ति तं वाजम् अस्माभिः याचितः सन् अस्मभ्यं वा सु सुष्ठु सनुहि देहि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, the food (वाजः अन्नं (*vājaḥ annaṃ*)) that is yours, which is to be shared in by the wise (विप्रेभिः मेधाविभिः सनित्वः संभजनीयः (*viprebhiḥ medhāvibhiḥ sanitvaḥ saṃbhajanīyaḥ*)), that food, when asked by us, give to us well (अस्माभिः याचितः सन् अस्मभ्यं वा सु सुष्ठु सनुहि देहि (*asmābhiḥ yācitaḥ san asmabhyaṃ vā su suṣṭhu sanuhi dehi*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ते (*te*)—in you
- यः उ (*yaḥ u*)—which
- वाजः (*vājaḥ*)—food
- विप्रेभिः (*viprebhiḥ*)—by the wise
- सनित्वः (*sanitvaḥ*)—being esteemed
- अस्ति (*asti*)—is
- तं (*taṃ*)—that food
- अस्माभिः (*asmābhiḥ*)—when asked by us
- सु (*su*)—exceedingly
- सनुहि (*sanuhi*)—give

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, the wealth that is in you, which all the wise praise and esteem, that wealth give to us when we ask you.
### Printed p. 215

##### English translation
O Indra, give us abundantly that wealth which is yours, and which is to be obtained by the wise (worshippers).
#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स॒द्यो॒जुव॑स्ते॒ वाजा॑ अ॒स्मभ्यं॑ वि॒श्वश्चं॑द्राः ।  
> वशै॑श्च म॒क्षू ज॑रंते ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सद्यःऽजुवः । ते । वाजाः । अस्मभ्यम् । विश्वऽचंद्राः ।  
> वशैः । च । मक्षु । जरंते ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र ते तव वाजाः अस्मभ्यं सद्योजुवः शीघ्रं गंतारो भवंतु । कीदृशास्ते । विश्वचंद्राः सर्वहिरण्योपेता बहूनामाह्लादका वा । अस्मदीयाश्च जनाः वशैः कामैरनेकैर्युक्ताः मक्षु शीघ्रं जरंते स्तुवंति ॥ ॥ ३८ ॥
> वेदार्थस्य प्रकाशेन तमो हार्दं निवारयन् ।
> पुमर्थांश्चतुरो देयाद्विद्यातीर्थमहेश्वरः ॥
> इति श्रीमद्राजाधिराजपरमेश्वरवैदिकमार्गप्रवर्तकश्रीवीरबुक्कभूपालसाम्राज्यधुरंधरेण सायणाचार्येण विरचिते माधवीये वेदार्थप्रकाश ऋक्संहिताभाष्ये
> षष्ठाष्टके पंचमोऽध्यायः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, may your food-offerings (वाजाः (*vājāḥ*)) become fast-going to us (सद्योजुवः शीघ्रं गंतारो भवंतु (*sadyojuvaḥ śīghraṃ gaṃtāro bhavaṃtu*)). Of what kind are they? विश्वचंद्राः सर्वहिरण्योपेता बहूनामाह्लादका वा (*viśvacaṃdrāḥ sarvahiraṇyopetā bahūnāmāhlādakā vā*): provided with all gold, or delighting many. And our people, furnished with many desires (वशैः कामैरनेकैर्युक्ताः (*vaśaiḥ kāmairanekairyuktāḥ*)), quickly praise you (मक्षु शीघ्रं जरंते स्तुवंति (*makṣu śīghraṃ jaraṃte stuvaṃti*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra) वशैः (*vaśaiḥ*)—our people, fulfilled in their desires
- मुक्षु (*mukṣu*)—quickly
- जरंते (*jaraṃte*)—praise you
- ते (*te*)—your
- विश्वचंद्राः (*viśvacaṃdrāḥ*)—delightful to all
- वाजाः (*vājāḥ*)—foods
- अस्मभ्यं (*asmabhyaṃ*)—to us
- सद्योजुवः (*sadyojuvaḥ*)—may they come quickly

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, our people, furnished with many desires, praise you quickly. May your foods, delightful to all, come to us quickly.

##### English translation
May your all-rejoicing riches speedily come to us; full of desires, men immediately offer their praises.
### Printed p. 216
वेदार्थस्य प्रकाशेन तमो हार्दं निवारयन् ॥ (*vedārthasya prakāśena tamo hārdaṃ nivārayan ‖*)
पुमर्थांश्चतुरो देयाद्विद्यातीर्थमहेश्वरः ॥ (*pumarthāṃścaturo deyādvidyātīrthamaheśvaraḥ ‖*)
इति श्रीमद्राजाधिराजपरमेश्वरवैदिकमार्गप्रवर्तकश्रीवीरबुक्कभूपाल साम्राज्यधरंधरेण सायणाचार्येण विरचिते माधवीये वेदार्थप्रकाश (*iti śrīmadrājādhirājaparameśvaravaidikamārgapravartakaśrīvīrabukkabhūpāla sāmrājyadharaṃdhareṇa sāyaṇācāryeṇa viracite mādhavīye vedārthaprakāśa*)
ऋक्संहिताभाष्ये षष्ठाष्टके पंचमोध्यायः ॥ (*ṛksaṃhitābhāṣye ṣaṣṭhāṣṭake paṃcamodhyāyaḥ ‖*)
॥ ओं तत्सत् ॥ (*‖ oṃ tatsat ‖*)
Here ends the fifth chapter in the sixth Aṣṭaka of the Ṛgveda-Saṃhitā, with Sāyaṇa's Bhāṣya and a Kannada translation.
यदक्षरपदभ्रष्टं मात्राहीनं तु यद्भवेत् । (*yadakṣarapadabhraṣṭaṃ mātrāhīnaṃ tu yadbhavet |*)
तत्सर्वं क्षम्यतां देव वागीश्वर नमोऽस्तुते । (*tatsarvaṃ kṣamyatāṃ deva vāgīśvara namo 'stute |*)
॥ शुभं भूयात् ॥ (*‖ śubhaṃ bhūyāt ‖*)
॥ मंगळं ॥ (*‖ maṃgaḷaṃ ‖*)
॥ श्री ॥ (*‖ śrī ‖*)
॥ श्री महागणाधिपतये नमः ॥ (*‖ śrī mahāgaṇādhipataye namaḥ ‖*)
वागीशाद्याः सुमनसः सर्वार्थानामुपक्रमे । (*vāgīśādyāḥ sumanasaḥ sarvārthānāmupakrame |*)
यं नत्वा कृतकृत्याः स्युस्तं नमामि गजाननं ॥ (*yaṃ natvā kṛtakṛtyāḥ syustaṃ namāmi gajānanaṃ ‖*)
यस्य निश्वसितं वेदा यो वेदेभ्योऽखिलं जगत् । (*yasya niśvasitaṃ vedā yo vedebhyo 'khilaṃ jagat |*)
निर्ममे तमहं वंदे विद्यातीर्थमहेश्वरं ॥ (*nirmame tamahaṃ vaṃde vidyātīrthamaheśvaraṃ ‖*)
वेदः शिवः शिवो वेदो वेदाध्यायी सदाशिवः । (*vedaḥ śivaḥ śivo vedo vedādhyāyī sadāśivaḥ |*)
तस्मात्सर्वप्रयत्नेन वेदमेव सदा जपेत् ॥ (*tasmātsarvaprayatnena vedameva sadā japet ‖*)
Ṛgveda-Saṃhitā with Sāyaṇa's Bhāṣya.
अथ षष्ठाष्टके षष्ठोऽध्यायः (*atha ṣaṣṭhāṣṭake ṣaṣṭho 'dhyāyaḥ*)
The sixth chapter in the sixth Aṣṭaka. The eighty-second sūkta in the eighth maṇḍala.
## Sūkta 82 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ॥ श्रीगणेशाय नमः ॥
> यस्य निःश्वसितं वेदा यो वेदेभ्योऽखिलं जगत् ।
> निर्ममे तमहं वंदे विद्यातीर्थमहेश्वरम् ॥
> ‘आ प्र द्रव' इति नवर्चं द्वितीयं सूक्तम् । तथा चानुक्रम्यते- ‘ आ प्र द्रव ' इति । ‘ऋषिश्चान्यस्मात् ' इति परिभाषया काण्वः कुसीद्यृषिः । प्राग्वत्सप्रीयपरिभाषया गायत्री छंदः । अनादेशपरिभाषयेंद्रो देवता । महाव्रते निष्केवल्ये सूक्तविनियोग उक्तः । द्वितीये रात्रिपर्याये मैत्रावरुणशस्त्रे ‘आ प्र द्रव ' इति तृचोऽनुरूपः । सूत्रितं च- ‘ आ प्र द्रव परावतो नह्यन्यं बळाकरमित्यष्टौ ' ( आश्व. श्रौ. ६. ४ ) इति ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Salutation to Gaṇeśa. Sāyaṇa bows to Vidyātīrtha-Maheśvara, from whose breath the Vedas came and who made the whole world out of the Vedas. The second sūkta, of nine Ṛks, is the one beginning आ प्र द्रव (*ā pra drava*); the Anukramaṇikā says: आ प्र द्रव (*ā pra drava*). By the rule ऋषिश्चान्यस्मात् (*ṛṣiścānyasmāt*) the seer is Kusīdin of the Kaṇva family (काण्वः कुसीद्यृषिः (*kāṇvaḥ kusīdyṛṣiḥ*)); by the rule प्राग्वत्सप्रीयपरिभाषया (*prāgvatsaprīyaparibhāṣayā*) the metre is Gāyatrī (गायत्री छंदः (*gāyatrī chaṃdaḥ*)); by the rule अनादेशपरिभाषयेंद्रो देवता (*anādeśaparibhāṣayeṃdro devatā*) the deity is Indra. The use of the sūkta in the Niṣkevalya śastra of the Mahāvrata has been stated. In the second rātri-paryāya, in the Maitrāvaruṇa śastra, the tṛca आ प्र द्रव (*ā pra drava*) is the Anurūpa. So it is stated in the sūtra: आ प्र द्रव परावतो नह्यन्यं बळाकरमित्यष्टौ (*ā pra drava parāvato nahyanyaṃ baḷākaramityaṣṭau*) (Āśv. Śrau. 6. 4).

### Printed p. 218

The Anuvāda: this sūkta beginning आ प्र द्रव (*ā pra drava*) is the second sūkta in the ninth Anuvāka. It has nine Ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: आ प्र द्रव (*ā pra drava*). The seer is the one called Kusīdī Kāṇva; Indra is the deity; the metre is Gāyatrī. It was already stated in the introduction to the preceding sūkta that this sūkta is employed in the Mahāvrateṣṭi for the Niṣkevalya śastra mantras. In the second rātri-paryāya, in the śastra mantras recited by the priest called Maitrāvaruṇa, the first three Ṛks of this sūkta, beginning आ प्र द्रव (*ā pra drava*), are to be recited as the Anurūpa tṛca; this is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra आ प्र द्रव परावतो सह्यस्यं बळाकरमित्यष्टा (*ā pra drava parāvato sahyasyaṃ baḷākaramityaṣṭā*) (Ā. 5-4). **NOTE**

Sūkta 82

Maṇḍala 8, Anuvāka 9, Sūkta 82

Aṣṭaka 6, Adhyāya 6, Varga 1, 2

Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 9

Seer: Kusīdiḥ Kāṇvaḥ

Deity: Indraḥ

Metre: Gāyatrī

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ प्र द्र॑व परा॒वतो॑ऽर्वा॒वत॑श्च वृत्रहन् ।  
> मध्वः॒ प्रति॒ प्रभ॑र्मणि ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । प्र । द्रव । पराऽवतः । अर्वाऽवतः । च । वृत्रऽहन् ।  
> मध्वः । प्रति । प्रऽभर्मणि ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वृत्रहन् अपामावरकस्य वृत्रासुरस्य हंतर्हे इंद्र प्रभर्मणि । प्रकृष्टानि भर्माणि भरणानि पशुग्रहादिसंपादनानि यस्मिन् स प्रभर्मा यज्ञः । यद्वा । प्रकृष्टाः कर्मणि कुशला भर्माणो देवानां हविष्प्रदानेन पोषका ऋत्विजो यस्मिन्निति स तथोक्तः । एतादृशे यज्ञे मध्वः मदकरान् सोमान् प्रति परावतः विप्रकृष्टात् दूरस्थाद्देशादपि अर्वावतश्च समीपस्थाद्देशादपि आभिमुख्येन प्र द्रव त्वं त्वरयागच्छ । मध्व इति ‘वा छंदसि' इति पूर्वसवर्णदीर्घाभावः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa addresses Indra as slayer of Vṛtra, the demon who held back the waters. The word प्रभर्मणि (*prabharmaṇi*) he takes as a name of the sacrifice, in two ways. In the first, the प्रभर्मा (*prabharmā*) is the sacrifice in which excellent acquisitions (bharmāṇi, bharaṇāni) such as the procuring of cattle and houses are accomplished. In the second ("or else"), it is the sacrifice in which the priests are the bharmāṇas: skilled in the rite and nourishers of the gods by giving them oblations. In such a sacrifice, for the sake of the intoxicating Soma-libations, Indra is to hasten hither facing us, quickly, both from a distant region and from a near one. Sāyaṇa adds that in मध्व (*madhva*) the lengthening that would ordinarily arise from the preceding similar vowel does not occur.

### Printed p. 219

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हे वृत्रहन् (*he vṛtrahan*)—O Indra, slayer of the demon Vṛtra
- प्रभर्मणि (*prabharmaṇi*)—in this sacrifice
- मध्वः (*madhvaḥ*)—the gladdening Soma-juice
- प्रति (*prati*)—with regard to it, in order to drink it
- परावतः (*parāvataḥ*)—whether you are in a distant region, from there
- अर्वावतश्च (*arvāvataśca*)—or, if you are in a near region, from there
- आ (*ā*)—towards our sacrifice
- प्र द्रव (*pra drava*)—come quickly

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you who slew Vṛtra, the demon who held back the waters in the clouds so that they did not pour down as rain: in this sacrifice, to drink the gladdening Soma, whether you are in a distant region or in a near one, come quickly from there towards our sacrifice.

##### English translation
Hasten, slayer of Vritra, from afar or from nigh, to the exhilarating (Soma-libations) in the sacrifice.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

प्रभर्मणि (*prabharmaṇi*)—in the sacrifice. The commentator explains this word in two ways. प्रकृष्टानि भर्माणि भरणानि पशुगृहादिसंपादनानि यस्मिन् स प्रभर्मा यज्ञः । (*prakṛṣṭāni bharmāṇi bharaṇāni paśugṛhādisaṃpādanāni yasmin sa prabharmā yajñaḥ |*) The sacrificial rite by performing which excellent and nourishing things such as cattle and houses can be obtained through the favour of the gods is called प्रभर्मा (*prabharmā*). Or else: प्रकृष्टाः कर्मणि कुशला भर्माणो देवानां हविष्प्रदानेन पोषका ऋत्विजो यस्मिन्निति स तथोक्तः । (*prakṛṣṭāḥ karmaṇi kuśalā bharmāṇo devānāṃ haviṣpradānena poṣakā ṛtvijo yasminniti sa tathoktaḥ |*) Or else, in this sacrificial rite the priests, skilled in the mantras and in the actions, please the gods by giving oblations and the like, and by their favour they are the supporters, that is, the nourishers, of the sacrificer. Therefore a sacrifice endowed with such a course of action is called प्रभर्मा (*prabharmā*).

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ती॒व्राः सोमा॑स॒ आ ग॑हि सु॒तासो॑ मादयि॒ष्णवः॑ ।  
> पिबा॑ द॒धृग्यथो॑चि॒षे ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तीव्राः । सोमासः । आ । गहि । सुतासः । मादयिष्णवः ।  
> पिब । दधृक् । यथा । ओचिषे ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र तीव्राः तीव्रमदाः । क्षिप्रं मदकारिण इत्यर्थः । मादयिष्णवः मादनशीला मादनकारिणो वेमे सोमासः सोमाः सुतासः त्वदर्थमभिषुताः । तस्मात् आ गहि अस्मदीयं यज्ञं प्रत्यागच्छ । आगत्य च तान् पिब । सोमपाने कारणमाह । त्वं यथा दधृक् धृष्टस्तत्प्रीतौ प्रगल्भः संस्तान् ऊचिषे समवैषि सेवसे । ततस्तान यथाकामं पिबेत्यर्थः । दृधृगिति ‘ञिधृषा प्रागल्भ्ये' इत्यस्मात्  ‘ ऋत्विग्दधृक् ' इत्यादिना क्विन्प्रत्ययांतो निपात्यते । ऊचिषे ।' उच समवाये । छांदसे लिटि रूपम् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa addresses Indra. These Soma-juices are तीव्राः (*tīvrāḥ*), of keen intoxication, that is, they quickly produce exhilaration; they are मादयिष्णवः (*mādayiṣṇavaḥ*), intoxicating by nature, and they have been pressed for you. Therefore come to our sacrifice, and having come, drink them. He gives the reason for drinking: since you, दधृक् (*dadhṛk*), that is, bold and confident in your delight in them, resort to them, drink them as you wish.

### Printed p. 220

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra) तीव्राः (*tīvrāḥ*)—quickly producing intoxication
- मादयिष्णवः (*mādayiṣṇavaḥ*)—producing great delight
- सोमासः (*somāsaḥ*)—Soma-juices
- सुतासः (*sutāsaḥ*)—have been pressed and made ready for you
- आ गहि (*ā gahi*)—therefore be pleased to come towards our sacrifice
- यथा (*yathā*)—(after you have come) in the manner in which
- धृष्टः (*dhṛṣṭaḥ*)—you say that you are pleased by the drinking of Soma
- ऊचिषे (*ūciṣe*)—so, that is, as much as you wish
- पिब (*piba*)—drink

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, intoxicating and delight-giving Soma-juices have been pressed and made ready for you. Therefore come towards our sacrifice and drink as much as you wish, so that you are satisfied by drinking the Soma.

##### English translation
Come hither, the strong intoxicating Soma is effused : drink, since you are boldly devoted to it.

### Printed p. 221

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

तीव्राः (*tīvrāḥ*)—After the Soma-juice has been taken from the plants, it is the custom to strain it through a cloth and the like, to consecrate it with mantras and the like, to offer it to the gods and to drink it. To make this Soma-juice palatable some admixtures (āśira) are added. Soma-juice mixed with milk is called गवाशिर (*gavāśira*), that mixed with curds दध्याशिर (*dadhyāśira*), and that mixed with barley flour यवाशिर (*yavāśira*). Soma-juice taken from the plant with nothing mixed in is called तीव्र (*tīvra*). Because no other substance is mixed with such Soma-juice, its power of producing intoxication (mada) is greater. It is said in many places that Indra has a special liking for such keen Soma-juice.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इ॒षा मं॑द॒स्वादु॒ तेऽरं॒ वरा॑य म॒न्यवे॑ ।  
> भुव॑त्त इंद्र॒ शं हृ॒दे ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इषा । मंदस्व । आत् । ऊं इति । ते । अरम् । वराय । मन्यवे ।  
> भुवत् । ते । इंद्र । शम् । हृदे ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र इषा सोमलक्षणेनान्नेन मंदस्व मोदस्व हृष्टो भव । उ इत्यवधारणे । आत् अनंतरमेव ते तव वराय शत्रुनिवारकाय मन्यवे क्रोधाय स सोमः अरम् अलं पर्याप्तो भवतु । क्रोधशमने समर्थो भवतु । यदा सोमं पिबति तदा मन्युं त्यजतीत्यर्थः । किंच ते तव हृदे हृदये स सोमः शं शंकरः सुखकरः भुवत् भवतु ॥
> तृतीये पर्याये ‘आ त्वशत्रो' इत्यनुरूपस्तृचः । सूत्रितं च- ‘ आ त्वेता नि दीदता स्वशत्रवा गहि' ( आश्व. श्रौ. ६. ४ ) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa addresses Indra: rejoice with the food, that is, the Soma-offering, and be delighted. He explains उ (*u*) as a particle of emphasis. Immediately afterwards let that Soma be fully sufficient, अरम् (*aram*), for your वराय मन्यवे (*varāya manyave*), your enemy-restraining anger; let it be able to appease the anger. The meaning is that when he drinks Soma he gives up his anger. Moreover let that Soma be शं (*śaṃ*), bringing happiness, in your heart. He then adds a ritual use: in the third paryāya the tṛca beginning आ त्वशत्रो (*ā tvaśatro*) is the Anurūpa, and it is stated in the sūtra आ त्वेता नि दीदता स्वशत्रवा गहि (*ā tvetā ni dīdatā svaśatravā gahi*) (Āśv. Śrau. 6. 4).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हे इंद्र (*he iṃdra*)—O Indra
- इषा (*iṣā*)—with the food in the form of Soma, that is, by drinking the Soma-juice
- मंदस्व उ (*maṃdasva u*)—be delighted
- आत् (*āt*)—afterwards
- ते (*te*)—your
- वराय (*varāya*)—arisen for the destruction of enemies

### Printed p. 222

- मन्यवे (*manyave*)—anger (wrath)
- अरं (*araṃ*)—may that Soma-drinking appease it (and)
- ते (*te*)—your
- हृदे (*hṛde*)—in the heart that Soma
- शं (*śaṃ*)—happy, that is, producing delight
- भुवत् (*bhuvat*)—may it be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, be delighted with the food in the form of Soma, that is, by drinking the Soma-juice. May the anger that arose in you for the destruction of enemies be appeased by the drinking of Soma, and may that Soma bring delight to your heart (mind).

##### English translation
Rejoice yourself with this food,—may it forthwith avail to (quench) your foe-restraining anger, may it produce happiness, Indra, in your heart.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

स सोमः क्रोधशमने समर्थो भवतु । यदा सोमं पिबति तदा मन्युं त्यजतीत्यर्थः । (*sa somaḥ krodhaśamane samartho bhavatu | yadā somaṃ pibati tadā manyuṃ tyajatītyarthaḥ |*) When Soma is drunk all anger is appeased; that is, the Soma-juice has the power of appeasing anger. Therefore, as soon as Indra has drunk Soma, all the anger he had for the destruction of enemies is appeased and he becomes glad of mind: this is the purport.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ त्व॑शत्र॒वा ग॑हि॒ न्यु१॒॑क्थानि॑ च हूयसे ।  
> उ॒प॒मे रो॑च॒ने दि॒वः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । तु । अशत्रो इति । आ । गहि । नि । उक्थानि । च । हूयसे ।  
> उपऽमे । रोचने । दिवः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अशत्रो सपत्नरहित । अस्य बहुविधबलत्वाद्रणाभिमुखं गंतारः शत्रवो न संतीत्यर्थः । तादृशेंद्र तु क्षिप्रम् आ गहि आयाहि अस्मद्यज्ञं प्रत्यागच्छ । यतः दिवः स्वतेजसा दीप्यमानात् द्युलोकात् । तत्रस्थैर्देवैरित्यर्थः । रोचने अग्निभिर्दीप्यमाने लोके च उपमे समीपे । स्तोतारः स्तोत्रशस्त्रात्मकं शब्दं कुर्वंत्यत्रेत्युपमो यज्ञः । तस्मिन्नस्मदीये यज्ञे च उक्थानि त्रिवृत्पंचदशादिलक्षणानि स्तोत्राणि प्रति नि हूयसे त्वं स्तोतव्यतया नितरामाहूयसे । यस्मादेवं तस्मादागच्छेति समन्वयः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa addresses Indra as अशत्रो (*aśatro*), one without rivals: because of his manifold strength there are no enemies who would go to meet him in battle. Such an Indra is to come quickly to our sacrifice. The reasons: he is called, as one to be praised, to the उक्थानि (*ukthāni*), the stotras of the kinds called trivṛt, pañcadaśa and the like, at our sacrifice, which is उपमे (*upame*), near, since the singers raise the sound of stotra and śastra in it. The sacrifice is situated दिवः (*divaḥ*) in relation to heaven, which shines by its own radiance, that is, in relation to the gods who dwell there, and रोचने (*rocane*) in a world illumined by the fires. Since this is so, let him come: such is the connection of the sentence.

### Printed p. 223

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अशत्रो (*aśatro*)—O Indra, who are without enemies
- तु (*tu*)—quickly
- आ गहि (*ā gahi*)—be pleased to come towards our sacrifice
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—from the heavenly world, that is, by the gods who are in the world of heaven
- रोचने (*rocane*)—shining
- उपमे (*upame*)—at our sacrifice
- उक्थानि (*ukthāni*)—reciting the hymns
- नि हूयसे (*ni hūyase*)—you are invoked; therefore be pleased to come to our sacrifice

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, at this sacrifice that we are now performing, the gods have come from the shining heavenly world and are seated. The priests and the sacrificers are invoking you while reciting hymns; therefore be pleased to come to this our sacrifice.

##### English translation
O you who have no enemies, come hither ; you are summoned from the resplendent heaven to the hymns at this our rite near at hand in this world illumined (by the sacred fires )

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

अशत्रो (*aśatro*)—बहुविधबलत्वाद्रणाभिमुखं गंतारः शत्रवो न संतीत्यर्थः (*bahuvidhabalatvādraṇābhimukhaṃ gaṃtāraḥ śatravo na saṃtītyarthaḥ*). Because Indra has exceedingly great strength and power, there is no enemy who would confront Indra and fight him in battle; therefore Indra is here called one without enemies.

दिवः (*divaḥ*)—स्वतेजसा दीप्यमाना ' द्युलोकात् । शत्रुस्थैर्देवैरित्यर्थः (*svatejasā dīpyamānā ' dyulokāt | śatrusthairdevairityarthaḥ*). **NOTE** The heavenly world shines exceedingly with its own radiance; the purport is: from the gods who are in such a heaven.

रोचने (*rocane*)—अग्निभिर्दीप्यमाने (*agnibhirdīpyamāne*). The meaning is: brightly shining with the fires called Āhavanīya, Gārhapatya and Dakṣiṇāgni. This word is an epithet of the word उपमे (*upame*) (यज्ञे (*yajñe*)).

### Printed p. 224

उपमे (*upame*)—समीपे । स्तोतारः स्तोत्रशस्त्रात्मकं शब्दं कुर्वंत्यत्रेत्युपमो यज्ञः (*samīpe | stotāraḥ stotraśastrātmakaṃ śabdaṃ kurvaṃtyatretyupamo yajñaḥ*). Although the ordinary meaning of the word उपमे (*upame*) is "near", here the singers and the priests recite stotra and śastra mantras in a loud voice near (Agni); therefore the meaning यज्ञे (*yajñe*), "in the sacrifice", has been stated for the word उपमे (*upame*).

उक्थानि (*ukthāni*)—स्तोत्राणि । त्रिवृत्पंचदशादिलक्षणानि स्तोत्राणि (*stotrāṇi | trivṛtpaṃcadaśādilakṣaṇāni stotrāṇi*). Stotras or stomas have varieties such as trivṛt, pañcadaśa and saptadaśa. The purport is that Indra is invoked and called by such stotras.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तुभ्या॒यमद्रि॑भिः सु॒तो गोभिः॑ श्री॒तो मदा॑य॒ कम् ।  
> प्र सोम॑ इंद्र हूयते ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तुभ्य । अयम् । अद्रिऽभिः । सुतः । गोभिः । श्रीतः । मदाय । कम् ।  
> प्र । सोमः । इंद्र । हूयते ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र अद्रिभिः अभिषवसाधनैर्ग्रावभिः अयं सोमः तुभ्यं त्वदर्थं सुतः अभिषुतः । ततो दशापवित्रेण पूत्वा गोभिः गोविकारैः क्षीरादिभिः श्रीतः सोऽस्माभिः परावत एव संस्कृतः सोमस्तव मदाय मदार्थं कं सुखेन प्र हूयते अग्नौ स्वाहा क्रियते । तस्मादागत्य सोमं पिब ॥ ॥ १ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa addresses Indra. This Soma has been pressed for you with the अद्रिभिः (*adribhiḥ*), the stones that serve as the means of pressing. Then, purified with the दशापवित्रेण (*daśāpavitreṇa*) (the ten-fold strainer), it has been mixed with गोविकारैः (*govikāraiḥ*), products of the cow such as milk. Thus prepared by us, **NOTE** the Soma is offered happily (कं (*kaṃ*)) for your exhilaration, that is, the स्वाहा (*svāhā*) offering is made in the fire. Therefore come and drink the Soma.

### Printed p. 225

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हे इंद्र (*he iṃdra*)—O Indra
- अद्रिभिः (*adribhiḥ*)—with the stones used for crushing and pressing out the Soma
- अयं सोमः (*ayaṃ somaḥ*)—this Soma-juice
- तुभ्य सुतः (*tubhya sutaḥ*)—has been pressed and made ready for you
- गोभिः (*gobhiḥ*)—with the milk, a product of the cow
- श्रीतः (*śrītaḥ*)—it is mixed
- सोमः (*somaḥ*)—such Soma-juice
- मदाय (*madāya*)—for your delight
- कं (*kaṃ*)—happily (addressed to you) in the fire
- प्र हूयते (*pra hūyate*)—is offered with the cry स्वाहा (*svāhā*)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, this Soma-juice has been pressed and made ready for you with the stones that are the instruments of pressing the Soma, and it has been mixed with milk. Such Soma-juice is offered in the fire for your delight, addressed to you, with the cry स्वाहा (*svāhā*).

##### English translation
Indra, this Soma, effused for you by the stones and mixed with milk is offered auspiciously (in the fire) for your exhilaration.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्र॑ श्रु॒धि सु मे॒ हव॑म॒स्मे सु॒तस्य॒ गोम॑तः ।  
> वि पी॒तिं तृ॒प्तिम॑श्नुहि ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्र । श्रुधि । सु । मे । हवम् । अस्मे इति । सुतस्य । गोऽमतः ।  
> वि । पीतिम् । तृप्तिम् । अश्नुहि ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र मे मदीयं हवं त्वद्विषयमाह्वानं सु सुष्ठु श्रुधि शृणु । तथा अस्मे अस्माभिः सुतस्य अभिषुतस्य गोमतः गव्यक्षीरवतः । क्षीरेण मिश्रितस्येत्यर्थः । तादृशस्य सोमस्य पीतिं पानं वि अश्नुहि विविधं प्राप्नुहि । तत्पानेन विविधां तृप्तिं च गच्छ । 'अशू व्याप्तौ' । व्यत्ययेन परस्मैपदम् ॥
> तृतीये पर्याय एवाच्छावाकशस्त्रे ‘य इंद्र चमसेषु' इति तृचः । सूत्रितं च--- य इंद्र चमसेष्वा सोमः प्र वः सताम् ' ( आश्व. श्रौ. ६. ४) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa addresses Indra: hear well my call addressed to you. Of the Soma pressed by us and गोमतः (*gomataḥ*), that is, mixed with cow's milk, attain the drinking in manifold ways, and by drinking it go to manifold satisfaction. He adds the ritual use: in the third paryāya, in the Acchāvāka śastra, the tṛca beginning य इंद्र चमसेषु (*ya iṃdra camaseṣu*) is used. It is stated in the sūtra: य इंद्र चमसेष्वा सोमः प्र वः सताम् (*ya iṃdra camaseṣvā somaḥ pra vaḥ satām*) (Āśv. Śrau. 6. 4).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हे इंद्र (*he iṃdra*)—O Indra
- मे (*me*)—my
- हवं (*havaṃ*)—call or prayer
- सु (*su*)—well (with attention)
- श्रुधि (*śrudhi*)—hear
- अस्मे (*asme*)—by us
- सुतस्य (*sutasya*)—pressed
- गोमतः (*gomataḥ*)—of the Soma-juice mixed with milk
- पीतिं (*pītiṃ*)—the drinking (having done it, and by it)
- तृप्तिं (*tṛptiṃ*)—satisfaction
- वि अश्नुहि (*vi aśnuhi*)—attain

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, hear well this prayer or call that I make. Drink this Soma-juice that has been pressed by us and mixed with cow's milk, and attain manifold satisfaction by it.

### Printed p. 226

##### English translation
Indra, hear with favour my call ; be present at the drinking of this our libation mixed with milk, and be satisfied.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> य इं॑द्र चम॒सेष्वा सोम॑श्च॒मूषु॑ ते सु॒तः ।  
> पिबेद॑स्य॒ त्वमी॑शिषे ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । इंद्र । चमसेषु । आ । सोमः । चमूषु । ते । सुतः ।  
> पिब । इत् । अस्य । त्वम् । ईशिषे ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र ते त्वदर्थं सुतः अभिषुतः यः सोमः चमसेषु एतन्नामकेषु पात्रेषु तथा चमूषु । चमंति यजंत्यत्रेति चम्वो ग्रहाः । तेषु च आ सर्वतः अस्ति अस्य तमेतं सोमं पिबेत् । इदवधारणे । पिबैव । कथमस्य सोमपानयोग्यता तत्राह । हे इंद्र त्वमीशिषे तस्य त्वमेवेश्वरो भवसि खलु । यत एवं ततः पिबेति समन्वयः । 'ईश ऐश्वर्ये '। लटि ‘ईशः से (पा. सू. ७. २. ७७ ) इतीडागमः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa addresses Indra. The Soma that has been pressed for you is in the चमसेषु (*camaseṣu*), vessels of that name, and in the चमूषु (*camūṣu*); he explains the word chamū as the ग्रहाः (*grahāḥ*) (cups), because in them one sacrifices (चमंति यजंत्यत्र (*camaṃti yajaṃtyatra*)). It is there on all sides **NOTE**; drink this very Soma; he explains इत् (*it*) as a particle of emphasis ("drink indeed"). How is this Soma fit to be drunk by him? Because you, Indra, are its lord. Since this is so, drink: such is the connection of the sentence.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हे इंद्र (*he iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ते (*te*)—for you
- सुतः (*sutaḥ*)—pressed
- यः सोमः (*yaḥ somaḥ*)—the Soma which
- चमसेषु (*camaseṣu*)—in the cup-vessels called chamasa
- चमूषु (*camūṣu*)—in the vessels called chamū, the cups (graha) from which the Soma is drunk
- आ (*ā*)—has been filled and made ready
- अस्य (*asya*)—this Soma-juice
- पिब इत् (*piba it*)—drink without fail (because)
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- ईशिषे (*īśiṣe*)—are the lord of all this Soma

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, this Soma-juice, which has just been pressed in order to be offered to you, has been filled and made ready in the chamasa vessels and in the vessels for drinking called chamū. You alone are the lord of this Soma. Therefore drink this Soma-juice without fail.

### Printed p. 227

##### English translation
Whatever Soma has been poured into the cups and the bowls for you drink it, Indra,—you are the sovereign.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the third paryāya, in the śastra mantras recited by the priest called Acchāvāka, the three Ṛks 7-9 of this sūkta, beginning य इंद्र चमसेषु (*ya iṃdra camaseṣu*), are to be recited; this is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra य इंद्र चमसेष्वा सोमः प्र वः सतां (*ya iṃdra camaseṣvā somaḥ pra vaḥ satāṃ*) (Ā. 6-4).

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यो अ॒प्सु चं॒द्रमा॑ इव॒ सोम॑श्च॒मूषु॒ ददृ॑शे ।  
> पिबेद॑स्य॒ त्वमी॑शिषे ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । अप्ऽसु । चंद्रमाःऽइव । सोमः । चमूषु । ददृशे ।  
> पिब । इत् । अस्य । त्वम् । ईशिषे ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र यः गृहीतः सोमः चमूषु ग्रहेषु ददृशे अंतर्दृश्यते । तत्र दृष्टांतः । चंद्रमाइव यथा चंद्रमाः अप्सु अंतरिक्षे निर्मलतया दृश्यते तद्वत् । यद्वा । अप्सूदकेषु चंद्रमाः प्रतिबिंबतया नानाविधो दृश्यते तथाष्टग्रहेष्वनेकरूपः सन् दृश्यते तमेतं सोमं पिबैव यतस्त्वमेवेशिषे खलु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa addresses Indra. The Soma that has been taken up is seen within the चमूषु (*camūṣu*), the cups. He gives the illustration (चंद्रमाइव (*caṃdramāiva*)): as the moon is seen in the अप्सु (*apsu*), in the atmosphere, by reason of its clearness, so the Soma is seen. Or else (यद्वा (*yadvā*)), as the moon, reflected in the waters, is seen in many forms, so the Soma is seen in the eight cups, being of many forms. Drink this very Soma, since you alone are indeed its lord.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra) त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- ईशिषे (*īśiṣe*)—are the lord
- अप्सु (*apsu*)—in the clear atmosphere
- चंद्रमा इव (*caṃdramā iva*)—clear like the moon, as it shines; [or अप्सु (*apsu*)—in water; चंद्रमा इव (*caṃdramā iva*)—as the reflected moon is seen]
- चमूषु (*camūṣu*)—in the cups (drinking vessels), filled
- यः सोमः (*yaḥ somaḥ*)—which Soma
- ददृशे (*dadṛśe*)—shines
- अस्य इत् (*asya it*)—this very Soma
- पिब (*piba*)—drink

### Printed p. 228

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you are the lord of everything. Soma-juice as clear as the moon shines in the clear atmosphere, or as the moon is reflected in clear water, has been made ready and set for you in the drinking vessels called chamū. Drink that very Soma.

##### English translation
Whatever Soma, is seen in the vessels like the moon (reflected) in the waters, drink it,—you are the sovereign.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यं ते॑ श्ये॒नः प॒दाभ॑रत्ति॒रो रजां॒स्यस्पृ॑तम् ।  
> पिबेद॑स्य॒ त्वमी॑शिषे ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यम् । ते । श्येनः । पदा । आ । अभरत् । तिरः । रजांसि । अस्पृतम् ।  
> पिब । इत् । अस्य । त्वम् । ईशिषे ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र श्येनः शंसनीयः पक्षी । पक्षिरूपधारिणी गायत्रीत्यर्थः । स पक्षी रजांसि अंतरिक्षादिलोकस्थितान् सोमपालान् गंधर्वान् तिरः तिरः कुर्वन् अस्पृतं शत्रुभिरस्पृष्टं संतं यं सोमं ते त्वदर्थं पदा पद्भ्याम् आभरत् । पदेति सवनद्वयाभिप्रायम् । सवनद्वय आहृतं सोमं त्वं पिब । गायत्री पक्षिरूपं धारयित्वा पद्यां   सोममाहरदित्यत्रार्थे यजुर्ब्राह्मणं--' पद्यां अ द्वे सवने समगृह्णान्मुखेनैकं तस्माद्द्वे सवने शुक्रवती प्रातःसवनं च माध्यंदिनं च ' (तै. सं. ६. १. ६. ४ ) इति । तं पिबैव । त्वमेव तस्येश्वरो भवसि ॥ ॥ २ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa addresses Indra. The श्येनः (*śyenaḥ*) is the praiseworthy bird; the meaning is Gāyatrī, who has taken the form of a bird. That bird, overcoming (तिरः तिरः कुर्वन् (*tiraḥ tiraḥ kurvan*)) the रजांसि (*rajāṃsi*), that is, the Gandharvas who guard the Soma and are stationed in the worlds beginning with the atmosphere, brought with its feet (पदा (*padā*) is पद्भ्याम् (*padbhyām*)) for you the Soma, अस्पृतं (*aspṛtaṃ*), untouched by enemies. The word "foot" has in view the two savanas: drink the Soma brought to the two savanas. For the point that Gāyatrī took the form of a bird and brought the Soma with her feet he cites the Yajur Brāhmaṇa: पद्यां अ द्वे सवने समगृह्णान्मुखेनैकं तस्माद्द्वे सवने शुक्रवती प्रातःसवनं च माध्यंदिनं च (*padyāṃ a dve savane samagṛhṇānmukhenaikaṃ tasmāddve savane śukravatī prātaḥsavanaṃ ca mādhyaṃdinaṃ ca*) **NOTE** (Tai. Saṃ. 6. 1. 6. 4) **GLOSS**. Drink that Soma; you alone are its lord.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra) श्येनः (*śyenaḥ*)—the hawk
- रजांसि (*rajāṃsi*)—and the Soma-guards who were guarding the Soma in the worlds beginning with the atmosphere
- तिरः (*tiraḥ*)—having set aside, that is, defeated
- अस्पृतं (*aspṛtaṃ*)—not touched by enemies

### Printed p. 229

- यं (*yaṃ*)—which Soma
- ते (*te*)—in order to offer to you
- पदा (*padā*)—with its feet
- आभरत् (*ābharat*)—brought
- अस्य इत् (*asya it*)—this very Soma-juice, in the Prātaḥsavana and Mādhyaṃdina savanas
- पिब (*piba*)—drink
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- ईशिषे (*īśiṣe*)—are the lord of all

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, when the hawk brought the Soma from heaven to offer it to you, it defeated the Gandharvas and others who were guarding it in the worlds beginning with the atmosphere, and brought it held in its two feet, so that it was not touched by enemies. Drink that very Soma in the Prātaḥsavana and Mādhyaṃdina savanas of this sacrifice. You are the lord of all.

##### English translation
Whatever Soma the hawk bore for you with its feet, having won it, till then inviolate, from the (guardians of the) upper worlds, drink it,–yon are the sovereign

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

This Ṛk mentions how the hawk, when it brought the Soma from heaven, defeated the Gandharvas who were its guardians and brought the Soma plants holding them with its two feet and its beak. The commentator indicates that the matter of the Gāyatrī metre itself bringing the Soma that was in heaven down to the earth in the form of a hawk is described in the Taittirīya Saṃhitā in sentences beginning पद्भ्यां द्वे सवने समुगृह्णात् (*padbhyāṃ dve savane samugṛhṇāt*) (Tai. Saṃ. 6-1-6-4). What is said in that Taittirīya Saṃhitā is explained at length here:

Formerly the Soma was in the world of the gods. The gods thought that it should be brought to the earth; and when they considered who was able to bring it, it was decided that the metres beginning with Gāyatrī were able to bring the Soma.

जगत्युदपतच्चतुर्दशाक्षरा सती साऽप्राप्य न्यवर्तत तस्यै द्वे अक्षरे अमिाये-ताग्ं सा पशुभिश्च दीक्षया चागच्छत्तस्माज्जगती छंदसां पशव्यतमा तेस्मा-त्रशुमुंतं दीक्षोपसमति त्रिष्टुगुदपतत्त्रयोदशाक्षरा सती साऽप्राप्य न्यवर्तत तस्यै द्वे अक्षरे अनिायेताग्ं सा दक्षिणाभिश्च तपसा चागच्छत्त-स्मात्त्रिष्टुभो लोके माध्यंदिने सवने दक्षिणा नीयंत एतत्फलु वाव तप (*jagatyudapataccaturdaśākṣarā satī sā 'prāpya nyavartata tasyai dve akṣare amiāye-tāgṃ sā paśubhiśca dīkṣayā cāgacchattasmājjagatī chaṃdasāṃ paśavyatamā tesmā-traśumuṃtaṃ dīkṣopasamati triṣṭugudapatattrayodaśākṣarā satī sā 'prāpya nyavartata tasyai dve akṣare aniāyetāgṃ sā dakṣiṇābhiśca tapasā cāgacchatta-smāttriṣṭubho loke mādhyaṃdine savane dakṣiṇā nīyaṃta etatphalu vāva tapa*) **GLOSS**

### Printed p. 230

इत्याहुर्यः स्वं ददातीति गायत्र्युदपतच्चतुरक्षरा सत्यजया ज्योतिषा तमुस्मा अजाभ्यरुंधत तदजाया अजत्वग्ं सा सोमं चाहरच्चत्वारि चाक्षराणि साष्टाक्षरा समुपद्यत (*ityāhuryaḥ svaṃ dadātīti gāyatryudapataccaturakṣarā satyajayā jyotiṣā tamusmā ajābhyaruṃdhata tadajāyā ajatvagṃ sā somaṃ cāharaccatvāri cākṣarāṇi sāṣṭākṣarā samupadyata*) ब्रह्मवादिनो वदंति कस्मात्सत्याद्गायत्री कनिष्ठा छंदसाग्ं सती यज्ञमुखं परीयायेति यदेवादः सोममाहरत्तस्माद्यज्ञमुखं पर्य्यैत्तस्मात्तेजस्विनीतमा पद्भ्यां द्वे सवने समुगृह्णान्मुखेनैकं यन्मुखेन समुगृह्णात्तदधयत्तस्माद्द्वेसवने शुक्रवती प्रातःसवनं च माध्यंदिनं च तस्मात्तृतीयसवनं ऋजीष-मभिषुण्वंति धीतमिव हि मन्यंते आतिरमवनयति सशुक्रत्वायाथो संभरत्येवैनत् । (*brahmavādino vadaṃti kasmātsatyādgāyatrī kaniṣṭhā chaṃdasāgṃ satī yajñamukhaṃ parīyāyeti yadevādaḥ somamāharattasmādyajñamukhaṃ paryyaittasmāttejasvinītamā padbhyāṃ dve savane samugṛhṇānmukhenaikaṃ yanmukhena samugṛhṇāttadadhayattasmāddvesavane śukravatī prātaḥsavanaṃ ca mādhyaṃdinaṃ ca tasmāttṛtīyasavanaṃ ṛjīṣa-mabhiṣuṇvaṃti dhītamiva hi manyaṃte ātiramavanayati saśukratvāyātho saṃbharatyevainat |*) (Tai. Saṃ. 6-1-6-2, 3, 4, 5)

At that time the Jagatī metre set out, saying that it would bring the Soma, and leapt into the atmosphere. At that time each pāda of the Jagatī metre had fourteen syllables. But it was unable to bring the Soma, and, exhausted and weary, it came back. Then it lost two of its fourteen syllables. But on its way back it brought with it the sacrificial animals that were there and the order of the dīkṣā (the rite of undertaking consecration in a sacrifice). Therefore it became known in the world that the Jagatī metre is rich in animals; and the dīkṣā always follows a sacrificer endowed with wealth of animals. After that the Triṣṭubh metre set out, saying that it would bring the Soma, and leapt into the atmosphere. At that time each pāda of the Triṣṭubh metre had thirteen syllables. The Triṣṭubh metre too could not bring the Soma; it grew weary and limp, lost two of its thirteen syllables and came back. On its way back it brought with it the sacrificial fee and austerity (tapas). Therefore, when a sacrifice is performed, the fees are brought and given (to the priests) at the Mādhyaṃdina savana. The giving away of what one has is called austerity. After that the Gāyatrī metre set out, saying that it would bring the Soma, and flew into the atmosphere in the form of a hawk. At that time each pāda of the Gāyatrī metre had four syllables. When setting out, Gāyatrī took with her, in company, for help, a shining she-goat. **NOTE** This goat helped Gāyatrī when she brought the Soma; therefore the goat became known by the name अजा (*ajā*). After that Gāyatrī brought back the Soma that was in heaven and also the four syllables that the Jagatī and Triṣṭubh metres had lost. The four syllables she had brought back and the four syllables she had before together made eight syllables in each pāda of Gāyatrī (twelve syllables for the Jagatī metre and 11 syllables for the Triṣṭubh metre). Here a doubt arises, as the expounders of the Veda say: although the Gāyatrī metre is much smaller than all the metres, how did it obtain prominence in the sacrifice? The answer is: this Gāyatrī brought the Soma. Therefore Gāyatrī has come to have precedence in sacrificial rites, and it shines with great brilliance.

### Printed p. 231

When this Gāyatrī brought the Soma from heaven, she took the form of the hawk and brought it holding with her two feet (of the three pādas of eight syllables of Gāyatrī, two are said to be the feet and the third the mouth) the two savanas called the Prātaḥsavana and the Mādhyaṃdina savana, and seizing with her mouth (her beak) the third, the Tṛtīyasavana. Since the Prātaḥsavana and the Mādhyaṃdina savana, which were held in the feet, remained unchanged just as they were, in both these savanas the Soma plants are crushed and the shining, pure juice is pressed out.
